# GLOSSARY — acronyms and specialist terms used in this knowledge base

**1086 terms** harvested from the 332 pages under `kb/` and defined in plain English. Each entry links to the KB page that explains the idea properly; the definitions here are short reminders, written to agree with those pages. Last regenerated 2026-10-12 by W6-F1.

## How to use it

- Look the term up in the A–Z tables below (acronyms, units such as dBc/Hz, and named concepts are mixed together and sorted alphabetically; terms that start with a digit are under **0–9**, Greek symbols under **Symbols**).
- **Expansion** is what the letters stand for; **Meaning** is a one-to-three-sentence plain-English explanation; **Formula / typical value** gives the defining equation or a number to sanity-check against (values follow the KB's canonical conventions: $T_0$ = 290 K, dBm re 1 mW, gain in dBi, 50 Ω, $e^{j\omega t}$ — see [canonical values](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md)); **See** links the page to read next.
- Some acronyms mean different things on different pages (for example *NF*, *PG*, *CR*). Check the [ambiguous-acronyms section](#ambiguous-acronyms-in-this-kb) before assuming a meaning.
- If a term is missing, it may be defined in bold on its page: search `kb/` for `**term**`.

## How to regenerate or extend it

- Definitions live in `tools/glossary_terms.txt` (one line per term, fields separated by ` ‖ `: term ‖ expansion ‖ meaning ‖ formula ‖ see ‖ aliases). The ambiguous-acronym section lives in `tools/glossary_ambiguous.md`. Edit those files, never this one.
- `python3 tools/glossary_harvest.py` re-harvests candidate terms from every page (all-caps tokens of 2–8 characters, mixed-case tokens such as LoRa or P1dB, dB-family units, bold phrases, "Long Form (ACR)" expansions and front-matter tags) into `research-logs/raw-harvest/glossary/`, including `ambiguous.tsv` (acronyms written out in more than one way).
- `python3 tools/glossary_harvest.py --missing 3` lists acronyms used on at least 3 pages that the glossary does not define yet. Add them to `tools/glossary_terms.txt`.
- `python3 tools/glossary_harvest.py --build` rewrites this file and fails if any **See** link points at a page that does not exist. Part numbers are left out on purpose, except for part families that the KB uses as concepts (for example SX126x, R820T).

**Jump to:** [0–9](#09) · [A](#a) · [B](#b) · [C](#c) · [D](#d) · [E](#e) · [F](#f) · [G](#g) · [H](#h) · [I](#i) · [J](#j) · [K](#k) · [L](#l) · [M](#m) · [N](#n) · [O](#o) · [P](#p) · [Q](#q) · [R](#r) · [S](#s) · [T](#t) · [U](#u) · [V](#v) · [W](#w) · [X](#x) · [Y](#y) · [Z](#z) · [Symbols](#symbols) · [Ambiguous acronyms](#ambiguous-acronyms-in-this-kb)

## 0–9

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **1PPS** | one pulse per second | A GNSS receiver's timing output with its edge aligned to the second (UTC or GNSS time) to tens of ns; used to time-stamp data and discipline clocks. | — | [21/gnss-timing-1pps](kb/21-gnss/gnss-timing-1pps.md) |
| **2.5-D solver** | planar EM solver | MoM solver for layered planar structures (PCB traces, filters) assuming infinite dielectric layers (Sonnet, AXIEM, Momentum). | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **50 Ω** | fifty-ohm reference impedance | The standard RF system impedance: a compromise between the lowest-loss (≈ 77 Ω) and highest-power (≈ 30 Ω) air coax. S-parameters, Γ and dBm-to-voltage conversions in the KB are referenced to 50 Ω. | 0 dBm in 50 Ω = 0.224 V rms | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |

## A

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **A-GNSS** | assisted GNSS | Supplying ephemeris, almanac, time and approximate position from outside (network or saved data) to cut TTFF; u-blox calls it AssistNow. | — | [21/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris](kb/21-gnss/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris.md) |
| **A-scope** | — | Radar display of echo amplitude versus range (time) along one line, like an oscilloscope trace. | — | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **ABCD parameters** | chain (transmission) parameters | Two-port description relating input voltage and current to output voltage and current; cascaded stages simply multiply their ABCD matrices. | [V1; I1] = [A B; C D][V2; I2] | [01/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd](kb/01-circuit-theory/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd.md) |
| **AC** | alternating current | A time-varying (usually sinusoidal) current or voltage; in SPICE, a small-signal frequency sweep. | — | [01/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance](kb/01-circuit-theory/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance.md) |
| **ACLR** | adjacent-channel leakage ratio | Transmitted power that leaks into the neighbouring channel relative to the wanted channel; set by PA non-linearity and phase noise (also called ACPR). | — | [06/transmitter-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/transmitter-architectures.md) |
| **ACMA** | Australian Communications and Media Authority | Australia's regulator; its LIPD class licence sets low-interference-potential-device limits at 433 and 915–928 MHz. | — | [19/international-rules-for-competitions](kb/19-regulations-safety/international-rules-for-competitions.md) |
| **acquisition** | — | The GNSS receiver's search over code phase and Doppler to find each satellite's signal before tracking it. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking.md) |
| **ACR** | adjacent-channel rejection | How strong a signal in the next channel can be (relative to a wanted signal near sensitivity) before reception fails. | SX1262 ACR 60–72 dB | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **active antenna** | — | An antenna with a built-in LNA (and filter), powered through its coax by a bias tee; standard for GNSS. | ZED-F9P wants 17–50 dB total gain incl. cable (ideal 30–40 dB), NF < 4 dB; supply 5–20 mA | [08/gnss-antennas](kb/08-antennas/gnss-antennas.md) |
| **active filter** | — | Filter built from op-amps, resistors and capacitors (Sallen–Key, multiple-feedback); no inductors, used at baseband and audio. | — | [05/active-filters](kb/05-analog-electronics/active-filters.md) |
| **active reflection coefficient** | scan impedance | The reflection an element sees when the whole array is driven and steered; it varies with scan angle and can approach 1 at scan blindness. | Γ_act,m = Σ S_mn w_n/w_m | [12/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling](kb/12-phased-arrays/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling.md) |
| **AD936x** | AD9361/AD9363/AD9364 family | Analog Devices' integrated zero-IF RF transceivers (70 MHz–6 GHz, up to 56 MHz bandwidth) inside Pluto, USRP B2xx, bladeRF-class and AntSDR radios. | — | [18/rf-transceiver-ics](kb/18-components-parts/rf-transceiver-ics.md) |
| **ADC** | analog-to-digital converter | Converts a voltage into numbers at a sample rate; its noise, distortion and clock jitter set an SDR's dynamic range. | ideal SQNR = 6.02N + 1.76 dB | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **ADI** | Analog Devices Inc. | Major maker of RF, data-converter and phased-array ICs (AD936x, ADF435x PLLs, ADAR1000) and of many app notes cited in the KB. | — | [18/sourcing-and-eval-boards](kb/18-components-parts/sourcing-and-eval-boards.md) |
| **admittance** | — | The reciprocal of impedance: conductance plus susceptance, in siemens. Parallel elements add in admittance. | Y = 1/Z = G + jB | [01/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance](kb/01-circuit-theory/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance.md) |
| **ADS** | Advanced Design System | Keysight's commercial RF/microwave circuit and EM simulator. | — | [16/tool-landscape-overview](kb/16-simulation-tools/tool-landscape-overview.md) |
| **ADS-B** | Automatic Dependent Surveillance–Broadcast | Aircraft position broadcasts at 1090 MHz; a favourite first SDR target. | 1090 MHz, PPM 1 Mb/s | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |
| **AFC** | automatic frequency control | A loop or routine that measures frequency error and retunes the receiver (or transmitter) to remove it. | — | [15/lora-impairments-doppler-drift-timing](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-impairments-doppler-drift-timing.md) |
| **AFSK** | audio frequency-shift keying | FSK done with audio tones fed into an FM radio (e.g. 1200/2200 Hz Bell 202 for APRS). | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **AGC** | automatic gain control | A loop that adjusts receiver gain so the signal reaching the ADC or demodulator stays in range despite large changes in input level. | — | [06/agc-loops-and-receiver-gain-control](kb/06-rf-circuits/agc-loops-and-receiver-gain-control.md) |
| **AGND** | analog ground | Pin or net name for the analog ground of a mixed-signal IC; on a board it normally joins DGND in one solid plane, with careful part placement instead of splits. | — | [13/mixed-signal-layout](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/mixed-signal-layout.md) |
| **AGT** | average gain test | NEC sanity check: a lossless antenna in free space should integrate to an average gain of 1 (0 dB); deviations reveal segmentation errors. | lossless in free space: 1.000 (0 dB); over perfect ground: 2.000 | [08/antenna-simulation-nec-openems](kb/08-antennas/antenna-simulation-nec-openems.md) |
| **AiP** | antenna in package | mm-wave chips with the antennas built into the package (e.g. 60 GHz radar SoCs). | — | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **Airspy** | — | Receive-only SDRs with 12-bit converters and good dynamic range for VHF/UHF and HF. | — | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **AIS** | Automatic Identification System | Ship position broadcasts on 161.975/162.025 MHz (GMSK 9.6 kb/s). | — | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |
| **Albersheim's equation** | — | Empirical closed form for the SNR needed for a given P_D and P_FA with N non-coherently integrated pulses (non-fluctuating target). | — | [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md) |
| **ALC** | automatic level control | A transmitter loop that holds output power constant by sensing it with a detector and adjusting drive. | — | [06/pa-control-protection-and-sequencing](kb/06-rf-circuits/pa-control-protection-and-sequencing.md) |
| **aliasing** | — | Frequencies above half the sample rate folding back into the band, where they cannot be distinguished from real signals. | alias at \|f − kf_s\| | [14/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling.md) |
| **Allan deviation** | — | Measure of an oscillator's fractional-frequency stability over an averaging time τ; the time-domain partner of phase noise. | σ_y(τ) | [07/phase-noise-and-jitter](kb/07-noise-linearity/phase-noise-and-jitter.md) |
| **almanac** | — | Coarse orbits of the whole constellation, valid for weeks; only speeds up the satellite search. | full GPS almanac takes 12.5 min | [21/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris](kb/21-gnss/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris.md) |
| **ALOHA** | — | Random-access scheme where nodes transmit whenever they like and collisions are retried; LoRaWAN uplinks are ALOHA. | pure ALOHA throughput S = Ge^{−2G} (max 18.4 %); slotted Ge^{−G} (36.8 %) | [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md) |
| **alpha–beta filter** | — | Fixed-gain position–velocity tracker; the steady-state form of a Kalman filter for a constant-velocity target. | — | [11/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers](kb/11-radar/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers.md) |
| **AM** | amplitude modulation | Information carried in the carrier's envelope; simple to detect, inefficient, sensitive to noise and fading. | modulation index m; sidebands at f_c ± f_m | [10/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb](kb/10-comms-modulation/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb.md) |
| **AM/AM** | amplitude-to-amplitude conversion | How a PA's gain compresses as input amplitude rises. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **AM/PM** | amplitude-to-phase conversion | How a PA's (or varactor's) phase shift changes with input amplitude; it distorts QAM and OFDM. | degrees per dB | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **amateur radio licence** | — | Licence to operate on amateur bands with higher power, for non-commercial purposes; in Singapore issued by IMDA after the RAE. | SG 70 cm: 430–440 MHz, ≤ 10 W ERP with IMDA consent | [19/amateur-radio-licensing](kb/19-regulations-safety/amateur-radio-licensing.md) |
| **ambiguity function** | — | Map of a waveform's matched-filter response versus delay and Doppler mismatch; shows its range and velocity resolution and range–Doppler coupling. | total volume fixed (\|χ\|² normalised to 1) | [11/ambiguity-function-waveforms](kb/11-radar/ambiguity-function-waveforms.md) |
| **analytic signal** | — | A complex signal with only positive frequencies, made from a real signal plus j times its Hilbert transform. | — | [14/iq-complex-baseband](kb/14-sdr-dsp/iq-complex-baseband.md) |
| **antenna factor** | — | EMC calibration number that converts the voltage at an antenna's 50 Ω port into incident field strength. | AF[dB/m] = E[dBµV/m] − V[dBµV] | [02/electromagnetic-units-and-constants](kb/02-electromagnetics/electromagnetic-units-and-constants.md) |
| **antenna temperature** | — | The noise temperature an antenna delivers, set by what it "sees" (cold sky ≈ tens of K, warm earth ≈ 290 K, man-made noise far more at HF). | — | [07/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature](kb/07-noise-linearity/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature.md) |
| **anti-alias filter** | — | Low-pass or band-pass filter before an ADC that removes signals that would fold into the wanted band. | — | [14/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling.md) |
| **anti-resonance** | — | A sharp impedance peak where a capacitor's inductance resonates with another capacitor or the plane; it can amplify supply noise. | — | [13/power-distribution-decoupling-for-rf](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/power-distribution-decoupling-for-rf.md) |
| **APCP** | ammonium perchlorate composite propellant | The usual amateur solid rocket propellant; its exhaust plume can attenuate radio links. | — | [08/antennas-on-rockets](kb/08-antennas/antennas-on-rockets.md) |
| **APDP** | antiparallel diode pair | Two diodes back to back; as a mixer it responds to twice the LO frequency (a sub-harmonic or harmonic mixer). | — | [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md) |
| **aperture jitter** | clock jitter | Random timing error of the sampling instant; it limits ADC SNR at high input frequencies regardless of bit count. | SNR = −20 log(2πf·σ_t): 100 fs → 87 dB @ 70 MHz, 71 dB @ 433 MHz | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **APRS** | Automatic Packet Reporting System | Amateur system that sends positions and telemetry as AX.25 packets (1200 baud AFSK on VHF); used for rocket and balloon tracking. | 144.39 MHz (US), 144.80 MHz (EU) | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **APSK** | amplitude and phase-shift keying | Constellation of concentric rings of PSK points; friendlier to non-linear satellite amplifiers than square QAM. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **APT** | automatic picture transmission | Analog weather-image downlink of the old NOAA polar satellites at 137 MHz (LRPT is the digital Meteor-M equivalent); a classic SDR + QFH project. | — | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |
| **ARQ** | automatic repeat request | Error control by acknowledgement and retransmission rather than (or as well as) FEC. | — | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **array calibration** | — | Measuring and correcting each channel's gain and phase error (and coupling) so beams point and null correctly. | — | [12/array-calibration](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-calibration.md) |
| **array factor** | — | The pattern of an array of isotropic elements, set only by element positions and weights; the full pattern is element pattern × array factor. | AF(u) = Σ w_n e^{jkx_n u}, u = sin θ | [12/array-factor-linear-arrays](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-factor-linear-arrays.md) |
| **array synthesis** | pattern synthesis | Choosing element weights to produce a desired pattern (Schelkunoff zero placement, Fourier and Woodward–Lawson sampling, optimisation). | — | [12/array-pattern-synthesis](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-pattern-synthesis.md) |
| **ARRL** | American Radio Relay League | US amateur radio association; publisher of the ARRL Handbook and Antenna Book. | — | [19/amateur-radio-licensing](kb/19-regulations-safety/amateur-radio-licensing.md) |
| **AS923** | LoRaWAN Asia 923 MHz channel plan | LoRaWAN regional parameters used in Singapore and much of Asia (923.2/923.4 MHz default channels). | — | [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **ASK** | amplitude-shift keying | Digital modulation by switching the carrier amplitude; OOK is its on–off form. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **attenuator** | pad | A resistive network that reduces signal by a fixed number of dB while keeping 50 Ω at both ports; also improves match. | Pi pad 10 dB in 50 Ω: 96.2 Ω shunt, 71.2 Ω series | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **AUT** | antenna under test | The antenna being measured on a range. | — | [08/antenna-measurement](kb/08-antennas/antenna-measurement.md) |
| **autocorrelation** | — | How similar a signal is to a time-shifted copy of itself. Sharp autocorrelation peaks make good radar waveforms and spreading codes. | R(τ) = E[x(t)x*(t−τ)] | [00/probability-random-processes-and-psd](kb/00-math-foundations/probability-random-processes-and-psd.md) |
| **availability** | — | Fraction of time (or of locations) a link meets its required SNR. | — | [09/link-margin-availability](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-margin-availability.md) |
| **AWGN** | additive white Gaussian noise | The reference channel model: Gaussian noise with flat PSD simply added to the signal. Datasheet sensitivities and BER curves assume it. | N0 = kT·F | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **AX.25** | — | Amateur packet-radio link protocol (HDLC-based), used by APRS. | — | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **axial ratio** | — | Ratio of the major to minor axis of the polarization ellipse; 0 dB (1:1) is perfect circular polarization. | AR = 20 log(E_major/E_minor); good CP < 3 dB | [08/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization](kb/08-antennas/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization.md) |

## B

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **back-drilling** | — | Drilling out the unused part of a via barrel to remove its resonant stub. | — | [13/vias-stitching-fencing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/vias-stitching-fencing.md) |
| **back-off** | — | Running a PA below its saturated or compression power to keep a signal with peaks (high PAPR) linear; efficiency falls. | back-off ≈ PAPR | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **backprojection** | — | Time-domain SAR image formation: for each pixel, sum every pulse's echo at that pixel's delay with phase correction. Exact but slow. | — | [11/sar-and-imaging](kb/11-radar/sar-and-imaging.md) |
| **balun** | balanced-to-unbalanced transformer | A device that connects a balanced load (dipole, differential IC pins) to an unbalanced line (coax, single-ended trace) and stops common-mode current. | — | [06/baluns-and-rf-transformers](kb/06-rf-circuits/baluns-and-rf-transformers.md) |
| **bandpass sampling** | undersampling | Deliberately sampling a narrow band-pass signal below twice its carrier, using aliasing to shift it to baseband; needs a good band-pass filter. | — | [14/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling.md) |
| **Barker code** | — | Binary phase codes (lengths up to 13) whose autocorrelation sidelobes are all at most 1; used for pulse compression and preambles. | Barker-13 sidelobes −22.3 dB | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **Barkhausen criterion** | — | Condition for steady oscillation: loop gain of exactly 1 at a phase of 0° (360°). Real oscillators start with loop gain > 1 and settle by limiting. | \|Aβ\| = 1, ∠Aβ = 0 | [05/oscillators](kb/05-analog-electronics/oscillators.md) |
| **Bartlett beamformer** | conventional (delay-and-sum) beamformer | Scans a steered beam and reports power versus angle; robust but resolution limited to about one beamwidth. | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **BAW** | bulk acoustic wave | Acoustic filter using a resonating film thickness (FBAR, SMR); works to higher frequency and power than SAW. | — | [06/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip.md) |
| **BBR** | battery-backed RAM | u-blox memory kept alive by V_BCKP so ephemeris, time and configuration survive power loss, giving hot starts. | — | [21/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris](kb/21-gnss/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris.md) |
| **beam squint** | — | Change of a phase-steered beam's direction with frequency, because a fixed phase shift is only the right delay at one frequency. True-time delay avoids it. | — | [12/phase-shifters-ttd-components](kb/12-phased-arrays/phase-shifters-ttd-components.md) |
| **beam steering** | — | Pointing an array's main beam by applying a progressive phase (or delay) across the elements. | Δφ = kd sin θ₀ | [12/beam-steering-grating-lobes](kb/12-phased-arrays/beam-steering-grating-lobes.md) |
| **beamforming** | — | Weighting and summing element signals to form a beam; analog (RF/IF phase shifters), digital (per-element ADCs) or hybrid. | — | [12/beamforming-architectures](kb/12-phased-arrays/beamforming-architectures.md) |
| **beat frequency** | — | The difference frequency between transmitted and received chirps in an FMCW radar (the "IF"); its value encodes range. | f_b = 2BR/(cT_c) | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **BeiDou** | BDS | China's GNSS (BDS-3), with B1I, B1C and B2a signals; many satellites visible from Singapore. | B1I 1561.098 MHz | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **BER** | bit error rate (ratio) | Fraction of bits received wrongly. | BPSK 10⁻⁵ needs Eb/N0 = 9.6 dB | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **Bessel filter** | Thomson filter | Low-pass with maximally flat group delay, so pulses keep their shape; slow roll-off. | — | [05/active-filters](kb/05-analog-electronics/active-filters.md) |
| **Bessel functions** | — | Solutions of the cylindrical wave equation; they give FM sideband amplitudes, circular-waveguide and patch modes, and the jinc pattern of a circular aperture. | FM carrier vanishes at β = 2.405 (J₀ zero) | [00/special-functions-bessel-sinc](kb/00-math-foundations/special-functions-bessel-sinc.md) |
| **BGA** | ball grid array | Package with solder balls on its underside; needs X-ray inspection and careful fan-out. (LGA: land grid array.) | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **bias tee** | — | A network (inductor to DC, capacitor to RF) that puts DC onto an RF line, e.g. to power an active GNSS antenna through its coax. | — | [06/amplifier-stability-and-biasing](kb/06-rf-circuits/amplifier-stability-and-biasing.md) |
| **BIBO** | bounded-input bounded-output stability | A system is BIBO-stable if every bounded input gives a bounded output: all poles in the left half-plane (or inside the unit circle). | — | [00/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems](kb/00-math-foundations/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems.md) |
| **bilinear transform** | — | Mapping an analog filter to a digital IIR filter by substituting for s; warps the frequency axis, so critical frequencies are pre-warped. | s = (2/T)(z − 1)/(z + 1) | [14/digital-filters-fir-iir](kb/14-sdr-dsp/digital-filters-fir-iir.md) |
| **BJT** | bipolar junction transistor | Current-controlled transistor (base current controls collector current); offers high transconductance per current and low voltage noise. | g_m = I_C/V_T ≈ I_C/26 mV | [05/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal](kb/05-analog-electronics/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal.md) |
| **blade antenna** | — | A flush or blade-shaped monopole for aircraft and rockets, with low drag. | — | [08/antennas-on-rockets](kb/08-antennas/antennas-on-rockets.md) |
| **bladeRF** | — | Nuand SDR family with an FPGA and AD9361/LMS-class transceiver. | — | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **BLE** | Bluetooth Low Energy | Low-power 2.4 GHz GFSK protocol (1–2 Mb/s); short range unless coded PHY is used. | — | [15/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4.md) |
| **blind speed** | — | Target radial speed at which an MTI filter cancels the target because its Doppler equals a multiple of the PRF; staggered PRFs remove it. | v_b = nλ·PRF/2 | [11/clutter-and-mti](kb/11-radar/clutter-and-mti.md) |
| **blocking** | — | Loss of sensitivity caused by a strong off-channel signal (through compression, reciprocal mixing or IMD). Datasheets quote the blocker level that degrades sensitivity by 3 dB. | SX1262 blocking 88/90/99 dB at ±1/2/10 MHz | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **BNC** | bayonet Neill–Concelman connector | Bayonet 50 Ω connector used on test equipment and oscilloscopes; fine to a few GHz. | — | [18/connectors-and-cables-parts](kb/18-components-parts/connectors-and-cables-parts.md) |
| **BOC** | binary offset carrier | GNSS modulation that multiplies the code by a square-wave subcarrier, splitting the spectrum into two lobes and sharpening the correlation peak (CBOC, TMBOC, AltBOC are variants). | BOC(1,1) | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **Bode plot** | — | Plots of a transfer function's magnitude (dB) and phase against log frequency; straight-line asymptotes make it quick to sketch. | — | [00/laplace-transfer-functions-bode](kb/00-math-foundations/laplace-transfer-functions-bode.md) |
| **Bode–Fano limit** | — | Fundamental limit on how well a reactive load can be matched over a band: you can trade bandwidth for match quality but not have both. | ∫ln(1/\|Γ\|)dω ≤ π/(RC) (parallel RC load) | [03/broadband-matching-bode-fano](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/broadband-matching-bode-fano.md) |
| **BOM** | bill of materials | The parts list for a board, with part numbers and quantities. | — | [18/sourcing-and-eval-boards](kb/18-components-parts/sourcing-and-eval-boards.md) |
| **bond wire** | — | The fine wire from a die to its package lead; about 1 nH per mm, a major parasitic in packaged RF parts. | ≈ 1 nH/mm | [04/package-parasitics-bondwires](kb/04-real-components-hf/package-parasitics-bondwires.md) |
| **BPF** | band-pass filter | Filter that passes a band and rejects both sides; the pre-selector in front of an LNA or SDR. | fractional bandwidth FBW = BW/f0 | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **BPSK** | binary phase-shift keying | Two phases (0°, 180°); the most power-efficient simple modulation. | BER = Q(√(2Eb/N0)): 10⁻⁵ at 9.6 dB | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **Brewster angle** | — | Incidence angle at which a vertically (TM, p) polarized wave enters a dielectric with no reflection. Over ground or sea, vertical polarization has a reflection dip here. | θ_B = atan(√εr) | [02/boundary-conditions-reflection-refraction](kb/02-electromagnetics/boundary-conditions-reflection-refraction.md) |
| **bring-up** | — | The first power-on and stage-by-stage verification of a new board, from supplies through to RF performance. | — | [17/rf-bring-up-debug-playbook](kb/17-test-measurement/rf-bring-up-debug-playbook.md) |
| **BSF** | band-stop (notch) filter | Filter that rejects one band and passes the rest. | — | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **BT** | bandwidth–time product | The 3 dB bandwidth of the Gaussian filter in GFSK/GMSK times the symbol duration; smaller BT means narrower spectrum but more ISI. (BT is also the radar/LoRa time-bandwidth product TB, and Bluetooth.) | BT 0.3 (GSM), 0.5 (Bluetooth) | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **Butler matrix** | — | A passive network of hybrids and fixed phase shifters that forms N fixed orthogonal beams from N elements. | beam crossover −3.70 dB (N = 4), −3.87 dB (N = 8) | [12/beamforming-architectures](kb/12-phased-arrays/beamforming-architectures.md) |
| **Butterworth filter** | — | Maximally flat passband response; moderate roll-off. | −3 dB at cut-off; 20N dB/decade | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **BVD model** | Butterworth–Van Dyke model | Electrical model of a crystal or acoustic resonator: a motional series RLC in parallel with the electrode capacitance C0. | series resonance fs = 1/(2π√(L1C1)) | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **BW (LoRa)** | LoRa bandwidth | The chirp's swept bandwidth (7.8–500 kHz; 125 kHz is common); it equals the chip rate. | BW125 = 125 kHz | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |

## C

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **C/A code** | coarse/acquisition code | GPS's civil ranging code on L1: a 1023-chip Gold code repeating every 1 ms. | one chip ≈ 293 m | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **C/N0** | carrier-to-noise-density ratio | Received carrier power divided by the noise PSD, in dB-Hz; bandwidth-independent link quality. | GNSS open sky 40–50 dB-Hz | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **C0G** | class-1 ceramic dielectric (also NP0) | The stable MLCC dielectric: 0 ± 30 ppm/°C, no DC-bias or ageing loss, low loss. Use it for anything that sets a frequency or impedance. | 0 ± 30 ppm/°C | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **CA-CFAR** | cell-averaging CFAR | CFAR that averages the reference cells; optimal in uniform noise but masks closely spaced targets. (Variants: GO greatest-of, SO smallest-of, OS ordered-statistic.) | — | [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md) |
| **CAD** | channel activity detection | A fast LoRa scan that checks for LoRa symbols on a channel without full reception (SX126x detects preamble and data; SX127x preamble only). (CAD also means computer-aided design.) | — | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **CAF** | cross-ambiguity function | Ambiguity function computed between a reference signal and a received signal (passive radar, TDOA/FDOA). (CAF is also the cyclic autocorrelation function in signal identification.) | — | [11/ambiguity-function-waveforms](kb/11-radar/ambiguity-function-waveforms.md) |
| **calibration (VNA)** | — | Measuring known standards to solve for the VNA's systematic errors (directivity, source match, tracking …) and remove them from later measurements. | — | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **canonical values** | — | The KB's single list of constants, design values and sign/unit conventions that every page must match (T0 = 290 K, e^{jωt}, dBi, E/N0 budgets, IMDA limits …). Check it before quoting a number. | — | [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) |
| **carrier phase** | — | GNSS measurement of the received carrier's accumulated phase; mm-level precise but with an unknown whole number of cycles (the ambiguity). | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **carrier recovery** | — | Estimating and tracking the received carrier's frequency and phase so coherent demodulation works. | — | [10/synchronization-carrier-timing](kb/10-comms-modulation/synchronization-carrier-timing.md) |
| **Carson's rule** | — | Estimate of the bandwidth holding about 98 % of an FM signal's power. | B ≈ 2(Δf + f_m) | [10/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb](kb/10-comms-modulation/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb.md) |
| **cascade analysis** | — | Stage-by-stage budget of gain, noise figure, compression and intercept points through a receiver to find which stage limits sensitivity or linearity. | 1/IIP3 = Σ G_before/IIP3_i | [07/receiver-cascade-analysis](kb/07-noise-linearity/receiver-cascade-analysis.md) |
| **cascode** | — | A common-emitter (or common-source) stage stacked under a common-base (gate) stage; high gain and bandwidth with good reverse isolation. | — | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **cavity filter** | — | Filter made of coupled metal cavities; very high Q and power handling, large, used at repeaters and base stations. | — | [06/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip.md) |
| **cavity resonator** | — | A closed metal box that resonates at frequencies set by its dimensions; behaves like a very high-Q LC circuit. Also unwanted box modes in shield cans. | rectangular TE101 f = (c/2)√(1/a² + 1/d²) | [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md) |
| **CB** | common base | BJT stage driven at the emitter: low input impedance, no Miller effect, wideband. (CG = common gate.) | — | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **CC** | common collector (emitter follower) | BJT buffer with voltage gain just under 1 and low output impedance. (CD = common drain / source follower.) | — | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **CC1101** | — | TI sub-GHz FSK/OOK transceiver; a cheap, flexible alternative to LoRa for higher data rates. | — | [15/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4.md) |
| **CCDF** | complementary cumulative distribution function | Probability that a signal's instantaneous power exceeds a given level above its average; the standard way to state PAPR. | — | [10/ofdm](kb/10-comms-modulation/ofdm.md) |
| **CCR** | co-channel rejection | How strong an interferer on the same channel can be relative to the wanted signal before reception fails; for LoRa it depends on SF. | SX1262 5–19 dB | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **CCSDS** | Consultative Committee for Space Data Systems | Body whose space-link standards (TM/TC framing, convolutional/RS/LDPC coding) are a reference for telemetry design. | — | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **CDMA** | code-division multiple access | Users share the same band at the same time, separated by different spreading codes (GPS, 3G). | — | [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md) |
| **CE** | common emitter | BJT stage with the emitter shared by input and output: voltage and current gain, inverting. (CS = common source for FETs.) Also CE marking — see the ambiguous-acronyms section. | A_v ≈ −g_m R_C | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **CE marking** | Conformité Européenne | The EU conformity mark; for radios it means compliance with the Radio Equipment Directive (RED) via harmonised ETSI standards. | — | [19/certification-fcc-ce-imda](kb/19-regulations-safety/certification-fcc-ce-imda.md) |
| **CEP** | circular error probable | Radius of the circle containing 50 % of position fixes. | u-blox "1.5 m CEP" (24 h static) | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **ceramic filter** | — | Filter made of coupled dielectric (coaxial ceramic) resonators; moderate size and loss, good power handling. | — | [18/filter-parts-saw-ceramic-ltcc](kb/18-components-parts/filter-parts-saw-ceramic-ltcc.md) |
| **CFAR** | constant false-alarm rate | Detection that sets each cell's threshold from the noise level estimated in neighbouring cells, so the false-alarm rate stays fixed as noise and clutter change. | CA-CFAR threshold factor α = N(P_FA^{−1/N} − 1) | [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md) |
| **CFO** | carrier frequency offset | Difference between the received carrier frequency and the receiver's LO, from crystal errors and Doppler. | 10 ppm at 915 MHz = 9.15 kHz | [10/synchronization-carrier-timing](kb/10-comms-modulation/synchronization-carrier-timing.md) |
| **CFR** | Code of Federal Regulations (US) | The codified US rules; radio rules are in Title 47 (47 CFR Parts 2, 15, 18, 97). | — | [19/certification-fcc-ce-imda](kb/19-regulations-safety/certification-fcc-ce-imda.md) |
| **CFRP** | carbon-fibre-reinforced polymer | Conductive composite used for rocket airframes; it shields and detunes antennas inside it, so antennas go outside or in a fibreglass section. | — | [08/antennas-on-rockets](kb/08-antennas/antennas-on-rockets.md) |
| **charge pump** | — | PLL circuit that pushes or pulls current pulses into the loop filter according to the PFD's output. | I_CP | [06/vco-and-pll-synthesizers](kb/06-rf-circuits/vco-and-pll-synthesizers.md) |
| **Chebyshev filter** | — | Equal ripple in the passband for a steeper roll-off than Butterworth of the same order. | passband ripple ↔ return loss: RL = −10 log(1 − 10^{−Lr/10}) | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **Chebyshev polynomial** | — | Polynomials that ripple between ±1 then grow fast; they give equal-ripple filters and the Dolph–Chebyshev array taper. | — | [00/special-functions-bessel-sinc](kb/00-math-foundations/special-functions-bessel-sinc.md) |
| **chip** | — | One element of a spreading sequence; the chip rate exceeds the data rate by the spreading factor. | GPS C/A 1.023 Mchip/s | [10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md) |
| **chip antenna** | — | A small ceramic SMD antenna; most of its radiation actually comes from the PCB ground plane, so layout and keep-out decide its performance. | — | [08/pcb-and-chip-antennas](kb/08-antennas/pcb-and-chip-antennas.md) |
| **Chu limit** | Chu–McLean limit | Lower bound on the Q (so upper bound on bandwidth) of an antenna that fits inside a sphere of radius a; small antennas are narrow-band or lossy. | Q ≥ 1/(ka)³ + 1/(ka); CP ½[1/(ka)³ + 2/(ka)] | [08/small-antenna-limits](kb/08-antennas/small-antenna-limits.md) |
| **CIC** | cascaded integrator–comb filter | Multiplier-free decimation/interpolation filter of integrators and combs; cheap for large rate changes but droops, so it is followed by a compensating FIR. | N-stage response (sinc)^N | [14/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic](kb/14-sdr-dsp/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic.md) |
| **circulator** | — | Three-port ferrite device that passes power around in one direction only (1→2→3→1); separates transmit and receive or protects a PA. | ≈ 0.25–0.5 dB IL, ≈ 20 dB isolation | [06/circulators-isolators](kb/06-rf-circuits/circulators-isolators.md) |
| **class A** | — | PA that conducts for the whole cycle: most linear, maximum 50 % efficiency. | η_max = 50 % | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **class AB** | — | PA biased slightly on, conducting for a little over half a cycle; the usual linear PA compromise. | η between 50 % and 78.5 % | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **class B** | — | PA that conducts for half a cycle (usually push-pull). | η_max = π/4 = 78.5 % | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **class C** | — | PA that conducts for less than half a cycle; efficient but only for constant-envelope signals (FM, FSK, LoRa). | η → 100 % as conduction angle → 0 (with output falling) | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **class D** | — | Switching PA (two switches) driving a tuned load. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **class E** | — | Single-switch PA whose load network makes the switch turn on at zero voltage and zero voltage slope, reaching high efficiency at RF. | ideal η = 100 %; R = 0.5768 V_CC²/P | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **class F** | — | PA that shapes the voltage and current waveforms with harmonic terminations to raise efficiency. | ideal half-sine current + flat 3rd-harmonic voltage: 90.7 %; 2nd and 3rd only: 81.6 % | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **clock harmonics** | — | Spurs at integer multiples of digital clock frequencies that can land in a receive band (e.g. 25 MHz × 63 = 1575 MHz on a GNSS board). | — | [13/mixed-signal-layout](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/mixed-signal-layout.md) |
| **clutter** | — | Unwanted echoes from ground, sea, rain or buildings that can hide targets. | — | [11/clutter-and-mti](kb/11-radar/clutter-and-mti.md) |
| **CME** | coronal mass ejection | A burst of solar plasma that can trigger geomagnetic storms 1–3 days later, degrading HF and GNSS. | — | [09/solar-activity-and-space-weather](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/solar-activity-and-space-weather.md) |
| **CMOS** | complementary metal–oxide–semiconductor | The mainstream digital and mixed-signal IC process; most transceivers (SX126x, AD9361) are CMOS. | — | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **CMRR** | common-mode rejection ratio | How well a differential amplifier ignores a signal applied equally to both inputs. | CMRR = A_diff/A_cm (dB) | [05/op-amps-real-limitations](kb/05-analog-electronics/op-amps-real-limitations.md) |
| **CN0566** | ADI Phaser | Analog Devices' 8-element 10–11 GHz phased-array and radar learning kit (ADAR1000 beamformers, driven by a Pluto). | — | [12/diy-phased-array-projects](kb/12-phased-arrays/diy-phased-array-projects.md) |
| **CNR** | carrier-to-noise ratio | Ratio of carrier (modulated signal) power to noise power in the receiver bandwidth; same idea as SNR before demodulation. | C/N = C/N0 − 10 log₁₀B | [09/link-budget-methodology](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-budget-methodology.md) |
| **co-site interference** | — | Interference between transmitters and receivers mounted close together (rocket avionics, a launch site, a ground station); includes desense, blocking and transmitter intermodulation. | — | [06/co-site-interference-and-transmitter-intermod](kb/06-rf-circuits/co-site-interference-and-transmitter-intermod.md) |
| **coax** | coaxial cable | Inner conductor inside a concentric shield, separated by dielectric; carries a TEM wave with the field fully enclosed. | Z0 = (60/√εr)·ln(b/a) | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **COBS** | consistent overhead byte stuffing | Byte framing that removes zero bytes from a packet so 0x00 can mark frame boundaries on a serial link. | overhead ≤ 1 byte per 254 | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **COCOM limits** | — | Export-control limits that make civil GNSS receivers stop reporting fixes above a speed and altitude (classically 1000 knots ≈ 515 m/s and 18 km). u-blox's envelope is 500 m/s, 80 km (50 km older firmware), 4 g. | design for a speed-only cut-off | [21/gnss-on-rockets-dynamics-and-limits](kb/21-gnss/gnss-on-rockets-dynamics-and-limits.md) |
| **CODATA** | Committee on Data for Science and Technology | The body whose periodic least-squares adjustment gives the recommended values of physical constants; the KB uses CODATA 2022. | η0 = 376.730 Ω; ε0 = 8.8541878188×10⁻¹² F/m | [02/electromagnetic-units-and-constants](kb/02-electromagnetics/electromagnetic-units-and-constants.md) |
| **code rate** | — | Fraction of transmitted bits that carry information; LoRa writes it as 4/5 … 4/8. | r = k/n | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **coding gain** | — | Reduction in required Eb/N0 that a code provides at a given error rate. | — | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **coherence bandwidth** | — | Frequency span over which the channel fades roughly together. | B_c ≈ 1/(5τ_rms) | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **coherence time** | — | Time over which the channel stays roughly constant; inversely related to Doppler spread. | T_c ≈ 0.423/f_D | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **coherent detection** | — | Detection that uses knowledge of the carrier phase (needs carrier recovery); non-coherent detection ignores phase and costs about 1 dB or more. | — | [10/matched-filter-detection](kb/10-comms-modulation/matched-filter-detection.md) |
| **cold-source method** | gain method | Noise-figure measurement from the output noise density with the input terminated, given the device gain; needs only a spectrum analyser with low DANL. | NF = N_out(dBm/Hz) + 174 − G | [17/noise-figure-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/noise-figure-measurement.md) |
| **Colpitts oscillator** | — | LC oscillator whose feedback comes from a capacitive divider across the tank; very common at RF and with crystals. | — | [05/oscillators](kb/05-analog-electronics/oscillators.md) |
| **comb generator** | — | Source of a dense set of harmonics of a reference frequency, used as a frequency and emissions reference. | — | [17/signal-generators-and-sources](kb/17-test-measurement/signal-generators-and-sources.md) |
| **common mode** | — | Current or voltage that flows in the same direction on both conductors of a pair (or on the outside of a coax shield); the main cause of cable radiation. | CM radiation ∝ f·L·I_CM (grows faster than DM) | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **common-mode choke** | — | A ferrite core with both conductors of a pair (or a coax) passing through it: it blocks current flowing the same way on both (common mode) but not the wanted differential current. | — | [04/ferrites-and-beads](kb/04-real-components-hf/ferrites-and-beads.md) |
| **condition number** | — | Ratio of a matrix's largest to smallest singular value; large values mean small measurement errors blow up in the solution (e.g. VNA calibration, GNSS geometry). | κ = σ_max/σ_min | [00/matrices-and-linear-algebra-for-networks](kb/00-math-foundations/matrices-and-linear-algebra-for-networks.md) |
| **conducted test** | — | Measuring a radio through a cable and attenuators instead of over the air, to separate radio performance from antenna effects. | — | [17/rf-bring-up-debug-playbook](kb/17-test-measurement/rf-bring-up-debug-playbook.md) |
| **conformal array** | — | An array whose elements follow a curved surface (cylinder, rocket body); each element points a different way. | — | [12/conformal-and-cylindrical-arrays](kb/12-phased-arrays/conformal-and-cylindrical-arrays.md) |
| **constellation** | — | Plot of a modulation's symbol points on the I/Q plane; noise, phase noise and distortion show as spread or warping. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **conversion loss** | — | Ratio of RF input power to IF output power of a passive mixer (negative of conversion gain). | diode DBM 5–7 dB | [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md) |
| **convolution** | — | The operation that gives an LTI system's output from its input and impulse response; multiplication in the frequency domain. | y(t) = ∫h(τ)x(t−τ)dτ ⇔ Y = HX | [00/linear-systems-convolution](kb/00-math-foundations/linear-systems-convolution.md) |
| **convolutional code** | — | FEC that encodes a continuous bit stream with a shift register; decoded by the Viterbi algorithm. | K = 7, r = ½ (CCSDS/NASA standard) | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **CORDIC** | coordinate rotation digital computer | Shift-and-add algorithm for rotations, sines/cosines and magnitudes; used in FPGA NCOs and demodulators. | ≈ 1 bit of accuracy per iteration | [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md) |
| **corner reflector** | — | Three mutually perpendicular plates (trihedral) that return energy straight back over a wide angle; a radar calibration target. | trihedral σ = 4πa⁴/(3λ²) | [11/radar-cross-section](kb/11-radar/radar-cross-section.md) |
| **correlator** | — | Multiplies the incoming signal by a local code replica and integrates; the peak tells code alignment. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking.md) |
| **Costas loop** | — | A PLL variant that recovers the carrier of a suppressed-carrier (BPSK/QPSK) signal; also used in GNSS tracking. | — | [10/synchronization-carrier-timing](kb/10-comms-modulation/synchronization-carrier-timing.md) |
| **COTS** | commercial off-the-shelf | Bought-in parts or modules rather than custom designs. | — | [18/rf-modules](kb/18-components-parts/rf-modules.md) |
| **coupling coefficient** | — | Fraction of one coil's flux that links another, between 0 and 1. | M = k√(L₁L₂) | [01/transformers-coupled-inductors](kb/01-circuit-theory/transformers-coupled-inductors.md) |
| **Courant condition** | Courant–Friedrichs–Lewy limit | Stability limit on an explicit time-domain solver's time step: a wave must not cross more than one cell per step. | Δt ≤ 1/(c√(1/Δx² + 1/Δy² + 1/Δz²)) | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **covariance matrix** | — | Matrix of correlations between array channels; adaptive beamformers and subspace methods start from it. | R = E[xxᴴ] | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **CP** | circular polarization | Polarization whose electric field rotates once per cycle with constant length; insensitive to the roll of a rocket, so favoured for ground stations and GNSS. (CP also means cyclic prefix in OFDM and charge pump in PLLs.) | linear antenna on a CP wave: −3 dB | [08/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization](kb/08-antennas/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization.md) |
| **CPFSK** | continuous-phase FSK | FSK in which the phase never jumps between symbols, keeping the spectrum compact. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **CPI** | coherent processing interval | The block of pulses or chirps processed together coherently (one Doppler FFT). | velocity resolution = λ/(2T_CPI) | [11/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes](kb/11-radar/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes.md) |
| **CPW** | coplanar waveguide | A trace with ground on both sides in the same layer. | — | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **CPWG** | grounded coplanar waveguide | Coplanar waveguide with an extra ground plane underneath, stitched with vias; the usual RF trace on thin 4-layer boards because it is compact and well isolated. | — | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **CR** | coding rate | LoRa's Hamming-type FEC rate, written 4/5 … 4/8. Semtech's time-on-air formula uses an integer CR = 1…4 for the same four settings, so "CR 1" and "CR 4/5" mean the same thing. | 4/(4+CR) | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **CRB** | Cramér–Rao bound | The smallest variance any unbiased estimator can achieve, set by the Fisher information. Gives best-case accuracy for frequency, phase, delay and angle estimates. | frequency CRB ∝ 1/(SNR·N³) | [00/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb](kb/00-math-foundations/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb.md) |
| **CRC** | cyclic redundancy check | A checksum computed by polynomial division that detects (not corrects) errors; LoRa uses a 16-bit CRC. | CRC-16 misses ≈ 1 in 65 536 random corruptions | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **critical frequency** | — | The highest frequency a layer reflects at vertical incidence (f_oF2 for the F2 layer). | f_c ≈ 9√N_max Hz (N in m⁻³) | [09/ionospheric-hf-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/ionospheric-hf-propagation.md) |
| **cross-modulation** | — | A strong modulated interferer transferring its modulation onto a weak wanted signal through third-order non-linearity. | — | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **cross-polarization** | — | Radiation in the polarization orthogonal to the intended one. | XPD = 20 log(\|E_co\|/\|E_cross\|) | [08/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization](kb/08-antennas/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization.md) |
| **crystal** | quartz crystal resonator | A quartz plate that resonates mechanically at a precise frequency; the reference behind every LoRa, GNSS and PLL clock. | — | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **crystal filter** | — | Narrow band-pass filter built from coupled quartz crystals; gives the sharp IF selectivity of classic receivers. | — | [06/duplexers-diplexers-and-crystal-filters](kb/06-rf-circuits/duplexers-diplexers-and-crystal-filters.md) |
| **CSMA** | carrier-sense multiple access | Listen before transmitting and back off if the channel is busy (Wi-Fi CSMA/CA). | — | [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md) |
| **CSS** | chirp spread spectrum | Spreading each symbol into a frequency sweep (chirp) across the whole bandwidth; LoRa's modulation. Robust to Doppler and multipath. | LoRa: TB = 2^SF | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **CTE** | coefficient of thermal expansion | How much a material grows per degree; mismatch between laminate, copper and components stresses vias and solder joints. | ppm/°C | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **CTIA** | — | Mobile-industry association whose OTA test plan defines TRP and TIS measurements. | — | [08/antenna-measurement](kb/08-antennas/antenna-measurement.md) |
| **curl** | — | Vector derivative measuring local rotation of a field; Maxwell's equations say a changing magnetic field has an electric curl and vice versa. | ∇×E = −∂B/∂t | [00/vector-calculus-for-em](kb/00-math-foundations/vector-calculus-for-em.md) |
| **cut-off frequency** | — | Frequency below which a waveguide mode cannot propagate (it decays instead). Coax also has a TE11 cut-off above which a second mode appears. | coax TE11: f_c ≈ c/[π√εr·(d+D)/2] (d, D = inner/outer diameters; LMR-400 ≈ 16.3 GHz) | [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md) |
| **CW radar** | continuous-wave radar | Radar that transmits continuously; an unmodulated CW radar measures only Doppler (speed), not range. | f_D = 2v/λ: 24 GHz → 160 Hz per m/s | [11/cw-and-doppler-radar](kb/11-radar/cw-and-doppler-radar.md) |
| **cyclic prefix** | — | A copy of the end of each OFDM symbol prepended to it, so multipath echoes shorter than the prefix cause no ISI and equalisation is one tap per subcarrier. | CP ≥ maximum delay spread | [10/ofdm](kb/10-comms-modulation/ofdm.md) |
| **cyclostationary analysis** | — | Detecting and classifying signals from periodicities in their statistics (symbol rate, carrier) using the cyclic autocorrelation; works below the noise. | — | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |

## D

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **D-layer absorption** | — | Daytime absorption of HF in the lowest ionospheric layer; worst at low HF, and the cause of HF blackouts after solar flares. | loss ∝ 1/f² | [09/ionospheric-hf-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/ionospheric-hf-propagation.md) |
| **D-RAP** | D-Region Absorption Prediction | NOAA product mapping HF absorption after solar flares. | — | [09/solar-activity-and-space-weather](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/solar-activity-and-space-weather.md) |
| **DAC** | digital-to-analog converter | Converts numbers into a voltage or current waveform; its output has images at multiples of the sample rate and a sinc (zero-order-hold) droop. | ZOH droop at fs/2: −3.92 dB | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **DANL** | displayed average noise level | The noise floor a spectrum analyser displays, normalised to 1 Hz RBW; sets its weak-signal limit. | DANL ≈ −174 + NF_SA dBm/Hz | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **DARE** | discrete algebraic Riccati equation | Equation whose solution is a Kalman filter's steady-state error covariance (and gain). | — | [00/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation](kb/00-math-foundations/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation.md) |
| **Darlington** | — | Two cascaded emitter followers acting as one high-gain transistor; MMIC gain blocks use a Darlington pair with resistive feedback to get 50 Ω broadband. | — | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **data cube** | radar data cube | The 3-D array of radar samples: fast time (range) × slow time (pulses) × channels (antennas), processed by FFTs along each axis. | — | [11/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes](kb/11-radar/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes.md) |
| **dB** | decibel | Ten times the base-10 logarithm of a power ratio. It turns the multiplications of a link or noise budget into additions; a voltage ratio uses 20 log because power goes as voltage squared. | 10 log₁₀(P₂/P₁) = 20 log₁₀(V₂/V₁) (same impedance); 3 dB ≈ ×2, 10 dB = ×10 | [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md) |
| **dB-Hz** | decibel-hertz | Unit of carrier-to-noise-density ratio C/N0: signal power divided by noise power in 1 Hz. Bandwidth-independent, so it is the standard GNSS signal-quality number. | open-sky GNSS 40–50 dB-Hz (≥ 45 healthy, < 35 fault) | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **dB/km** | decibels per kilometre | Specific attenuation of a propagation path (gases, rain). | oxygen at 60 GHz ≈ 14.7 dB/km (sea level) | [09/atmospheric-rain-attenuation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/atmospheric-rain-attenuation.md) |
| **dB/m** | decibels per metre | Attenuation per unit length of a cable or transmission line. | RG-58 ≈ 0.29 dB/m, RG-316 ≈ 0.72 dB/m at 433 MHz | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **dBc** | decibels relative to the carrier | Level of a spur, harmonic or sideband relative to the main carrier power. Negative numbers mean below the carrier. | harmonic at −40 dBc is 10⁴ times weaker than the carrier | [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md) |
| **dBc/Hz** | decibels relative to the carrier per hertz | Unit of single-sideband phase noise: noise power in a 1 Hz band at a given offset from the carrier, relative to the carrier. | 𝓛(f) e.g. −100 dBc/Hz at 10 kHz offset; integrate over offset to get jitter | [07/phase-noise-and-jitter](kb/07-noise-linearity/phase-noise-and-jitter.md) |
| **dBd** | decibels relative to a half-wave dipole | Antenna gain compared with a lossless half-wave dipole. Common in amateur and broadcast specs, where it makes numbers look 2.15 dB smaller than dBi. | dBd = dBi − 2.15 | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **DBF** | digital beamforming | Digitising every element and forming beams in software; flexible (many simultaneous beams, adaptive nulls) but needs one receiver per element. | uncorrelated noise improves by 10 log N | [12/beamforming-architectures](kb/12-phased-arrays/beamforming-architectures.md) |
| **dBFS** | decibels relative to full scale | Signal level relative to the largest signal an ADC or DAC can represent (0 dBFS = full-scale sine). | an ideal 12-bit ADC: SQNR = 74 dB, noise floor ≈ −74 dBFS in the Nyquist band | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **dBFS/Hz** | dBFS per hertz | Noise spectral density of a data converter relative to full scale, in a 1 Hz band. Used to compare ADCs at different sample rates. | NSD = −SNR − 10 log₁₀(fs/2) dBFS/Hz | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **dBi** | decibels relative to an isotropic radiator | Antenna gain compared with an ideal antenna that radiates equally in all directions. The KB quotes gain in dBi unless it says otherwise. | half-wave dipole 2.15 dBi; dBi = dBd + 2.15 | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **dBic** | decibels relative to an isotropic circularly polarised antenna | Gain of a circularly polarised antenna measured with a matching circular reference. A linear antenna receiving the same CP wave sees 3 dB less. | dBic (CP) ≈ dBi (linear) + 3 dB for the same CP wave | [08/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization](kb/08-antennas/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization.md), [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) |
| **dBm** | decibels relative to 1 milliwatt | Absolute power on a log scale, referenced to 1 mW. The KB's default unit for signal power. | dBm = 10 log₁₀(P/1 mW); 0 dBm = 1 mW, 30 dBm = 1 W, 33 dBm = 2 W | [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md) |
| **DBM** | double-balanced mixer | Diode-ring (or transistor) mixer that cancels both the LO and RF at the IF port; the classic passive mixer. | conversion loss 5–7 dB (ideal switching 3.92 dB); "Level 7" = +7 dBm LO | [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md) |
| **dBm/Hz** | dBm per hertz | Power spectral density on a log scale; the natural unit for noise floors. | thermal noise kT0 = −174 dBm/Hz at 290 K | [07/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature](kb/07-noise-linearity/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature.md) |
| **dBm/MHz** | dBm per megahertz | Power spectral density in a 1 MHz band; used in UWB and radar regulations. | 77–81 GHz SG exemption: mean ≤ −3 dBm/MHz | [19/singapore-imda-rules](kb/19-regulations-safety/singapore-imda-rules.md) |
| **DBPSK** | differential BPSK | BPSK where the data is in the phase change between symbols, so no absolute phase reference is needed. | ≈ 1 dB worse than coherent BPSK at 10⁻⁵ | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **dBsm** | decibels relative to one square metre | Radar cross-section on a log scale. | 1 m² = 0 dBsm; person ≈ −5 dBsm (mm-wave), small drone ≈ −20 to −5 dBsm | [11/radar-cross-section](kb/11-radar/radar-cross-section.md) |
| **dBW** | decibels relative to 1 watt | Absolute power referenced to 1 W; used in satellite and GNSS specifications. | dBW = dBm − 30; GPS L1 C/A minimum −158.5 dBW = −128.5 dBm | [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md) |
| **dBZ** | decibels of radar reflectivity factor | Weather-radar unit of rain reflectivity Z (mm⁶/m³). | ≈ 40 dBZ heavy rain | [09/atmospheric-rain-attenuation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/atmospheric-rain-attenuation.md) |
| **dBµV** | decibels relative to 1 microvolt | Voltage level on a log scale, common in EMC receivers and in receiver sensitivity specs. | in 50 Ω: dBµV = dBm + 107 | [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md) |
| **dBµV/m** | decibels relative to 1 microvolt per metre | Electric-field strength on a log scale, used by EMC limits and field-strength regulations. | E[dBµV/m] = P[dBm] + 107 + AF[dB/m] (antenna factor); far field E = √(30·EIRP)/d | [02/electromagnetic-units-and-constants](kb/02-electromagnetics/electromagnetic-units-and-constants.md) |
| **DC** | direct current | Constant (zero-frequency) current or voltage; bias and supply. In spectra "DC" means 0 Hz. | — | [01/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance](kb/01-circuit-theory/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance.md) |
| **DC bias derating** | — | The drop in a class-2 MLCC's capacitance when DC voltage is applied; a "10 µF" 0603 can deliver a fraction of that at its rated voltage. | — | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **DC block** | — | A series capacitor that passes RF and blocks DC; chosen near its series resonance so its impedance is lowest at the operating frequency. | — | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **DC spike** | — | A spur at 0 Hz (the tuned centre) of a zero-IF SDR's spectrum, from LO leakage and DC offsets; offset tuning moves the wanted signal away from it. | — | [14/iq-complex-baseband](kb/14-sdr-dsp/iq-complex-baseband.md) |
| **DCR** | DC resistance | Winding resistance of an inductor or ferrite at DC; sets its I²R loss and voltage drop. | — | [04/inductors-at-rf](kb/04-real-components-hf/inductors-at-rf.md) |
| **DDC** | digital down-converter | NCO mixer plus decimating filters that select one channel from a wideband ADC stream and bring it to complex baseband. | — | [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md) |
| **DDS** | direct digital synthesis | Generating a waveform from a phase accumulator, a sine lookup and a DAC; fine frequency steps and fast hopping, with spurs from truncation and DAC images. | resolution = f_clk/2^N | [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md) |
| **DE** | driven element | The fed element of a Yagi (the others are parasitic). (In LoRa formulas DE is the low-data-rate-optimise flag.) | — | [08/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic](kb/08-antennas/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic.md) |
| **de-embedding** | — | Mathematically removing a fixture's measured or modelled S-parameters to get the DUT's own. | T_DUT = T_A⁻¹·T_meas·T_B⁻¹ | [17/fixtures-and-deembedding](kb/17-test-measurement/fixtures-and-deembedding.md) |
| **dechirp** | — | Mixing a received chirp with a reference chirp so it collapses to a single tone (FMCW beat, stretch processing, LoRa demodulation). | — | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **decimation** | — | Low-pass filtering then keeping every M-th sample, to reduce sample rate. | — | [14/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic](kb/14-sdr-dsp/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic.md) |
| **decoupling** | bypassing | Capacitors placed close to IC supply pins (with short via paths) to supply fast current and keep RF and switching noise off the supply rail. | — | [13/power-distribution-decoupling-for-rf](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/power-distribution-decoupling-for-rf.md) |
| **delay spread** | — | Spread of arrival times of multipath copies; if comparable to a symbol, it causes ISI. | coherence bandwidth B_c ≈ 1/(5τ_rms) | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **DEM** | digital elevation model | Terrain-height grid (e.g. SRTM 30 m) used for path profiles and diffraction loss. | — | [09/diffraction-knife-edge](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/diffraction-knife-edge.md) |
| **desensitisation** | desense | The rise in a receiver's effective noise floor (fall in sensitivity) caused by a nearby transmitter or strong signal. | — | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **detector (SA)** | — | How a spectrum analyser reduces samples in each display bin: peak, sample, average (RMS or log) or quasi-peak. Log averaging reads noise 2.51 dB low. | log-average noise bias −2.51 dB | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **Df** | dissipation factor | Laminate-datasheet name for the loss tangent tan δ. | FR-4 0.015–0.022; RO4350B 0.0037 @ 10 GHz | [02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md) |
| **DF** | direction finding | Locating a transmitter's bearing (e.g. a landed rocket's beacon) with a directional antenna or an array. (DF also means dissipation factor of a dielectric or capacitor.) | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **DFM** | design for manufacture | Designing within a fab's capabilities (minimum trace/space, drill, annular ring, finishes) so the board can be built reliably. | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **DFT** | discrete Fourier transform | Fourier transform of N samples, giving N frequency bins spaced fs/N apart. | X[k] = Σ x[n]e^{−j2πkn/N}; bin spacing fs/N | [00/fourier-series-and-transform](kb/00-math-foundations/fourier-series-and-transform.md) |
| **differential mode** | — | The wanted signal current: equal and opposite on the two conductors of a pair. Its radiation is small because the fields nearly cancel. | DM radiation ∝ f²·A·I | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **differential pair** | — | Two matched transistors sharing a tail current; amplifies the difference between inputs and rejects what is common. Core of op-amps, Gilbert mixers and LVDS. | — | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **diode detector** | — | Schottky-diode circuit that turns RF into a DC voltage; square-law at low power, linear (envelope) at high power. | — | [06/detectors-and-power-meters](kb/06-rf-circuits/detectors-and-power-meters.md) |
| **diplexer** | — | Filter pair that splits or combines two frequency bands (e.g. 433 MHz and 915 MHz) on one antenna port. | — | [06/duplexers-diplexers-and-crystal-filters](kb/06-rf-circuits/duplexers-diplexers-and-crystal-filters.md) |
| **dipole** | half-wave dipole | Two λ/4 conductors fed in the middle: the reference antenna, with a figure-8 pattern around its axis. | 2.15 dBi; 73.1 + j42.5 Ω at exactly λ/2; ≈ 72 Ω resonant at 0.47–0.49λ | [08/dipoles-and-monopoles](kb/08-antennas/dipoles-and-monopoles.md) |
| **direct sampling** | — | Digitising the RF (or an IF) directly with a fast ADC and doing all mixing digitally. | — | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **directional coupler** | — | Four-port that samples a fraction of the wave travelling one way on a line, with little of the reverse wave. | directivity = isolation − coupling | [06/couplers-dividers-combiners](kb/06-rf-circuits/couplers-dividers-combiners.md) |
| **directivity** | — | How much an antenna concentrates radiated power in its best direction compared with an isotropic radiator, ignoring losses. | D = 4πU_max/P_rad; dipole 1.64 (2.15 dBi) | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **discone** | — | A disc above a cone; a vertically polarised, very wideband omnidirectional antenna for scanners and SDRs. | — | [08/multiband-and-wideband-antennas](kb/08-antennas/multiband-and-wideband-antennas.md) |
| **displacement current** | — | The term ∂D/∂t in Ampère's law: a changing electric field acts like a current. It lets current "flow" through a capacitor and makes radiation possible. | I_D = C dV/dt | [02/maxwells-equations](kb/02-electromagnetics/maxwells-equations.md) |
| **diversity** | — | Using two or more independently fading copies (antennas, polarizations, frequencies, times) and combining or selecting them to cut fade depth. | — | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **Dk** | dielectric constant | Laminate-datasheet name for relative permittivity εr. "Process" Dk is a bulk test value; "design" Dk is what circuits actually see and should be used for line widths. | RO4350B design Dk 3.66 (process 3.48); FR-4 4.3–4.7 | [02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md) |
| **DL6WU** | — | Günter Hoch's long-Yagi design method and gain formula; the KB's ground-station Yagis use it. | G ≈ 9.2 + 3.39 ln(B/λ) dBd | [08/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic](kb/08-antennas/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic.md) |
| **DLL** | delay-locked loop | The loop that keeps a receiver's code replica aligned with the incoming code using early, prompt and late correlators. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking.md) |
| **DNP** | do not populate | BOM/schematic marking for footprints left empty (e.g. optional matching parts). | — | [20/design-review-checklists](kb/20-projects-recipes/design-review-checklists.md) |
| **DOA** | direction of arrival | Estimating the angle a signal comes from with an array. | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **DOF** | degrees of freedom | Number of independent variables in a model or adaptive filter (6-DOF trajectory sim; STAP adaptive DOF). | — | [11/clutter-and-mti](kb/11-radar/clutter-and-mti.md) |
| **Doherty amplifier** | — | Two PAs (carrier and peaking) combined so efficiency stays high over several dB of back-off. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **DOP** | dilution of precision | Factor by which satellite geometry multiplies ranging error into position error (GDOP, PDOP, HDOP, VDOP, TDOP). | σ_pos ≈ DOP × UERE | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **Doppler rate** | — | How fast the Doppler shift changes, set by acceleration along the line of sight; it is what drags LoRa and GNSS receivers off frequency during boost. | ḟ = f·a/c: 14.2 Hz/s per g at 433 MHz, 30.2 Hz/s per g at 923 MHz | [15/lora-impairments-doppler-drift-timing](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-impairments-doppler-drift-timing.md) |
| **Doppler shift** | — | Change in received frequency caused by relative motion. | f_D = f·v/c: 300 m/s at 433 MHz → 433 Hz | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **double difference** | — | Differencing GNSS observations between two receivers and two satellites, cancelling both clocks and most common errors; the core of RTK. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **DPD** | digital predistortion | Pre-distorting the digital baseband signal with the inverse of the PA's non-linearity so the output comes out clean. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **drain efficiency** | — | RF output power divided by DC supply power of a PA (collector efficiency for BJTs). | η_D = P_out/P_DC | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **drive level** | — | Power dissipated in a crystal by its oscillator; too much shifts frequency and ages or cracks the crystal. | typically µW | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **DSA** | digital step attenuator | An attenuator switched in binary steps (e.g. 0.5 dB up to 31.5 dB) by a digital word; used for AGC and array calibration. | 6-bit 31.5 dB in 0.5 dB steps | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **DSP** | digital signal processing | Processing sampled signals with arithmetic (filters, FFTs, demodulators), in software, a DSP chip or an FPGA. | — | [14/digital-filters-fir-iir](kb/14-sdr-dsp/digital-filters-fir-iir.md) |
| **DSP48** | — | Xilinx FPGA multiply–accumulate block; the resource that limits FIR and FFT size. | — | [14/fpga-for-sdr](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fpga-for-sdr.md) |
| **DSSS** | direct-sequence spread spectrum | Multiplying data by a fast pseudo-random chip sequence to spread it over a wide band; the receiver despreads and gains the chip-to-bit ratio. GPS uses it. | G_proc = R_c/R_b | [10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md) |
| **DTFT** | discrete-time Fourier transform | Fourier transform of an infinite sampled sequence; a continuous, periodic function of frequency. The DFT samples it. | period fs in frequency | [00/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems](kb/00-math-foundations/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems.md) |
| **DUC** | digital up-converter | Interpolating filters plus NCO mixer that move a baseband signal up to an IF for a DAC. | — | [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md) |
| **dummy load** | — | A 50 Ω termination rated for the transmitter's power, so it can be tested without radiating. | — | [17/rf-power-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/rf-power-measurement.md) |
| **duplexer** | — | Filter pair that lets a transmitter and receiver on different frequencies share one antenna. | — | [06/duplexers-diplexers-and-crystal-filters](kb/06-rf-circuits/duplexers-diplexers-and-crystal-filters.md) |
| **DUT** | device under test | The component or system being measured. | — | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **duty cycle** | — | Fraction of time a transmitter is on: pulse width × PRF for a radar; time-on-air fraction for an ISM radio (often limited by regulation). | SG 433 MHz SRD: ≤ 10 % (TS SRD 26a) | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md), [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **DVB-T** | Digital Video Broadcasting – Terrestrial | OFDM digital TV standard; the RTL-SDR dongle was made to receive it, and its transmitters serve as passive-radar illuminators. | — | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |
| **DX** | — | Amateur shorthand for long-distance contacts. | — | [09/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction.md) |
| **dynamic model** | — | Receiver setting that tells its navigation filter what motion to expect (portable, automotive, airborne 1/2/4 g); "Airborne 4 g" is the usual rocket choice. | — | [21/gnss-on-rockets-dynamics-and-limits](kb/21-gnss/gnss-on-rockets-dynamics-and-limits.md) |
| **dynamic range** | — | The span between the weakest signal a receiver can detect and the strongest it can handle, in dB; the upper end can be compression, intermodulation or ADC full scale. | compression DR = IP1dB − N | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |

## E

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
