|---|---|---|---|---|
| **E/N0** | signal energy to noise density ratio | The detection SNR used in KB link and radar budgets (the radar χ); 2E/N0 appears only as the real-passband matched-filter peak. | P_D 0.9, P_FA 10⁻⁶, Swerling 0: 13.2 dB non-coherent, 12.6 dB coherent | [10/matched-filter-detection](kb/10-comms-modulation/matched-filter-detection.md), [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) |
| **E22** | EBYTE E22-400T33D (and family) | EBYTE UART LoRa module around the SX1268 with an external PA (≈ 33 dBm, 2 W); the Space Bar primary FC's 433 MHz downlink. | 33 ± 0.5 dBm; f = 410.125 + CH MHz; prefer CH 24 | [15/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec.md) |
| **E220** | EBYTE E220-900T30D | EBYTE UART LoRa module around the LLCC68 (30 dBm, 1 W) for 850–930 MHz; the Space Bar secondary FC's radio. Its default 873.125 MHz is not a Singapore band. | steps 30/27/24/21 dBm; f = 850.125 + CH MHz; TX ≈ 620 mA | [15/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec.md) |
| **Eb/N0** | energy per bit to noise density ratio | SNR normalised per information bit; lets modulations and codes with different bandwidths be compared fairly. | Eb/N0 = SNR·B/R_b; Shannon limit −1.59 dB | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **EBYTE** | — | Chinese maker of UART-controlled LoRa and FSK modules (E22, E220, E32); convenient but with optimistic datasheet sensitivities. | "−147/−148 dBm" claims are not physical (≈ −128 dBm at 2.4 kb/s) | [15/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec.md) |
| **ECEF** | earth-centred, earth-fixed | Cartesian coordinates rotating with the earth, in which GNSS positions are computed before conversion to latitude/longitude/height or local ENU. | — | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **edge launch** | — | An SMA (or similar) connector clamped to the board edge with its pin on the trace; the transition must be tuned to keep return loss high at GHz. | — | [13/connectors-and-launches](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/connectors-and-launches.md) |
| **EER** | envelope elimination and restoration | Linearisation that amplifies a constant-envelope phase signal efficiently and re-applies the envelope through the supply. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **effective aperture** | — | The area from which an antenna collects power from a passing wave. A fixed-gain antenna has a smaller aperture at higher frequency, which is why free-space path loss grows with f. | A_e = Gλ²/(4π) | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **effective permittivity** | — | The single εr that would give a quasi-TEM line its actual phase velocity; for microstrip it lies between 1 and εr because some field is in air. | λ_g = λ0/√ε_eff | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **EFHW** | end-fed half-wave antenna | A half-wave wire fed at one end through a high-ratio (≈ 49:1) transformer; popular portable HF antenna. | — | [08/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole](kb/08-antennas/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole.md) |
| **EIA** | Electronic Industries Alliance | Former US standards body; its codes survive in capacitor dielectric names (X7R) and E-series values. | — | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **EIRP** | effective isotropic radiated power | Transmit power plus antenna gain (minus feed loss) in a direction: the power an isotropic antenna would need to give the same field. | EIRP = P_t − L_feed + G_t (dBm, dBi) | [09/link-budget-methodology](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-budget-methodology.md) |
| **EKF** | extended Kalman filter | Kalman filter for non-linear models, linearised about the current estimate at each step. | — | [00/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation](kb/00-math-foundations/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation.md) |
| **electrical length** | — | Length of a line measured in wavelengths or degrees of phase at the operating frequency. | θ = βℓ = 360°·ℓ/λ_g | [03/input-impedance-stubs-quarter-wave](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/input-impedance-stubs-quarter-wave.md) |
| **elliptic filter** | Cauer filter | Ripple in both passband and stopband, with transmission zeros; the steepest transition for a given order. | — | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **EM** | electromagnetic | Of electric and magnetic fields together; "EM simulation" means a field solver. | — | [02/maxwells-equations](kb/02-electromagnetics/maxwells-equations.md) |
| **embedded element pattern** | — | Pattern of one element measured inside the array with all others terminated; includes coupling effects. | — | [12/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling](kb/12-phased-arrays/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling.md) |
| **EMC** | electromagnetic compatibility | The ability of equipment to work without being disturbed by, or disturbing, other equipment; covers emissions and immunity. | — | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **EME** | Earth–Moon–Earth (moonbounce) | Amateur communication by reflecting signals off the Moon; needs high EIRP and very low-noise receivers. | path loss 252.1 dB at 144 MHz, 261.6 dB at 432 MHz | [09/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation.md) |
| **EMF** | electromotive force / electromagnetic field | Two uses: induced voltage around a loop (Faraday's law), and, in exposure contexts, the RF field itself. | — | [19/rf-exposure-safety](kb/19-regulations-safety/rf-exposure-safety.md) |
| **EMI** | electromagnetic interference | Unwanted electromagnetic energy that degrades a circuit; the problem EMC design prevents. | — | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **EN** | European Norm | Prefix of European standards (e.g. ETSI EN 300 220). | — | [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **ENBW** | equivalent noise bandwidth | The width of an ideal rectangular filter that passes the same noise power as a real filter or window. | Hann window 1.50 bins (1.76 dB); single-pole RC: (π/2)·f_3dB | [14/fft-spectral-analysis-windows](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fft-spectral-analysis-windows.md) |
| **ENIG** | electroless nickel immersion gold | Flat gold-over-nickel PCB finish; good for fine-pitch parts, but the nickel adds RF loss and can cause PIM at high frequency. | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **ENOB** | effective number of bits | The resolution of an ideal ADC that would give the same SINAD as the real one. | ENOB = (SINAD − 1.76)/6.02 | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **ENR** | excess noise ratio | How much hotter a calibrated noise source is than 290 K when on, in dB; used in Y-factor noise-figure measurement. | ENR = 10 log((T_hot − T0)/T0); common 6 or 15 dB | [17/noise-figure-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/noise-figure-measurement.md) |
| **envelope tracking** | — | Varying a PA's supply voltage with the signal envelope to keep efficiency high at back-off. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **en–in model** | voltage- and current-noise model | Describes an amplifier's noise as a voltage source e_n and a current source i_n at its input; the best source resistance is their ratio. | R_opt = e_n/i_n | [05/low-noise-analog-design](kb/05-analog-electronics/low-noise-analog-design.md) |
| **EPAD** | exposed pad | The metal paddle under a QFN or similar package; it is the RF and thermal ground and needs many vias to the ground plane. | — | [13/thermal-management-rf](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/thermal-management-rf.md) |
| **ephemeris** | — | A satellite's precise orbit and clock parameters, valid for about 2–4 hours; needed before a position can be computed. | 18–36 s to decode (GPS) | [21/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris](kb/21-gnss/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris.md) |
| **EQS** | electro-quasi-static | Slow-field regime dominated by charge and capacitance; magnetic induction negligible. | — | [02/quasi-static-vs-full-wave](kb/02-electromagnetics/quasi-static-vs-full-wave.md) |
| **ERC/REC 70-03** | CEPT recommendation on short-range devices | The CEPT table of European SRD bands and limits (e.g. 863–870 MHz sub-bands with duty-cycle limits). | — | [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **ERP** | effective radiated power | Like EIRP but referenced to a half-wave dipole instead of an isotrope; many regulations (incl. Singapore's SRD rules) use it. | ERP = EIRP − 2.15 dB | [09/link-budget-methodology](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-budget-methodology.md), [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) |
| **error terms** | — | The systematic VNA errors a calibration models: directivity, source match, reflection tracking (one-port); plus load match, transmission tracking and isolation (12-term two-port). | S11M = E_DF + E_RF S11/(1 − E_SF S11) | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **Es/N0** | energy per symbol to noise density ratio | SNR normalised per symbol. | Es/N0 = Eb/N0 + 10 log₁₀(bits/symbol) | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **ESD** | electrostatic discharge | A sudden discharge of static charge (from people or cables) that can destroy RF front ends; HBM and CDM models rate device robustness. | HBM test: 100 pF through 1.5 kΩ | [19/lab-safety-esd-high-power](kb/19-regulations-safety/lab-safety-esd-high-power.md) |
| **ESL** | equivalent series inductance | The parasitic inductance of a capacitor's body, pads and vias; above the self-resonant frequency the capacitor behaves like an inductor. | mounted MLCC: 0201 ≈ 0.25 nH, 0402 ≈ 0.4 nH, 0603 ≈ 0.65 nH | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **ESP32** | — | Espressif's Wi-Fi/Bluetooth microcontroller family (ESP32-S3 on the Space Bar boards); its clocks and radio are a coexistence concern for GNSS and LoRa. | — | [13/mixed-signal-layout](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/mixed-signal-layout.md) |
| **ESPRIT** | estimation of signal parameters via rotational invariance techniques | Subspace DOA method for arrays made of two identical shifted sub-arrays; gives angles directly without a search. | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **ESR** | equivalent series resistance | The resistance that models all losses of a capacitor (or crystal, or inductor) as one series resistor. It sets component Q and heating. | capacitor Q = 1/(ωC·ESR) | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **ETSI** | European Telecommunications Standards Institute | Writes the EU harmonised radio standards, e.g. EN 300 220 for 25–1000 MHz short-range devices. | — | [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **Euler's formula** | — | The identity linking complex exponentials to sines and cosines; the basis of phasors, the Fourier transform and IQ signals. | e^{jθ} = cos θ + j sin θ | [00/complex-numbers-and-phasors](kb/00-math-foundations/complex-numbers-and-phasors.md) |
| **EuRoC** | European Rocketry Challenge | European student rocketry competition in Portugal; EU 433/868 MHz SRD rules apply. | — | [19/international-rules-for-competitions](kb/19-regulations-safety/international-rules-for-competitions.md) |
| **eval board** | evaluation board | A vendor's reference board for trying a chip before designing your own; a known-good layout to copy. | — | [18/sourcing-and-eval-boards](kb/18-components-parts/sourcing-and-eval-boards.md) |
| **EVM** | error vector magnitude | RMS distance between received and ideal constellation points relative to the ideal; one number for all impairments. | EVM_rms ≈ 1/√SNR | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **explicit header** | — | LoRa header that carries payload length, coding rate and CRC flag; implicit mode omits it when both ends are configured identically. | — | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **EXTINT** | external interrupt input | u-blox pin that time-stamps an external event (e.g. ignition) in GNSS time. | — | [21/gnss-timing-1pps](kb/21-gnss/gnss-timing-1pps.md) |
| **eye diagram** | — | Overlaid traces of a demodulated signal one or two symbols wide; an open eye means low ISI and noise. | — | [10/pulse-shaping-isi](kb/10-comms-modulation/pulse-shaping-isi.md) |

## F

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **factor-of-two traps** | 3 dB traps | Places where two correct conventions differ by 2 (3 dB): 𝓛 = S_φ/2, E/N0 vs 2E/N0, real vs complex processing gain, peak vs RMS phasors, ERP vs EIRP (2.15 dB), dBi vs dBic, mean vs median fading. | — | [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) |
| **fade margin** | — | Extra margin reserved for fading so that a link stays up for a stated fraction of the time. | Rayleigh, vs mean power: 10/20/30 dB for 90/99/99.9 % | [09/link-margin-availability](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-margin-availability.md) |
| **fading** | — | Fluctuation of received signal strength with time, position or frequency caused by multipath, shadowing or polarization changes. | — | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **far field** | — | Region far enough from an antenna that fields fall as 1/r, E and H are in phase with E/H = η0, and the pattern no longer changes with distance. | Fraunhofer distance r > 2D²/λ (and r ≫ λ) | [02/near-field-far-field](kb/02-electromagnetics/near-field-far-field.md) |
| **Faraday rotation** | — | Rotation of a linearly polarised wave's plane as it crosses the magnetised ionosphere; large at VHF, a reason satellites use CP. | Ω ∝ TEC/f² | [09/ionospheric-hf-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/ionospheric-hf-propagation.md) |
| **fast time** | — | Radar term for time within one pulse repetition interval (samples along range); slow time is pulse-to-pulse time (samples along Doppler). The KB pages use the idea but rarely the words (gap D1 #32). | range FFT along fast time, Doppler FFT along slow time | [11/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes](kb/11-radar/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes.md) |
| **FastHenry** | — | Free 3-D inductance extractor for conductor geometries (partial and loop inductance). | — | [16/impedance-and-rf-calculators](kb/16-simulation-tools/impedance-and-rf-calculators.md) |
| **FBW** | fractional bandwidth | Bandwidth divided by centre frequency. Narrow FBW from lumped parts costs insertion loss because of finite component Q. | FBW = (f₂ − f₁)/f0 | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **FC** | flight computer | The rocket's avionics board (Space Bar primary and secondary FCs) carrying sensors, GNSS, LoRa radio and pyro channels. | — | [15/rocket-telemetry-radio-design](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/rocket-telemetry-radio-design.md) |
| **FCC** | Federal Communications Commission | US spectrum regulator; Part 15 covers unlicensed devices, Part 97 amateur radio, Part 18 ISM equipment. | — | [19/certification-fcc-ce-imda](kb/19-regulations-safety/certification-fcc-ce-imda.md) |
| **FCC ID** | — | The grant identifier a certified US radio product carries; a certified module can keep its grant inside a host if the integration conditions are met. | — | [19/certification-fcc-ce-imda](kb/19-regulations-safety/certification-fcc-ce-imda.md) |
| **FD** | finite difference | Approximating derivatives by differences on a grid; the basis of FDTD and of simple 2-D field solvers. | f′ ≈ (f(x+h) − f(x−h))/2h | [00/numerical-methods-overview](kb/00-math-foundations/numerical-methods-overview.md) |
| **FDMA** | frequency-division multiple access | Users are given separate frequency channels. | — | [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md) |
| **FDTD** | finite-difference time-domain | EM simulation that steps Maxwell's equations in time on a grid (Yee cells); one run gives a wideband result. | cell ≤ λ/15–λ/20; Courant limit on time step | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **FEC** | forward error correction | Adding redundant bits so the receiver can fix errors without retransmission. | K = 7 r½ soft Viterbi: ≈ 5.4 dB coding gain at BER 10⁻⁵ | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **feedback** | — | Feeding part of the output back to the input; negative feedback trades gain for accuracy, bandwidth and linearity, but can oscillate if the loop has too much phase shift. | closed-loop gain A/(1 + Aβ) | [05/feedback-and-stability](kb/05-analog-electronics/feedback-and-stability.md) |
| **FEI** | frequency error indication | The LoRa/FSK chip's measured carrier frequency error for a received packet; logs Doppler and crystal drift, and drives AFC. | — | [15/lora-impairments-doppler-drift-timing](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-impairments-doppler-drift-timing.md) |
| **FEM** | finite element method | EM (or mechanical) method that solves fields on a tetrahedral mesh in the frequency domain; handles complex geometry and materials (HFSS). | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **ferrite bead** | — | A lossy ferrite inductor that turns unwanted RF current into heat while passing DC; its impedance is mostly resistive at its rated frequency. | impedance quoted at 100 MHz; drops with DC current | [04/ferrites-and-beads](kb/04-real-components-hf/ferrites-and-beads.md) |
| **ferrite rod antenna** | — | A coil on a ferrite rod; a compact LF/MF receive antenna. | — | [08/loop-antennas](kb/08-antennas/loop-antennas.md) |
| **FET** | field-effect transistor | Voltage-controlled transistor (gate voltage controls channel current): MOSFET, JFET, MESFET, HEMT. | — | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **FFT** | fast Fourier transform | Any fast algorithm for the DFT; cost grows as N log N instead of N². The workhorse of SDR spectra, FMCW range processing and LoRa demodulation. | N = 1024: ≈ 10⁴ instead of 10⁶ operations | [14/fft-spectral-analysis-windows](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fft-spectral-analysis-windows.md) |
| **FHSS** | frequency-hopping spread spectrum | Hopping the carrier among many channels in a pseudo-random pattern; dodges narrowband interference and satisfies some band rules (FCC §15.247). | — | [10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md) |
| **fibre-weave effect** | — | Variation of local εr as a trace runs over glass bundles or resin gaps; causes skew in fast differential pairs. | — | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **fibreglass** | glass-fibre composite | RF-transparent airframe material (G10/FR-4-like); the usual radome or nose cone around rocket antennas. | εr ≈ 4–5 | [08/antennas-on-rockets](kb/08-antennas/antennas-on-rockets.md) |
| **field strength** | — | Magnitude of the electric field (V/m, or dBµV/m), the quantity EMC limits and some regulations specify instead of power. | E = √(30·EIRP)/d (far field) | [02/poynting-vector-power-flow](kb/02-electromagnetics/poynting-vector-power-flow.md) |
| **FIR** | finite impulse response filter | Digital filter whose output is a weighted sum of recent inputs only; always stable and can have exactly linear phase. | taps ≈ (A_dB/22)·(f_s/Δf) (Harris rule) | [14/digital-filters-fir-iir](kb/14-sdr-dsp/digital-filters-fir-iir.md) |
| **Fisher information** | — | A measure of how sharply the likelihood depends on a parameter; its inverse is the Cramér–Rao bound. | var(θ̂) ≥ 1/I(θ) | [00/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb](kb/00-math-foundations/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb.md) |
| **FIT** | finite integration technique | Integral-form cousin of FDTD used by CST Studio. | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **flash ADC** | — | ADC with one comparator per code level; fastest but power-hungry and low resolution. | — | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **flicker noise** | 1/f noise | Low-frequency noise whose PSD rises as 1/f below a corner frequency; it hurts zero-IF receivers and close-in oscillator phase noise. | corner f_c from Hz (BJTs) to MHz (CMOS) | [07/shot-and-flicker-noise](kb/07-noise-linearity/shot-and-flicker-noise.md) |
| **FLL** | frequency-locked loop | A loop that tracks frequency rather than phase; more robust to dynamics than a PLL, used for GNSS pull-in and under high jerk. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking.md) |
| **FM** | frequency modulation | Information carried in the carrier's instantaneous frequency; constant envelope, trades bandwidth for SNR above threshold. | Carson's bandwidth ≈ 2(Δf + f_m) | [10/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb](kb/10-comms-modulation/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb.md) |
| **FMCW** | frequency-modulated continuous wave | CW radar whose frequency is swept in chirps; mixing the echo with the transmit chirp gives a beat frequency proportional to range. Used by automotive mm-wave radars and DIY builds. | f_b = 2RS/c (S = chirp slope B/T); range resolution c/(2B) | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **FMR** | ferromagnetic resonance | Precession resonance of magnetisation in a biased ferrite; the physics behind circulators and isolators. | ω0 = γμ0H0 | [02/magnetic-materials-permeability](kb/02-electromagnetics/magnetic-materials-permeability.md) |
| **FNBW** | first-null beamwidth | Angular width of the main beam between its first nulls. | — | [12/array-factor-linear-arrays](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-factor-linear-arrays.md) |
| **folded dipole** | — | A dipole folded back into a narrow loop; same pattern, about four times the feed resistance. | ≈ 290 Ω | [08/dipoles-and-monopoles](kb/08-antennas/dipoles-and-monopoles.md) |
| **FOM (PLL)** | normalised phase-noise floor (figure of merit) | A PLL chip's in-band phase-noise floor normalised to 1 Hz PFD and N = 1; lets you predict in-band noise for any setting. | ADF4351 −221 dBc/Hz; LMX2594 −236 dBc/Hz | [06/vco-and-pll-synthesizers](kb/06-rf-circuits/vco-and-pll-synthesizers.md) |
| **FOT** | frequency of optimum traffic | A working HF frequency about 85 % of the monthly median MUF. | FOT ≈ 0.85·MUF | [09/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction.md) |
| **Fourier transform** | — | Splits a signal into its sinusoidal frequency components; the link between time-domain waveforms and spectra (and between an antenna aperture and its far-field pattern). | X(f) = ∫x(t)e^{−j2πft}dt | [00/fourier-series-and-transform](kb/00-math-foundations/fourier-series-and-transform.md) |
| **FPGA** | field-programmable gate array | Reconfigurable logic chip used for high-rate SDR processing (DDCs, FFTs, radar). | — | [14/fpga-for-sdr](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fpga-for-sdr.md) |
| **FR-4** | flame-retardant woven glass–epoxy laminate | The standard cheap PCB material; usable to a few GHz, with εr and loss that vary by glass style and supplier. | εr 4.3–4.7 at 1 GHz, tan δ 0.015–0.022; KB examples 4.4 (JLC 7628), 4.5 (JLC 2-layer core) | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **fractional-N** | — | PLL whose divider ratio alternates to average a non-integer N, allowing fine steps with a high PFD frequency (at the cost of fractional spurs). | — | [06/vco-and-pll-synthesizers](kb/06-rf-circuits/vco-and-pll-synthesizers.md) |
| **frequency multiplier** | — | A circuit that produces an integer multiple of its input frequency (by a non-linearity plus filtering); multiplying by N raises phase noise by 20 log N. | ×N adds 20 log₁₀N dB of phase noise | [06/frequency-multipliers-dividers](kb/06-rf-circuits/frequency-multipliers-dividers.md) |
| **frequency plan** | — | The choice of LO, IF and sample frequencies so that images, harmonics and spurs fall outside the wanted band. | — | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **Fresnel coefficients** | — | Reflection and transmission coefficients of a plane wave at a flat boundary, different for TE (s) and TM (p) polarization. | — | [02/boundary-conditions-reflection-refraction](kb/02-electromagnetics/boundary-conditions-reflection-refraction.md) |
| **Fresnel zone** | — | Ellipsoidal regions around the line of sight where path-length differences are multiples of λ/2; keeping the first zone ~60 % clear avoids extra loss. | R₁ = 17.32√(d₁d₂/((d₁+d₂)f_GHz)) m (d in km) | [09/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon.md) |
| **Friis formula (noise)** | Friis cascade formula | Total noise factor of a chain of stages: each later stage's excess noise is divided by the gain before it, so the first stage dominates. | F = F₁ + (F₂−1)/G₁ + (F₃−1)/(G₁G₂) + … | [07/noise-figure-and-friis](kb/07-noise-linearity/noise-figure-and-friis.md) |
| **Friis transmission equation** | — | Received power between two antennas in free space from transmit power, both gains and the wavelength. | P_r = P_tG_tG_r(λ/4πd)² | [09/friis-free-space-path-loss](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/friis-free-space-path-loss.md) |
| **front-to-back ratio** | — | Ratio of an antenna's gain in its forward direction to its gain directly behind. | — | [08/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic](kb/08-antennas/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic.md) |
| **FSK** | frequency-shift keying | Digital modulation by switching between frequencies; constant envelope, so it suits efficient non-linear PAs. | 2-FSK non-coherent BER = ½e^{−Eb/2N0} | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **FSPL** | free-space path loss | The spreading loss between two isotropic antennas in empty space. It grows with frequency only because a fixed-gain antenna's aperture shrinks. | FSPL = 32.45 + 20 log f_MHz + 20 log d_km dB (433 MHz, 10 km: 105.2 dB) | [09/friis-free-space-path-loss](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/friis-free-space-path-loss.md) |
| **fT** | transition frequency | Frequency at which a transistor's current gain falls to 1; a rough speed figure. | BFP740 f_T = 44 GHz | [05/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal](kb/05-analog-electronics/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal.md) |
| **FT8** | — | Weak-signal amateur digital mode (8-FSK, 15 s slots) widely used on HF; WSPR is a related beacon mode for propagation studies. | decodes to ≈ −20 dB SNR in 2.5 kHz | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |
| **FTW** | frequency tuning word | The number loaded into an NCO or DDS phase accumulator that sets its output frequency. | f_out = FTW·f_clk/2^N (32-bit at 125 MHz: 29 mHz steps) | [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md) |
| **full scale** | — | The largest input (or output) amplitude a data converter can handle without clipping; reference for dBFS. | P_FS e.g. +4 dBm into the ADC input | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **full-wave** | — | Analysis that keeps all of Maxwell's equations, including radiation and propagation delay; needed once a structure is a sizeable fraction of a wavelength. | — | [02/quasi-static-vs-full-wave](kb/02-electromagnetics/quasi-static-vs-full-wave.md) |

## G

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **g-sensitivity** | acceleration sensitivity | Fractional frequency shift of a crystal per g of acceleration or vibration; on a rocket it modulates the carrier and can break GNSS or LoRa lock. | ≈ 2 ppb/g typical (Semtech AN1200.14): 15 g boost ≈ 27 Hz at 915 MHz | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **G/T** | gain-to-noise-temperature ratio | Figure of merit of a receiving station: antenna gain divided by system noise temperature, in dB/K. | C/N0 = EIRP − L + G/T + 228.6 dB | [09/link-budget-methodology](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-budget-methodology.md) |
| **G5RV** | — | A popular multiband HF dipole fed through a specific length of ladder line; needs a tuner on most bands. | — | [08/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole](kb/08-antennas/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole.md) |
| **GaAs** | gallium arsenide | III–V semiconductor with fast electrons; used for pHEMT LNAs, switches and HBT PAs. | — | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **gain** | antenna gain | Directivity multiplied by radiation efficiency: the concentration you actually get after losses. "Realized gain" also subtracts mismatch loss. | G = η_rad·D | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **gain block** | — | A broadband, internally matched 50 Ω MMIC amplifier (often a Darlington with feedback) that needs only bias and DC blocks. | NF ≈ 3–5 dB | [18/lna-pa-gain-block-mmics](kb/18-components-parts/lna-pa-gain-block-mmics.md) |
| **gain margin** | — | How far below 0 dB a loop's gain is where its phase reaches −180°. | GM = −20 log\|T(jω₁₈₀)\| | [05/feedback-and-stability](kb/05-analog-electronics/feedback-and-stability.md) |
| **gain staging** | — | Setting each gain stage of an SDR or receiver so weak signals rise above the ADC noise without strong ones clipping. | — | [14/sdr-gain-staging-and-reception-recipes](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-gain-staging-and-reception-recipes.md) |
| **gain-comparison method** | gain-transfer method | Measuring an antenna's gain by swapping it with a reference antenna of known gain. | — | [08/antenna-measurement](kb/08-antennas/antenna-measurement.md) |
| **Galileo** | — | The European GNSS, with E1 (1575.42 MHz, CBOC) and E5a/E5b signals and the free High Accuracy Service. | — | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **GaN** | gallium nitride | Wide-bandgap semiconductor for high-power, high-voltage RF transistors (HEMTs); needs negative gate bias and correct bias sequencing. | 28–50 V drain | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **Gaussian noise** | — | Noise whose amplitude follows the normal (bell-curve) distribution; thermal noise is Gaussian. | — | [00/probability-random-processes-and-psd](kb/00-math-foundations/probability-random-processes-and-psd.md) |
| **GBW** | gain–bandwidth product | For a voltage-feedback op-amp, open-loop gain × frequency is roughly constant above its dominant pole; it sets closed-loop bandwidth. | f_−3dB ≈ GBW/G_noise | [05/op-amps-real-limitations](kb/05-analog-electronics/op-amps-real-limitations.md) |
| **GFSK** | Gaussian frequency-shift keying | FSK whose data is first smoothed by a Gaussian filter, narrowing the spectrum; used by Bluetooth, many ISM radios and LoRa modules' FSK mode. | BT = 0.5 (Bluetooth) | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **Gilbert cell** | — | Active double-balanced mixer made of cross-coupled differential pairs; gives conversion gain and is used in most ICs. | — | [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md) |
| **GLONASS** | — | Russia's GNSS; its legacy signals use a different carrier per satellite (FDMA), so receivers need inter-frequency bias corrections for RTK. | L1 ≈ 1602 MHz + k·0.5625 MHz | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **GMSK** | Gaussian minimum-shift keying | MSK with Gaussian pre-filtering; used by GSM and AIS. | GSM BT = 0.3 | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **GND** | ground | The circuit's 0 V reference net; at RF, a continuous plane rather than a single node. | — | [13/grounding-and-return-paths](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/grounding-and-return-paths.md) |
| **GNSS** | global navigation satellite system | Generic name for satellite positioning systems (GPS, GLONASS, Galileo, BeiDou, plus regional QZSS and NavIC). | signals at L-band 1.16–1.61 GHz, ≈ −128.5 dBm at the ground | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **GNSS-SDR** | — | Open-source software GNSS receiver that processes raw IQ samples (e.g. from a MAX2769 front end or an SDR). | — | [21/sdr-gnss-and-diy-receivers](kb/21-gnss/sdr-gnss-and-diy-receivers.md) |
| **GNU Radio** | — | Open-source framework of signal-processing blocks connected into flowgraphs; the standard SDR software toolkit. | — | [14/gnu-radio-and-software-stack](kb/14-sdr-dsp/gnu-radio-and-software-stack.md) |
| **Gold code** | — | A family of PRN sequences with low cross-correlation, built from two m-sequences; used for GPS C/A. | — | [10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md) |
| **GPIO** | general-purpose input/output | An MCU pin under software control (e.g. an LoRa module's DIO/IRQ or a PA enable). | — | [13/mixed-signal-layout](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/mixed-signal-layout.md) |
| **GPS** | Global Positioning System | The US GNSS. L1 C/A at 1575.42 MHz is the signal every receiver uses first. | L1 C/A: BPSK, 1023 chips at 1.023 Mcps, 50 b/s; minimum −128.5 dBm | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **GPS time** | — | Continuous time scale used by GPS (no leap seconds); it differs from UTC by the leap-second count (18 s since 2017). | GPS − UTC = 18 s | [21/gnss-timing-1pps](kb/21-gnss/gnss-timing-1pps.md) |
| **GPSDO** | GPS-disciplined oscillator | An OCXO or TCXO steered by a GNSS receiver's 1PPS to give a long-term accurate 10 MHz reference. | ~10⁻¹¹–10⁻¹² long-term | [21/gnss-timing-1pps](kb/21-gnss/gnss-timing-1pps.md) |
| **grating lobe** | — | An extra full-strength main beam that appears when elements are spaced too far apart for the scan angle. | none if d/λ < 1/(1 + \|sin θ₀\|); d = λ/2 safe to ±90° | [12/beam-steering-grating-lobes](kb/12-phased-arrays/beam-steering-grating-lobes.md) |
| **Gray coding** | — | Labelling constellation points so neighbours differ by one bit, so most symbol errors cost one bit. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **ground bounce** | — | Voltage spikes on a chip's ground from fast current changes through package and via inductance. | V = L di/dt | [13/grounding-and-return-paths](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/grounding-and-return-paths.md) |
| **ground plane** | — | The conducting surface (PCB copper, radials, vehicle body) that completes a monopole or patch; a small ground plane detunes and degrades the antenna. Also the reference copper layer of a PCB. | — | [08/dipoles-and-monopoles](kb/08-antennas/dipoles-and-monopoles.md) |
| **ground plane (PCB)** | reference plane | A continuous copper layer that every RF and fast trace uses as its return path. | — | [13/grounding-and-return-paths](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/grounding-and-return-paths.md) |
| **ground station** | — | The receiving (and commanding) end of the rocket link: antenna (often a tracking Yagi or helix), LNA, filter, receiver and logging. | — | [15/ground-station-design](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ground-station-design.md) |
| **ground wave** | surface wave | A vertically polarised wave guided along the earth's surface; dominant at LF/MF, tiny at VHF and above. | — | [09/ground-wave-and-lf-mf-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/ground-wave-and-lf-mf-propagation.md) |
| **group delay** | — | How long each frequency's envelope takes to pass through a network. Non-flat group delay smears pulses and chirps. | τ_g = −dφ/dω | [01/resonance-and-q](kb/01-circuit-theory/resonance-and-q.md) |
| **GSM** | Global System for Mobile communications (2G) | Cellular standard using GMSK with BT 0.3 and TDMA. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **Guanella balun** | current balun | Transmission-line balun that forces equal and opposite currents; the preferred antenna balun. | — | [06/baluns-and-rf-transformers](kb/06-rf-circuits/baluns-and-rf-transformers.md) |
| **guided wavelength** | — | Wavelength of a signal on a transmission line or in a waveguide. | λ_g = λ0/√ε_eff | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **GUM** | Guide to the Expression of Uncertainty in Measurement | The standard method for combining measurement uncertainties. | — | [17/rf-power-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/rf-power-measurement.md) |

## H

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **h-parameters** | hybrid parameters | Mixed two-port description used for low-frequency BJT models (h_fe = current gain). | — | [01/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd](kb/01-circuit-theory/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd.md) |
| **HackRF** | HackRF One | Half-duplex 1 MHz–6 GHz SDR transceiver with 8-bit samples up to 20 MS/s. | — | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **hairpin filter** | — | Microstrip band-pass filter of folded λ/2 coupled resonators. | — | [06/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip.md) |
| **half-band filter** | — | FIR filter with its cut-off at a quarter of the sample rate, so nearly half its taps are zero; efficient for ÷2 decimation stages. | — | [14/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic](kb/14-sdr-dsp/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic.md) |
| **Hammerstad–Jensen** | — | Accurate closed-form microstrip impedance and effective-permittivity formulas used by most calculators. | — | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **Hamming code** | — | Simple block code that corrects one bit error per block; LoRa's 4/7 and 4/8 rates use Hamming-type codes. | Hamming(7,4) | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **Hamming distance** | — | Number of bit positions in which two codewords differ; a CRC's "HD" says how many bit errors it is guaranteed to detect up to a given length. | — | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **harmonic balance** | — | Frequency-domain non-linear circuit simulation that solves directly for the steady-state harmonics; efficient for mixers, PAs and oscillators. | — | [16/rf-circuit-simulators-harmonic-balance](kb/16-simulation-tools/rf-circuit-simulators-harmonic-balance.md) |
| **harmonic distortion** | — | Output energy at integer multiples of the input frequency; HD2 and HD3 name the second and third harmonics. | THD = √(ΣP_harmonics)/P_fund | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **HASL** | hot-air solder levelling | Cheap tin-alloy PCB finish; uneven surface, acceptable for most sub-GHz RF. | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **HB100** | — | Cheap 10.525 GHz CW Doppler radar module (motion sensor) used in hobby speed radars. | — | [11/diy-radar-hardware](kb/11-radar/diy-radar-hardware.md) |
| **HBT** | heterojunction bipolar transistor | Bipolar transistor with a different semiconductor in emitter and base for higher speed; used in GaAs/InGaP PAs and SiGe LNAs. | — | [05/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal](kb/05-analog-electronics/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal.md) |
| **HDL** | hardware description language | Verilog or VHDL code that describes FPGA logic. | — | [14/fpga-for-sdr](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fpga-for-sdr.md) |
| **helical antenna** | helix | A wire wound as a helix; with circumference ≈ λ (axial mode) it radiates a CP beam along its axis. | realistic ≈ 13 dBic for 10 turns (Kraus formula is 3–5 dB high) | [08/helical-antennas](kb/08-antennas/helical-antennas.md) |
| **Heltec** | — | Maker of ESP32 + LoRa development boards (WiFi LoRa 32, Wireless Stick), common in Meshtastic. | — | [15/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec.md) |
| **HEMT** | high-electron-mobility transistor | Heterojunction FET with a very fast, low-noise channel; the standard LNA and microwave device. | — | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **HERO** | hazards of electromagnetic radiation to ordnance | The risk that RF fields induce enough current in an electro-explosive device (pyro charge, e-match) to fire it. | MIL-STD-464: EID ≥ 16.5 dB below MNFS | [19/lab-safety-esd-high-power](kb/19-regulations-safety/lab-safety-esd-high-power.md) |
| **Hertzian dipole** | — | An infinitesimally short current element; the building block from which all antenna radiation can be summed. | D = 1.5 (1.76 dBi); R_rad = 789(l/λ)² Ω | [02/radiation-from-currents-hertzian-dipole](kb/02-electromagnetics/radiation-from-currents-hertzian-dipole.md) |
| **HIL** | hardware-in-the-loop | Testing flight hardware and software by feeding it simulated sensor and radio inputs on the bench. | — | [15/rocket-telemetry-radio-design](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/rocket-telemetry-radio-design.md) |
| **Hilbert transform** | — | A 90° phase shift applied to every frequency; used to build the analytic signal (one-sided spectrum) and in image-reject mixers. | — | [14/iq-complex-baseband](kb/14-sdr-dsp/iq-complex-baseband.md) |
| **horn antenna** | — | A flared waveguide; predictable gain, used as a reference and as a dish feed. | — | [08/horn-reflector-aperture-antennas](kb/08-antennas/horn-reflector-aperture-antennas.md) |
| **HPBW** | half-power beamwidth | Angular width of the main beam between the points where power has fallen to half (−3 dB). | uniform line array ≈ 0.886 λ/(N d cos θ₀) rad | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **HPF** | high-pass filter | Filter that passes frequencies above its cut-off. | — | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **HPG** | u-blox High Precision GNSS firmware | ZED-F9P firmware line (RTK); SPG is the standard-precision line on MAX-M10S. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox.md) |
| **hybrid coupler** | — | Coupler that splits power equally with 90° (branch-line, Lange) or 180° (rat-race) between outputs. | — | [06/couplers-dividers-combiners](kb/06-rf-circuits/couplers-dividers-combiners.md) |

## I

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **IACS** | International Annealed Copper Standard | Reference conductivity of annealed copper; the KB's design value for copper loss. | σ = 5.80×10⁷ S/m (1.7241 µΩ·cm) | [02/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness](kb/02-electromagnetics/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness.md) |
| **IBIS** | I/O Buffer Information Specification | Behavioural model format for digital I/O drivers, used in signal-integrity simulation. | — | [16/spice-circuit-simulation](kb/16-simulation-tools/spice-circuit-simulation.md) |
| **IC** | integrated circuit | A complete circuit on one silicon (or GaAs) die. | — | [18/rf-transceiver-ics](kb/18-components-parts/rf-transceiver-ics.md) |
| **ICNIRP** | International Commission on Non-Ionizing Radiation Protection | Body whose guidelines (2020 edition) set RF exposure limits that many countries, including Singapore's NEA, follow. | general public: f/200 W/m² for 400–2000 MHz (2.17 W/m² at 433 MHz); occupational f/40 | [19/rf-exposure-safety](kb/19-regulations-safety/rf-exposure-safety.md) |
| **IEC** | International Electrotechnical Commission | International standards body (e.g. IEC 61000 EMC and ESD tests, IEC 60027 units). | — | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **IEEE radar bands** | — | Letter names for microwave bands: L 1–2, S 2–4, C 4–8, X 8–12, Ku 12–18, K 18–27, Ka 27–40, V 40–75, W 75–110 GHz. | GPS L1 is L-band; 10.5 GHz HB100 is X-band | [11/radar-range-equation](kb/11-radar/radar-range-equation.md) |
| **IF** | intermediate frequency | The fixed frequency a superheterodyne receiver converts the RF to for filtering and gain. | — | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **IFA** | inverted-F antenna | A shorted monopole bent parallel to the board edge, with a tap feed for matching; a common printed PCB antenna. | — | [08/pcb-and-chip-antennas](kb/08-antennas/pcb-and-chip-antennas.md) |
| **IFBW** | IF bandwidth | The VNA receiver's filter bandwidth; narrower IFBW lowers the noise floor (raises dynamic range) but slows the sweep. | 10× narrower → 10 dB lower noise | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **IFFT** | inverse fast Fourier transform | The FFT run backwards: builds a time signal from frequency bins. An OFDM transmitter is essentially an IFFT. | — | [10/ofdm](kb/10-comms-modulation/ofdm.md) |
| **IGRF** | International Geomagnetic Reference Field | Standard model of the earth's magnetic field; it gives the dip angle and declination used in ionospheric and magnetometer work. | — | [09/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction.md) |
| **IIP2** | input-referred second-order intercept point | The input level at which second-order products would equal the fundamental; critical for direct-conversion receivers. | SFDR₂ = ½(IIP2 − N) | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **IIP3** | input-referred third-order intercept point | IP3 expressed as the input power per tone; used for receiver stages and cascades. | 1/IIP3_total = Σ G_before/IIP3_i (linear) | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **IIR** | infinite impulse response filter | Digital filter with feedback; fewer coefficients than FIR for a sharp response, but non-linear phase and possible instability. | — | [14/digital-filters-fir-iir](kb/14-sdr-dsp/digital-filters-fir-iir.md) |
| **IL** | insertion loss | Loss caused by inserting a component (filter, cable, switch) into a matched path. | IL = −20 log₁₀\|S21\| | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **IM2** | second-order intermodulation product | Products at f₁ ± f₂; in zero-IF receivers the difference term falls at DC. They rise 2 dB per dB. | — | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **IM3** | third-order intermodulation product | The products at 2f₁ − f₂ and 2f₂ − f₁, which land next to the wanted signals and cannot be filtered; they rise 3 dB per 1 dB of input. | P_IM3 = 3P_in − 2·IIP3 (per-tone, dBm) | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **image frequency** | — | The unwanted frequency on the other side of the LO that mixes to the same IF as the wanted signal; it must be filtered or cancelled. | f_image = f_RF ± 2f_IF | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **IMD** | intermodulation distortion | New frequencies created when two or more signals pass through a non-linear stage, at sums and differences of their multiples. | — | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **IMDA** | Infocomm Media Development Authority | Singapore's spectrum and telecom regulator; its TS SRD specification sets the licence-exempt short-range-device limits. | 433.05–434.79 MHz: 10 mW e.r.p., ≤ 10 % duty (26a) | [19/singapore-imda-rules](kb/19-regulations-safety/singapore-imda-rules.md) |
| **IMM** | interacting multiple model | Tracking filter that runs several motion models (e.g. coast and boost) in parallel and mixes them by their likelihoods. | — | [00/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation](kb/00-math-foundations/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation.md) |
| **impedance** | — | The complex ratio of voltage to current at one frequency: resistance (real part) plus reactance (imaginary part), in ohms. | Z = R + jX; Z_L = jωL, Z_C = 1/(jωC) | [01/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance](kb/01-circuit-theory/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance.md) |
| **impedance matching** | — | Adding lossless elements so a load looks like the source impedance (or its conjugate), to deliver maximum power or set a noise match. | — | [03/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed.md) |
| **impulse response** | — | Output of a linear system when the input is a very short spike; it fully characterises an LTI system. | h(t) ⇔ H(f) | [00/linear-systems-convolution](kb/00-math-foundations/linear-systems-convolution.md) |
| **IMU** | inertial measurement unit | Accelerometers and gyroscopes (often with magnetometer); fused with GNSS and baro in a rocket's navigation filter. | — | [00/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation](kb/00-math-foundations/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation.md) |
| **inductive degeneration** | — | A small inductor in the source/emitter of an LNA transistor that creates a real input resistance without adding noise, so noise and gain matches can coincide. | R_in ≈ g_m L_s/C_gs = ω_T L_s | [06/lna-design](kb/06-rf-circuits/lna-design.md) |
| **inductor Q** | — | Ratio of an inductor's reactance to its loss resistance at a given frequency; air-core and wire-wound chips reach 40–100, multilayer chips less. | Q = ωL/R | [04/inductors-at-rf](kb/04-real-components-hf/inductors-at-rf.md) |
| **InGaP** | indium gallium phosphide | Emitter material for GaAs HBTs used in gain-block MMICs and handset PAs. | — | [05/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal](kb/05-analog-electronics/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal.md) |
| **integer ambiguity** | — | The unknown whole number of carrier cycles in a carrier-phase measurement; RTK "float" estimates it as a real number, "fixed" resolves it to an integer. | float dm-level, fixed cm-level | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **integration gain** | — | SNR improvement from adding N pulses: N (coherent) or somewhat less (non-coherent). | coherent 10 log N | [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md) |
| **interleaving** | — | Reordering coded bits so a burst of errors is spread across many codewords, where FEC can fix them. | — | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **interpolation** | — | Raising the sample rate by inserting samples and filtering out images. | — | [14/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic](kb/14-sdr-dsp/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic.md) |
| **ionosphere** | — | Layers of the upper atmosphere (D, E, F1, F2, ≈ 60–400 km) ionised by the sun; they refract HF back to earth and add delay and Faraday rotation at VHF and above. | — | [09/ionospheric-hf-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/ionospheric-hf-propagation.md) |
| **ionospheric delay** | — | Extra GNSS signal delay from free electrons; proportional to TEC/f², so dual-frequency receivers can remove it. Klobuchar is GPS's broadcast single-frequency model. | 1 TECU ≈ 0.162 m at L1 | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **IP3** | third-order intercept point | The extrapolated power at which IM3 products would equal the fundamentals; a figure of merit for linearity. IIP3 at the input, OIP3 at the output. | OIP3 = IIP3 + G | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **IQ** | in-phase and quadrature | The pair of baseband signals (cosine and sine components) that together represent a band-pass signal as a complex number, so positive and negative frequencies can be told apart. | x(t) = I(t) + jQ(t); s(t) = Re{x(t)e^{j2πf_ct}} | [14/iq-complex-baseband](kb/14-sdr-dsp/iq-complex-baseband.md) |
| **IQ imbalance** | — | Gain and phase mismatch between the I and Q paths; creates an image of each signal mirrored about the centre frequency. | IRR ≈ 40 dB for 1° and 0.1 dB | [14/iq-complex-baseband](kb/14-sdr-dsp/iq-complex-baseband.md) |
| **IREC** | Intercollegiate Rocket Engineering Competition (Spaceport America Cup) | US student rocketry competition run by ESRA; its DTEG and Student Band Plan set the telemetry rules. | SRAD 33 cm 902.0–909.0 MHz (Mode A ≤ 1 W, 500 kHz–1 MHz BW); 70 cm SRAD 420.6–438.0 MHz ≤ 200 mW, ham licence | [19/international-rules-for-competitions](kb/19-regulations-safety/international-rules-for-competitions.md) |
| **IRR** | image-rejection ratio | How much an image-reject mixer or receiver suppresses the image relative to the wanted signal; limited by I/Q gain and phase matching. | 1° and 0.1 dB imbalance ≈ 40 dB | [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md) |
| **ISAR** | inverse synthetic-aperture radar | Imaging a rotating or manoeuvring target with a fixed radar; the target's motion supplies the aperture. | — | [11/sar-and-imaging](kb/11-radar/sar-and-imaging.md) |
| **ISF** | impulse sensitivity function | Hajimiri–Lee description of how much a noise impulse shifts an oscillator's phase depending on when in the cycle it arrives; explains noise up-conversion. | — | [06/oscillator-phase-noise-ltv-isf](kb/06-rf-circuits/oscillator-phase-noise-ltv-isf.md) |
| **ISI** | intersymbol interference | Smearing of one symbol into the next, from band-limiting filters or multipath. | — | [10/pulse-shaping-isi](kb/10-comms-modulation/pulse-shaping-isi.md) |
| **ISM band** | industrial, scientific and medical band | Frequency bands where unlicensed low-power devices are allowed under general rules (433.05–434.79 MHz, 902–928 MHz in Region 2, 2.4 GHz, 5.8 GHz). Rules differ by country. | — | [19/spectrum-allocation-ism-bands](kb/19-regulations-safety/spectrum-allocation-ism-bands.md) |
| **isolation** | — | How much signal leaks between ports that should be disconnected (switch off-state, coupler isolated port, TX-to-RX). | — | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **isolator** | — | A circulator with one port terminated: power passes forward but reflections are absorbed, protecting a PA or oscillator from load changes. | — | [06/circulators-isolators](kb/06-rf-circuits/circulators-isolators.md) |
| **isotropic radiator** | — | An imaginary lossless antenna that radiates equally in every direction; the 0 dBi reference for gain. | — | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **ITAR** | International Traffic in Arms Regulations | US export rules for defence articles (USML); some high-dynamic GNSS receivers and rocket items fall under them or under EAR/MTCR controls. | — | [21/gnss-on-rockets-dynamics-and-limits](kb/21-gnss/gnss-on-rockets-dynamics-and-limits.md) |
| **ITM** | Irregular Terrain Model (Longley–Rice) | Terrain-aware path-loss model for 20 MHz–20 GHz using a terrain profile; used by many coverage tools. | — | [09/empirical-path-loss-models](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/empirical-path-loss-models.md) |
| **ITU** | International Telecommunication Union | UN agency that coordinates spectrum worldwide; its Radio Regulations divide the world into three ITU regions with different allocations (Singapore is in Region 3). | — | [19/spectrum-allocation-ism-bands](kb/19-regulations-safety/spectrum-allocation-ism-bands.md) |
| **ITU band designations** | — | Decade names for radio frequencies: VLF 3–30 kHz, LF 30–300 kHz, MF 300 kHz–3 MHz, HF 3–30 MHz, VHF 30–300 MHz, UHF 300 MHz–3 GHz, SHF 3–30 GHz, EHF 30–300 GHz. Broadcast "LW/MW/SW" are long, medium and short wave (≈ LF, MF, HF). | 433 and 915 MHz are UHF; 24/77 GHz are SHF/EHF | [00/rf-concepts-for-beginners](kb/00-math-foundations/rf-concepts-for-beginners.md) |
| **ITU-R** | International Telecommunication Union – Radiocommunication Sector | The UN body that publishes the Radio Regulations and the propagation recommendations (P-series) the KB uses: P.525 free space, P.526 diffraction, P.676 gases, P.838 rain. | — | [09/atmospheric-rain-attenuation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/atmospheric-rain-attenuation.md) |

## J

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **j** | imaginary unit | The square root of −1, written j in engineering so that i can stay "current". | j² = −1; e^{jθ} = cos θ + j sin θ | [00/complex-numbers-and-phasors](kb/00-math-foundations/complex-numbers-and-phasors.md) |
| **J-inverter** | admittance inverter | An idealised quarter-wave-like coupling element used to design coupled-resonator band-pass filters (K-inverter = impedance inverter). | — | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **J-pole** | — | A half-wave radiator end-fed through a quarter-wave matching stub; a simple ground-plane-free vertical. | — | [08/dipoles-and-monopoles](kb/08-antennas/dipoles-and-monopoles.md) |
| **jamming** | — | Interference, deliberate or not, that overwhelms the weak GNSS signals. | J/S in dB | [21/gnss-interference-jamming-spoofing](kb/21-gnss/gnss-interference-jamming-spoofing.md) |
| **JEDEC** | — | Semiconductor standards body (package outlines, MSL and ESD test standards). | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **JESD204B** | — | High-speed serial interface standard between fast data converters and FPGAs. | — | [18/adcs-dacs-for-sdr](kb/18-components-parts/adcs-dacs-for-sdr.md) |
| **JFET** | junction field-effect transistor | FET whose gate is a reverse-biased junction; very low current noise, good for high-impedance low-noise front ends. | — | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **jitter** | — | Random variation in the timing of a clock or data edge; integrated phase noise expressed in time. | σ_t = √(2∫𝓛 df)/(2πf0) | [07/phase-noise-and-jitter](kb/07-noise-linearity/phase-noise-and-jitter.md) |
| **JLCPCB** | — | Chinese PCB fabrication and assembly service used by Space Bar; its stackup templates and impedance calculator define the trace widths to order. | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **JPDA** | joint probabilistic data association | Multi-target version of PDA that shares ambiguous measurements among nearby tracks. | — | [11/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers](kb/11-radar/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers.md) |

## K

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **k-factor (earth)** | effective earth-radius factor | Factor by which the earth's radius is enlarged to account for atmospheric bending; 4/3 for a standard atmosphere. (Not the Rollett or Rician K.) | k = 4/3 | [09/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon.md) |
| **K-factor (Rollett)** | Rollett stability factor | A number from a transistor's S-parameters that tells, with \|Δ\| < 1, whether it is unconditionally stable. (Not the Rician K-factor or the 4/3-earth k-factor.) | K = (1 − \|S11\|² − \|S22\|² + \|Δ\|²)/(2\|S12S21\|) | [06/amplifier-stability-and-biasing](kb/06-rf-circuits/amplifier-stability-and-biasing.md) |
| **K-index** | — | A 0–9 measure of geomagnetic disturbance over 3 hours; the planetary Kp ≥ 5 marks a storm that degrades HF and GNSS. | — | [09/solar-activity-and-space-weather](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/solar-activity-and-space-weather.md) |
| **Kalman filter** | — | Recursive estimator that blends a motion model with noisy measurements, weighting each by its uncertainty; the standard tracker for radar and navigation. | predict → update; gain K = PHᵀ(HPHᵀ+R)⁻¹ | [00/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation](kb/00-math-foundations/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation.md) |
| **KCL** | Kirchhoff's current law | The currents into any node sum to zero (charge is conserved). Valid only while the circuit is electrically small. | ΣI = 0 | [01/network-theorems-max-power-transfer](kb/01-circuit-theory/network-theorems-max-power-transfer.md) |
| **KiCad** | — | Free open-source schematic and PCB design suite used by Space Bar (includes ngspice). | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **Kirschning–Jansen** | — | Accurate model of microstrip dispersion (how ε_eff rises with frequency). | — | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **knife-edge diffraction** | — | Loss caused by a single sharp obstacle near the path, found from the Fresnel–Kirchhoff parameter ν. | grazing (ν = 0) ≈ 6 dB | [09/diffraction-knife-edge](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/diffraction-knife-edge.md) |
| **KrakenSDR** | — | Five coherent RTL-SDR-class receivers with a shared clock and calibration noise source, for direction finding and passive radar below 1.77 GHz. | — | [12/diy-phased-array-projects](kb/12-phased-arrays/diy-phased-array-projects.md) |
| **Kramers–Kronig relations** | — | Causality forces the real and imaginary parts of a response (e.g. εr′ and εr″) to be linked: a material with loss must also show dispersion. | — | [00/linear-systems-convolution](kb/00-math-foundations/linear-systems-convolution.md) |
| **kT/C noise** | — | Thermal noise frozen onto a capacitor when a switch opens; independent of the resistance. | v_rms = √(kT/C): 1 pF → 64 µV | [05/low-noise-analog-design](kb/05-analog-electronics/low-noise-analog-design.md) |
| **kTB** | Boltzmann constant × temperature × bandwidth | The available thermal noise power in a bandwidth B; the floor every receiver starts from. | −174 dBm/Hz + 10 log₁₀B: 125 kHz → −123 dBm | [07/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature](kb/07-noise-linearity/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature.md) |
| **KVL** | Kirchhoff's voltage law | The voltages around any closed loop sum to zero; strictly true only when no changing magnetic flux threads the loop (quasi-static). | ΣV = 0 | [02/quasi-static-vs-full-wave](kb/02-electromagnetics/quasi-static-vs-full-wave.md) |

## L

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **L-network** | — | The simplest matching network: one series and one shunt reactance. Its Q is fixed by the resistance ratio. | Q = √(R_high/R_low − 1) | [03/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed.md) |
| **L1** | GPS L1 band | The 1575.42 MHz GNSS band (GPS L1, Galileo E1, BeiDou B1C, QZSS). L2 = 1227.60 MHz, L5 = 1176.45 MHz. | λ ≈ 19.0 cm | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **Laplace transform** | — | Transform that turns linear differential equations into algebra in the complex frequency s; gives transfer functions, poles and zeros. | F(s) = ∫₀^∞ f(t)e^{−st}dt; s = σ + jω | [00/laplace-transfer-functions-bode](kb/00-math-foundations/laplace-transfer-functions-bode.md) |
| **LBT** | listen before talk | Regulatory form of carrier sensing that some bands require (e.g. instead of duty-cycle limits in parts of EU 868 MHz). | — | [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **LC** | inductor–capacitor | A circuit (tank, filter, matching network) built from inductors and capacitors. | f0 = 1/(2π√(LC)) | [01/resonance-and-q](kb/01-circuit-theory/resonance-and-q.md) |
| **LCMV** | linearly constrained minimum variance | Generalisation of MVDR with several linear constraints (e.g. fixed nulls or derivative constraints). | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **LCR meter** | — | Bench instrument that measures inductance, capacitance and resistance (with Q/D) at chosen test frequencies. | — | [17/lab-setup-by-budget](kb/17-test-measurement/lab-setup-by-budget.md) |
| **LCSC** | — | Electronic-component distributor linked to JLCPCB assembly; its part numbers (C-numbers) and basic/extended part categories decide assembly cost. | — | [18/sourcing-and-eval-boards](kb/18-components-parts/sourcing-and-eval-boards.md) |
| **LDMOS** | laterally diffused MOS | Silicon power MOSFET for VHF–3 GHz PAs; rugged and cheap. | — | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **LDO** | low-dropout regulator | Linear regulator that works with a small input-to-output voltage difference; quiet, so used to feed oscillators, LNAs and ADCs. | — | [05/power-supplies-for-rf-and-analog](kb/05-analog-electronics/power-supplies-for-rf-and-analog.md) |
| **LDPC** | low-density parity-check code | Modern block code decoded iteratively; operates within a fraction of a dB of the Shannon limit for long blocks (5G, DVB-S2, CCSDS). | — | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **LDRO** | low-data-rate optimisation | LoRa mode that drops two bits per symbol (SF − 2) to tolerate frequency drift when symbols are long; mandatory when the symbol time is ≥ 16.38 ms (SF11/12 at 125 kHz). | drift limit BW/(3·2^SF) per packet (×16 with LDRO) | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **least squares** | — | Choosing model parameters to minimise the sum of squared residuals; used for calibration fits, GNSS position solutions and FIR design. | x̂ = (HᵀH)⁻¹Hᵀy | [00/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb](kb/00-math-foundations/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb.md) |
| **Leeson's model** | — | Empirical formula for oscillator phase noise: a 1/f² region inside the resonator half-bandwidth, a 1/f³ region below the flicker corner, and a flat floor. | 𝓛 = 10 log[(FkT0/2P_s)(1 + (f0/2Q_L f_m)²)(1 + f_c/f_m)] | [07/phase-noise-and-jitter](kb/07-noise-linearity/phase-noise-and-jitter.md) |
| **LEO** | low earth orbit | Orbits a few hundred to ~2000 km up (ISS, CubeSats, weather satellites); GEO is the 35 786 km geostationary orbit used by SBAS. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **LFM** | linear frequency modulation (chirp) | A pulse whose frequency sweeps linearly; the classic compressible waveform. | compressed −3 dB width ≈ 0.88/B; sidelobes −13.4 dB unweighted | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **LHCP** | left-hand circular polarization | CP rotating the other way; a single reflection (ground, dish) turns RHCP into LHCP. | — | [08/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization](kb/08-antennas/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization.md) |
| **LimeSDR** | — | Full-duplex SDR built on the LMS7002M transceiver (≈ 100 kHz–3.8 GHz). | — | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **limiter** | — | A PIN or Schottky diode circuit that passes small signals and clamps large ones, protecting an LNA or SDR input. | — | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **LINC** | linear amplification with non-linear components | Splits a varying-envelope signal into two constant-envelope signals, amplifies each efficiently, and recombines them. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **link budget** | — | A line-by-line tally of gains and losses from transmitter to receiver, ending in received power and margin over sensitivity. | P_r = EIRP − L_path − L_misc + G_r | [09/link-budget-methodology](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-budget-methodology.md) |
| **link margin** | — | How many dB the received power exceeds the receiver's sensitivity; the allowance for fading, pointing and polarization losses. | margin = P_r − S | [09/link-margin-availability](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-margin-availability.md) |
| **LLCC68** | — | Low-cost SX126x-class LoRa transceiver limited to a subset of SF/BW combinations; used in EBYTE E220 modules. | — | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **LMR-400** | — | Thick (10 mm) foam-dielectric low-loss 50 Ω coax for ground-station feed lines (LMR-240 and LMR-195 are thinner versions). | 433 MHz: LMR-400 0.087 dB/m; LMR-240 0.17 dB/m | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **LMS** | least mean squares | Simple adaptive-filter algorithm that nudges weights along the error gradient each sample (adaptive equalisers, noise cancellers, beamformers). RLS converges faster at more cost. | w ← w + µ e x* | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **LNA** | low-noise amplifier | The first amplifier after the antenna (and pre-select filter), designed for minimum noise figure; its gain hides the noise of everything after it. | GNSS LNA e.g. BGA524N6: 19.6 dB, NF 0.55 dB | [06/lna-design](kb/06-rf-circuits/lna-design.md) |
| **LO** | local oscillator | The oscillator (or synthesiser) whose signal a mixer multiplies with the RF to convert frequency. Its phase noise and leakage matter. | — | [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md) |
| **load capacitance** | — | The capacitance a crystal must see to oscillate at its marked frequency; wrong load caps pull the frequency by tens of ppm. | C_x = 2(C_L − C_stray) for two equal caps | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **load line** | — | The optimum PA load resistance set by the supply voltage swing and output power (not by conjugate matching). | R_opt ≈ (V_DD − V_knee)²/(2P_out) | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **load-pull** | — | Measuring or simulating a transistor's output power and efficiency over many load impedances to find the best one. | — | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **loaded Q** | — | Q of a resonator including the source and load resistances; it sets the actual bandwidth. A resonator's insertion loss depends on the ratio of loaded to unloaded Q. | IL = −20 log(1 − Q_L/Q_u) | [01/resonance-and-q](kb/01-circuit-theory/resonance-and-q.md) |
| **log detector** | logarithmic amplifier | Detector whose output voltage is proportional to input power in dBm over a wide (≈ 60–100 dB) range (e.g. AD8307, AD8318). | slope ≈ 25 mV/dB (AD8307) | [06/detectors-and-power-meters](kb/06-rf-circuits/detectors-and-power-meters.md) |
| **loop antenna** | — | A wire loop; small loops (≪ λ) respond to the magnetic field, have tiny radiation resistance and are used for receiving and direction finding. | small loop R_rad = 31 200 (A/λ²)² Ω | [08/loop-antennas](kb/08-antennas/loop-antennas.md) |
| **loop filter** | — | The low-pass network between charge pump and VCO that sets a PLL's bandwidth, phase margin and spur suppression. | — | [06/vco-and-pll-synthesizers](kb/06-rf-circuits/vco-and-pll-synthesizers.md) |
| **LoRa** | Long Range (Semtech's chirp modulation) | Semtech's proprietary chirp-spread-spectrum physical layer; trades data rate for sensitivity, receiving below the noise floor. The KB's telemetry link. | R_b = SF·(BW/2^SF)·4/(4+CR) | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **LoRaWAN** | LoRa wide-area network | The network protocol (MAC, gateways, servers, security) built on LoRa for IoT; usually not what a rocket link needs. | — | [15/lorawan-vs-raw-lora](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lorawan-vs-raw-lora.md) |
| **LOS** | line of sight | An unobstructed straight path between antennas (in practice, one with the first Fresnel zone mostly clear). | — | [09/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon.md) |
| **loss tangent** | — | Ratio of a dielectric's loss to its stored energy per cycle. Dielectric loss in a line grows in proportion to frequency × tan δ. | tan δ = εr″/εr′ = 1/Q_d | [02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md) |
| **low-IF** | — | Receiver that mixes to a small non-zero IF (hundreds of kHz to a few MHz) to dodge DC problems while keeping integration easy; SX126x and RTL-SDR's R820T use it. | R820T2 IF ≈ 3.57 MHz | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **low-pass prototype** | — | A normalised (1 Ω, 1 rad/s) low-pass ladder whose element values gₖ are tabulated; scaling and transformation turn it into any LPF, HPF, BPF or BSF. | — | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **LPDA** | log-periodic dipole array | A frequency-independent directional antenna of dipoles whose lengths and spacings scale by a constant ratio; nearly constant gain over a wide band. | — | [08/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic](kb/08-antennas/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic.md) |
| **LPF** | low-pass filter | Filter that passes frequencies below its cut-off and attenuates those above; on a transmitter it removes harmonics. | — | [01/passive-filter-design-prototypes](kb/01-circuit-theory/passive-filter-design-prototypes.md) |
| **LPI** | liquid photo-imageable (solder mask) | The usual PCB solder-mask chemistry. | εr ≈ 3.3–4.0, tan δ ≈ 0.02–0.03 | [02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md) |
| **LPWAN** | low-power wide-area network | Class of long-range, low-rate IoT networks (LoRaWAN, Sigfox, NB-IoT). | SG 917–925 MHz LPWAN end devices: 100 mW e.r.p., ≤ 1 % duty | [15/lorawan-vs-raw-lora](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lorawan-vs-raw-lora.md) |
| **LR-FHSS** | long-range frequency-hopping spread spectrum | Semtech uplink modulation that hops narrow fragments with heavy coding, for very high network capacity. | — | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **LR11xx** | Semtech LR1110/LR1120/LR1121 | Multi-band LoRa transceivers (sub-GHz and 2.4 GHz) with GNSS/Wi-Fi scanning for geolocation. | — | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **LSB** | least significant bit | The smallest step of a data converter's output (also "lower sideband" in SSB). | ideal ADC LSB = V_FS/2^N | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **LTCC** | low-temperature co-fired ceramic | Multilayer ceramic technology for tiny integrated filters, baluns and diplexers. | — | [18/filter-parts-saw-ceramic-ltcc](kb/18-components-parts/filter-parts-saw-ceramic-ltcc.md) |
| **LTE** | Long-Term Evolution (4G) | Cellular standard using OFDMA downlink and SC-FDMA uplink. | — | [10/ofdm](kb/10-comms-modulation/ofdm.md) |
| **LTI** | linear time-invariant | A system that obeys superposition and behaves the same at all times; fully described by its impulse response or transfer function. Filters, lines and antennas are LTI; mixers and amplifiers in compression are not. | y = h * x | [00/linear-systems-convolution](kb/00-math-foundations/linear-systems-convolution.md) |
| **LUF** | lowest usable frequency | The lowest frequency that still gives an adequate SNR on an HF path, set by D-layer absorption and noise. | — | [09/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction.md) |
| **LUT** | look-up table | Memory table replacing a calculation (sine table in an NCO, per-frequency calibration table in an array). | — | [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md) |
| **LVDS** | low-voltage differential signalling | Differential digital signalling (≈ 350 mV swing) used for converter and clock interfaces. | — | [18/adcs-dacs-for-sdr](kb/18-components-parts/adcs-dacs-for-sdr.md) |

## M

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **MAC** | medium access control | Protocol layer that decides who transmits when (ALOHA, CSMA, TDMA). | — | [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md) |
| **MAG** | maximum available gain | The highest gain a stable transistor can give with simultaneous conjugate matching at both ports. | MAG = \|S21/S12\|(K − √(K²−1)), K > 1 | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **MAP** | maximum a posteriori | Estimation or detection that also weighs prior knowledge (prior probabilities) with the likelihood. Equals ML when all outcomes are equally likely a priori. | — | [00/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb](kb/00-math-foundations/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb.md) |
| **MAPL** | maximum allowable path loss | The path loss at which a link's margin reaches zero; EIRP plus receive gain minus sensitivity. | MAPL = EIRP + G_r − S | [09/link-budget-methodology](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/link-budget-methodology.md) |
| **matched filter** | — | The receiving filter that maximises output SNR for a known pulse in white noise: its impulse response is the time-reversed conjugate pulse. | peak SNR = 2E/N0 (real passband) = E/N0 (complex envelope) | [10/matched-filter-detection](kb/10-comms-modulation/matched-filter-detection.md) |
| **maximum power transfer** | — | A source delivers the most power to a load equal to the complex conjugate of its own impedance; only half the source's power then reaches the load. | Z_L = Z_s* ; P_max = \|V_s\|²/(8R_s) (peak V) | [01/network-theorems-max-power-transfer](kb/01-circuit-theory/network-theorems-max-power-transfer.md) |
| **Maxwell's equations** | — | The four laws of electromagnetism: charges make electric fields, there are no magnetic charges, a changing magnetic field induces an electric field, and currents plus changing electric fields make magnetic fields. Everything in the KB follows from them. | ∇×E = −jωB; ∇×H = J + jωD (phasor form) | [02/maxwells-equations](kb/02-electromagnetics/maxwells-equations.md) |
| **MCU** | microcontroller unit | A small processor with memory and peripherals on one chip (ESP32-S3, STM32, Teensy) that controls the radio and logs data. | — | [13/mixed-signal-layout](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/mixed-signal-layout.md) |
| **MDS** | minimum discernible (detectable) signal | Input level equal to the receiver noise floor (SNR = 0 dB in a stated bandwidth); a hardware figure, not a demodulation threshold. | MDS = −174 + NF + 10 log₁₀B | [07/sensitivity-and-mds](kb/07-noise-linearity/sensitivity-and-mds.md) |
| **MEEP** | — | MIT's open-source FDTD package with Python bindings. | — | [16/python-rf-toolkit](kb/16-simulation-tools/python-rf-toolkit.md) |
| **MEMS** | micro-electromechanical systems | Microscopic mechanical structures on silicon: accelerometers, gyros, and MEMS oscillators (an alternative to quartz). | — | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **mesh convergence** | — | Refining a simulation mesh until results stop changing; the basic check that an EM result is trustworthy. | — | [16/simulation-validation](kb/16-simulation-tools/simulation-validation.md) |
