| **Meshtastic** | — | Open-source LoRa mesh messaging firmware that floods packets through nodes; busy meshes congest because airtime is shared. | 50 B on LongFast (SF11/250 kHz) ≈ 641 ms | [15/lora-mesh-capacity-and-congestion](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-mesh-capacity-and-congestion.md) |
| **meteor scatter** | — | Brief VHF reflections from ionised meteor trails, lasting from milliseconds to seconds. | — | [09/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation.md) |
| **micro-coax** | — | Very thin 50 Ω coax (1.13 mm, 0.81 mm) used in U.FL pigtails; very lossy, so keep it short. | 1.13 mm: 1.35 dB/m at 433 MHz, 2.65 dB/m at 1575 MHz | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **microstrip** | — | A trace on top of a PCB over a ground plane: the most common PCB transmission line (quasi-TEM). | 50 Ω on JLC04161H-7628 (0.2104 mm prepreg): 0.359 mm masked / 0.377 mm bare | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **MIL-STD-464** | — | US military standard for system-level electromagnetic environmental effects, including RF safety margins for ordnance. | — | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **Miller effect** | — | A capacitance between input and inverting output appears multiplied by (1 + gain) at the input; it kills bandwidth of common-emitter stages. Cascodes avoid it. | C_in = C_μ(1 + \|A_v\|) | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **MIMO radar** | multiple-input multiple-output radar | Radar with several transmitters and receivers whose waveforms are separable, creating a virtual array of N_tx × N_rx elements for angle resolution. | virtual elements = N_tx·N_rx | [11/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays](kb/11-radar/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays.md) |
| **Mini-Circuits** | — | Maker of catalogue RF components (mixers, amplifiers, filters, attenuators) whose datasheets define much RF vocabulary (e.g. "Level 7" mixers). | — | [18/sourcing-and-eval-boards](kb/18-components-parts/sourcing-and-eval-boards.md) |
| **MININEC** | — | A small MoM wire-antenna code (basis of MMANA-GAL); less accurate with ground and tapered wires than NEC. | — | [16/nec-antenna-modelling](kb/16-simulation-tools/nec-antenna-modelling.md) |
| **mismatch loss** | — | The fraction of available power not delivered because part of it is reflected. | ML = −10 log₁₀(1 − \|Γ\|²); \|Γ\| = 0.1 → 0.04 dB | [03/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch.md) |
| **mismatch uncertainty** | — | Measurement error caused by the interaction of source and load reflections; can be several tenths of a dB. | limits 20 log(1 ± \|Γ_s\|\|Γ_L\|) | [17/rf-power-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/rf-power-measurement.md) |
| **mixed-mode S-parameters** | — | S-parameters of a differential pair split into differential, common-mode and conversion terms (Sdd21, Scd21 …). | — | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **mixer** | — | A circuit that multiplies an RF signal by a local-oscillator signal to shift it in frequency, producing sum and difference frequencies. | f_IF = \|f_RF − f_LO\| | [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md) |
| **ML** | maximum likelihood | Estimation or detection that picks the parameter value (or symbol) that makes the observed data most probable. Also "mismatch loss" and "multilayer" on some pages — see the ambiguous-acronyms section. | — | [00/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb](kb/00-math-foundations/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb.md) |
| **MLCC** | multilayer ceramic capacitor | The standard surface-mount capacitor: interleaved metal and ceramic layers. Dielectric class decides stability. | — | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **MMCX** | micro-miniature coaxial connector | Small snap-on RF connector that can rotate; used on some GNSS modules and SDRs. | — | [18/connectors-and-cables-parts](kb/18-components-parts/connectors-and-cables-parts.md) |
| **MMIC** | monolithic microwave integrated circuit | An RF/microwave circuit (amplifier, mixer, switch) built entirely on one die, typically GaAs, SiGe or GaN. | — | [18/lna-pa-gain-block-mmics](kb/18-components-parts/lna-pa-gain-block-mmics.md) |
| **MMSE** | minimum mean-square error | Estimator that minimises the average squared error; for Gaussian problems it is the conditional mean, and the Kalman filter is its recursive form. | — | [00/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb](kb/00-math-foundations/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb.md) |
| **mmWave** | millimetre wave | Frequencies of 30–300 GHz (wavelength 1–10 mm); in practice the 24–81 GHz radar and 5G bands. | 77 GHz λ ≈ 3.9 mm | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **mmWave radar SoC** | — | Single-chip 60/77 GHz FMCW radars with integrated TX/RX and DSP (TI IWR/AWR series, Infineon BGT60). | AWR1843: 76–81 GHz, 3 TX/4 RX | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **MNA** | modified nodal analysis | The matrix method SPICE uses to write circuit equations: node voltages plus currents in voltage sources and inductors. | — | [16/spice-circuit-simulation](kb/16-simulation-tools/spice-circuit-simulation.md) |
| **MNFS** | maximum no-fire stimulus | The largest current or power an electro-explosive device can take for 5 minutes with ≤ 0.1 % firing probability; RF safety margins are referred to it. | — | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **MnZn** | manganese–zinc ferrite | High-permeability, lower-resistivity ferrite for lower frequencies and large cable cores (Fair-Rite 31, 73). | mix 31: µᵢ 1500, 1–300/500 MHz | [04/ferrites-and-beads](kb/04-real-components-hf/ferrites-and-beads.md) |
| **modulation** | — | Varying a carrier's amplitude, frequency or phase (or several together) to carry information. | — | [10/modulation-choice-guide](kb/10-comms-modulation/modulation-choice-guide.md) |
| **modulation index** | — | Depth of modulation: for AM the fraction of envelope change, for FM/FSK the peak deviation relative to the modulating frequency (h = 2Δf/R for FSK). | FSK h = 2Δf/R_s; MSK h = 0.5 | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **MoM** | method of moments | EM method that solves for currents on conductors (wires, surfaces) with integral equations; efficient for antennas in open space (NEC). | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **monopole** | quarter-wave monopole | Half a dipole standing on a ground plane that supplies the image of the missing half. | 36.5 + j21.3 Ω, 5.16 dBi on an infinite ground | [08/dipoles-and-monopoles](kb/08-antennas/dipoles-and-monopoles.md) |
| **monopulse** | — | Angle measurement from a single pulse by comparing sum and difference beams of a split antenna; the standard precision tracking method. | — | [11/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers](kb/11-radar/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers.md) |
| **monostatic radar** | — | Radar with transmitter and receiver at the same place (often the same antenna). Bistatic radar separates them; passive radar uses someone else's transmitter. | — | [11/radar-range-equation](kb/11-radar/radar-range-equation.md) |
| **Monte Carlo** | — | Estimating a result by running many random trials (e.g. random noise, random tolerances) and averaging. Many KB numbers are "MC-verified". | error falls as 1/√N trials | [00/numerical-methods-overview](kb/00-math-foundations/numerical-methods-overview.md) |
| **MOSFET** | metal–oxide–semiconductor field-effect transistor | FET with an insulated gate; the basis of CMOS and LDMOS. | saturation: I_D = ½µC_ox(W/L)V_ov² | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **MPE** | maximum permissible exposure | The FCC's RF exposure limits in power density. | general population f/1500 mW/cm² (300–1500 MHz): 2.89 W/m² at 433 MHz | [19/rf-exposure-safety](kb/19-regulations-safety/rf-exposure-safety.md) |
| **MQS** | magneto-quasi-static | Slow-field regime dominated by current and inductance; displacement current negligible. | — | [02/quasi-static-vs-full-wave](kb/02-electromagnetics/quasi-static-vs-full-wave.md) |
| **MS/s** | mega-samples per second | Sample rate of an ADC, DAC or SDR stream. For complex IQ samples, the usable bandwidth equals the sample rate; for real samples it is half. | RTL-SDR stable ≈ 2.4 MS/s | [14/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling.md) |
| **MSE** | mean-square error | Average squared estimation error, equal to variance plus squared bias. | MSE = var + bias² | [00/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb](kb/00-math-foundations/estimation-theory-ml-map-crb.md) |
| **MSG** | maximum stable gain | The gain limit quoted when a device is only conditionally stable (K < 1). | MSG = \|S21/S12\| | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **MSK** | minimum-shift keying | Continuous-phase FSK with h = 0.5, the smallest spacing for orthogonal tones; equivalent to offset-QPSK with sinusoidal shaping. | h = 0.5 | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **MSL (package)** | moisture sensitivity level | Rating of how long a plastic package may be exposed to air before it must be baked prior to reflow. (In openEMS, MSL port = microstrip-line port.) | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **MTI** | moving-target indication | Filtering (e.g. pulse-to-pulse cancellers) that removes stationary clutter so moving targets stand out. | two-pulse canceller blind speeds at v = nλ·PRF/2 | [11/clutter-and-mti](kb/11-radar/clutter-and-mti.md) |
| **MUF** | maximum usable frequency | The highest frequency the ionosphere will return on a given path at a given time. | MUF ≈ f_oF2·sec θ (flat-earth secant law) | [09/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction.md) |
| **multipath** | — | Several copies of a signal arriving by different paths (ground, buildings, rocket body); they add or cancel, causing fading and delay spread. | — | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **Mur boundary** | — | A simple first-order absorbing boundary condition in FDTD; cheaper but less absorbing than a PML. | keep ≥ λ/4 from the structure | [08/antenna-simulation-nec-openems](kb/08-antennas/antenna-simulation-nec-openems.md) |
| **MUSIC** | multiple signal classification | Subspace DOA method: finds directions whose steering vectors are orthogonal to the noise eigenvectors of the covariance matrix; super-resolves closely spaced sources. | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **mutual coupling** | — | Interaction between nearby array elements that changes each element's impedance and pattern with scan angle. | — | [12/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling](kb/12-phased-arrays/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling.md) |
| **mutual inductance** | — | Voltage induced in one circuit per rate of change of current in another. | v₂ = M di₁/dt | [02/inductance-capacitance-from-fields](kb/02-electromagnetics/inductance-capacitance-from-fields.md) |
| **MVDR** | minimum-variance distortionless response (Capon) | Adaptive beamformer that keeps unit gain toward the look direction and minimises everything else, placing nulls on interferers. | w = R⁻¹a/(aᴴR⁻¹a) | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |

## N

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **N-type** | N connector | Large threaded 50 Ω connector for outdoor and higher-power use. | — | [18/connectors-and-cables-parts](kb/18-components-parts/connectors-and-cables-parts.md) |
| **NanoVNA** | — | Low-cost hand-held two-port (one-path) VNA; with LiteVNA and LibreVNA, the KB's default bench instrument. | — | [17/nanovna-librevna-practical](kb/17-test-measurement/nanovna-librevna-practical.md) |
| **NavIC** | Navigation with Indian Constellation | India's regional navigation system (formerly IRNSS). | — | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **navigation message** | — | The slow data stream on a GNSS signal carrying time, clock corrections, ephemeris and almanac. | GPS LNAV 50 b/s; subframes 1–3 take 18 s of a 30 s frame | [21/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris](kb/21-gnss/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris.md) |
| **NCO** | numerically controlled oscillator | Digital oscillator made from a phase accumulator and a sine lookup (or CORDIC); used for digital mixing in DDCs and DUCs. | resolution f_clk/2^N | [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md) |
| **near field** | — | Region close to an antenna where stored (reactive) fields dominate and the pattern has not formed; within about λ/2π for small antennas. Also the "radiating near field" (Fresnel region) of large antennas. | reactive near field r < λ/2π (small antennas) | [02/near-field-far-field](kb/02-electromagnetics/near-field-far-field.md) |
| **near-field probe** | — | A small loop (H-field) or stub (E-field) probe on a spectrum analyser for finding which part of a board radiates. | — | [13/emc-emi-fundamentals](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/emc-emi-fundamentals.md) |
| **NEC** | Numerical Electromagnetics Code | Method-of-moments wire-antenna simulator (NEC-2 open, NEC-4 licensed); front ends include 4nec2, xnec2c, EZNEC and PyNEC. | segments ≤ λ/10 | [16/nec-antenna-modelling](kb/16-simulation-tools/nec-antenna-modelling.md) |
| **NEES** | normalised estimation error squared | Consistency test for a Kalman filter in simulation: the squared error weighted by the filter's own covariance should average the state dimension. (NIS is the innovation version.) | E[NEES] = n | [00/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation](kb/00-math-foundations/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation.md) |
| **neper** | natural-log unit of ratio | Logarithmic unit using natural logs of amplitude; attenuation constants α in transmission-line and wave equations come out in Np/m. | 1 Np = 8.686 dB | [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md) |
| **Newton–Raphson** | — | Iterative root-finder that follows the local slope; used by SPICE and harmonic-balance solvers to converge non-linear circuits. | x_{n+1} = x_n − f(x_n)/f′(x_n) | [00/numerical-methods-overview](kb/00-math-foundations/numerical-methods-overview.md) |
| **NF** | noise figure | How much a device degrades the signal-to-noise ratio, in dB, with a 290 K source. Its linear form is the noise factor F. (NF also abbreviates "near field" in NF2FF and in 02's source keys.) | NF = 10 log₁₀F; F = SNR_in/SNR_out; −174 + NF + 10 log B = noise floor | [07/noise-figure-and-friis](kb/07-noise-linearity/noise-figure-and-friis.md) |
| **NF2FF** | near-field to far-field transformation | Computing far-field patterns from fields on a closed box around a simulated (or measured) antenna. | — | [16/openems-workflow](kb/16-simulation-tools/openems-workflow.md) |
| **NF_min** | minimum noise figure | Lowest noise figure a transistor reaches when driven from its optimum source impedance; one of the four noise parameters (with Γ_opt and R_n). | ATF-54143 ≈ 0.5 dB at 2 GHz | [06/lna-design](kb/06-rf-circuits/lna-design.md) |
| **NFC** | near-field communication | 13.56 MHz inductively coupled short-range link (≈ cm) between loop antennas. | — | [08/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt](kb/08-antennas/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt.md) |
| **NiZn** | nickel–zinc ferrite | High-resistivity ferrite family that works to hundreds of MHz (Fair-Rite 43, 61). | mix 43: µᵢ 800, 20–300 MHz suppression | [04/ferrites-and-beads](kb/04-real-components-hf/ferrites-and-beads.md) |
| **NLFM** | non-linear frequency modulation | A chirp with a curved frequency sweep that shapes the spectrum to give low range sidelobes without weighting loss. | — | [11/ambiguity-function-waveforms](kb/11-radar/ambiguity-function-waveforms.md) |
| **NMEA** | NMEA 0183 | The text sentence format ($GPGGA, $GPRMC …) that GNSS receivers output by default. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox.md) |
| **noise factor** | — | The linear (not dB) ratio of input to output SNR with a 290 K source; Friis's cascade formula works with it. | F = 1 + T_e/T0 | [07/noise-figure-and-friis](kb/07-noise-linearity/noise-figure-and-friis.md) |
| **noise floor** | — | The receiver noise power in its bandwidth, referred to the input. | N = −174 + NF + 10 log₁₀B dBm | [07/sensitivity-and-mds](kb/07-noise-linearity/sensitivity-and-mds.md) |
| **noise gain** | — | The gain an op-amp applies to its own input noise and offset; for an inverting stage it exceeds the signal gain by 1. | G_n = 1 + R_F/R_G | [05/low-noise-analog-design](kb/05-analog-electronics/low-noise-analog-design.md) |
| **noise marker** | — | Spectrum-analyser marker that reads noise density in dBm/Hz, correcting for RBW shape and detector bias. | — | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **noise match** | — | Presenting a transistor with the source impedance Γ_opt that minimises its noise figure; usually not the same as the gain (conjugate) match. | NF = NF_min + 4R_n\|Γs − Γopt\|²/(Z0(1−\|Γs\|²)\|1+Γopt\|²) | [06/lna-design](kb/06-rf-circuits/lna-design.md) |
| **noise source** | — | A calibrated broadband noise generator (usually an avalanche diode) with a known ENR. | — | [17/noise-figure-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/noise-figure-measurement.md) |
| **noise temperature** | — | A device's added noise expressed as the temperature of a resistor that would give the same noise; convenient for very low-noise systems and antennas. | T_e = T0(F − 1): NF 1 dB ↔ 75 K, 3 dB ↔ 289 K | [07/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature](kb/07-noise-linearity/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature.md) |
| **non-ionising radiation** | — | Radio and microwave photons carry far too little energy to break chemical bonds; their only established hazard is heating (and nerve stimulation at low frequency). | 433 MHz photon ≈ 1.8 µeV vs ≈ 10 eV to ionise | [19/rf-exposure-safety](kb/19-regulations-safety/rf-exposure-safety.md) |
| **nonlinearity** | — | Output not proportional to input; produces harmonics, compression and intermodulation. Modelled by a power series a₁x + a₂x² + a₃x³. | — | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **nRF24L01** | — | Nordic 2.4 GHz GFSK transceiver used in hobby links. | — | [15/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4.md) |
| **NTF** | noise transfer function | How a sigma-delta modulator shapes quantisation noise out of the signal band. | — | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **NTIA** | National Telecommunications and Information Administration | US agency managing federal spectrum; publisher of the ITM (Longley–Rice) propagation model. | — | [09/empirical-path-loss-models](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/empirical-path-loss-models.md) |
| **NTRIP** | Networked Transport of RTCM via Internet Protocol | Protocol for streaming RTCM corrections over the internet from a caster. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **NVIS** | near-vertical-incidence skywave | HF propagation by sending signals nearly straight up so the ionosphere reflects them back over a 0–400 km area; uses low horizontal antennas. | — | [08/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole](kb/08-antennas/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole.md) |
| **Nyquist ISI criterion** | — | A pulse shape gives zero ISI if it crosses zero at every other symbol instant; raised-cosine pulses satisfy it. | — | [10/pulse-shaping-isi](kb/10-comms-modulation/pulse-shaping-isi.md) |
| **Nyquist rate** | — | Sampling must exceed twice the highest frequency (real samples) or the bandwidth (complex samples) to avoid aliasing. | real: f_s > 2B; complex: f_s > B | [14/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling.md) |
| **Nyquist zone** | — | Each fs/2-wide band of input frequencies that fold onto the same output band of a sampler. | zone n spans (n−1)fs/2 … nfs/2 | [14/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling.md) |

## O

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **OBW** | occupied bandwidth | The bandwidth containing a stated fraction (often 99 %) of a signal's power; regulators limit it per band. | — | [19/singapore-imda-rules](kb/19-regulations-safety/singapore-imda-rules.md) |
| **OCF dipole** | off-centre-fed dipole | A dipole fed about one-third of the way along, giving a usable impedance on several harmonically related HF bands. | — | [08/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole](kb/08-antennas/wire-antennas-hf-random-wire-fan-dipole.md) |
| **OCTC** | open-circuit time constants | Hand method to estimate an amplifier's −3 dB bandwidth by summing the RC time constant seen by each capacitor. | ω_h ≈ 1/Σ R_i0 C_i | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **OCXO** | oven-controlled crystal oscillator | A crystal kept in a small heated oven for very low drift; power-hungry, used in lab references. | ~10⁻⁸ incl. temperature and ageing; warm-up minutes, ~0.6 W | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **ODE** | ordinary differential equation | Equation linking a function of one variable to its derivatives; circuit transients are ODEs in time. | RC: τ dv/dt + v = v_in | [00/differential-equations-circuits-waves](kb/00-math-foundations/differential-equations-circuits-waves.md) |
| **OFDM** | orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing | Splits data across many closely spaced orthogonal subcarriers (built with an IFFT); robust to multipath with a cyclic prefix, but has high PAPR. | subcarrier spacing = 1/T_symbol | [10/ofdm](kb/10-comms-modulation/ofdm.md) |
| **OIP3** | output-referred third-order intercept point | IP3 expressed as output power per tone; datasheets of amplifiers and gain blocks usually quote it. | OIP3 = IIP3 + G | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **Okumura–Hata model** | Hata model | Empirical path-loss formulas fitted to urban and suburban measurements (150–1500 MHz; COST-231 extends to 2 GHz). | — | [09/empirical-path-loss-models](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/empirical-path-loss-models.md) |
| **op-amp** | operational amplifier | High-gain differential amplifier used with feedback; its real limits are gain–bandwidth, slew rate, noise, offset and output drive. | closed-loop BW ≈ GBW/noise gain | [05/op-amps-real-limitations](kb/05-analog-electronics/op-amps-real-limitations.md) |
| **openEMS** | — | Free FDTD (EC-FDTD) electromagnetic solver driven from Python or Octave through the CSXCAD geometry library; used in the KB for antennas and PCB structures. | — | [16/openems-workflow](kb/16-simulation-tools/openems-workflow.md) |
| **operating power gain** | — | Power delivered to the load divided by the power actually entering the amplifier input; depends only on the load termination. Textbooks write G_P; the KB writes G_power so it is not confused with the processing gain G_proc. | G_power = P_L/P_in ≥ G_T | [06/lna-design](kb/06-rf-circuits/lna-design.md), [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **OQPSK** | offset QPSK | QPSK with the Q bits delayed half a symbol so the phase never jumps 180°; smaller envelope dips through non-linear PAs. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **oscillator** | — | An amplifier with positive feedback through a frequency-selective network (LC tank, crystal, ring) that produces a steady sinusoid or clock. | — | [05/oscillators](kb/05-analog-electronics/oscillators.md) |
| **OSP** | organic solderability preservative | Thin organic coating that leaves bare copper; lowest RF loss, short shelf life. | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **OTA** | over-the-air | Testing a radio through its antenna in free space or a chamber, as opposed to a cabled (conducted) test. | — | [17/antenna-range-ota-testing](kb/17-test-measurement/antenna-range-ota-testing.md) |

## P

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **P1dB** | 1 dB compression point | Power level at which an amplifier's gain has dropped 1 dB below its small-signal value; quoted at input (IP1dB) or output (OP1dB). | for a pure cubic, IIP3 − IP1dB = 9.64 dB (rule of thumb only) | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **PA** | power amplifier | The final transmitter stage that raises the signal to antenna power; efficiency, linearity, harmonics and protection from bad loads dominate its design. | E22-400T33D: 33 dBm (2 W) | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **packet SNR** | — | The SNR the LoRa demodulator reports per packet (can be negative); with RSSI and frequency error it is the key logged telemetry diagnostic. | SnrPkt/4 dB on SX127x | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **PAE** | power-added efficiency | PA efficiency that subtracts the input drive power, so it fairly compares low-gain stages. | PAE = (P_out − P_in)/P_DC | [06/power-amplifiers](kb/06-rf-circuits/power-amplifiers.md) |
| **PAPR** | peak-to-average power ratio | How much a signal's peaks exceed its average power; high PAPR (OFDM, QAM, multi-carrier) forces PA back-off. | OFDM needs ≈ 10 dB back-off: a 2 W PA gives ≈ 0.2 W average | [10/ofdm](kb/10-comms-modulation/ofdm.md) |
| **Parks–McClellan** | Remez exchange FIR design | Algorithm for equiripple optimal FIR filters. | — | [14/digital-filters-fir-iir](kb/14-sdr-dsp/digital-filters-fir-iir.md) |
| **Part 15** | 47 CFR Part 15 | US rules for unlicensed transmitters, e.g. §15.247 (902–928 MHz FHSS or ≥ 500 kHz digital modulation (DTS), up to 1 W). A fixed-channel 125 kHz LoRa link does not qualify as DTS. | — | [19/certification-fcc-ce-imda](kb/19-regulations-safety/certification-fcc-ce-imda.md) |
| **partial inductance** | — | Inductance assigned to a segment of a current loop (e.g. one via) by convention; only the whole loop's inductance is physical, so every via or trace figure must say where its return path is. | — | [02/inductance-capacitance-from-fields](kb/02-electromagnetics/inductance-capacitance-from-fields.md) |
| **passive radar** | — | Radar that uses broadcast transmitters (FM, DVB-T) as illuminators and cross-correlates a reference channel with the echo channel. | coherent gain = BT | [11/diy-radar-hardware](kb/11-radar/diy-radar-hardware.md) |
| **patch antenna** | microstrip antenna | A resonant rectangle (or disc) of copper over a ground plane on a PCB; low profile, ≈ 6–9 dBi, narrow band. A truncated corner or two feeds makes it CP. | L ≈ λ0/(2√ε_eff) − 2ΔL | [08/patch-antennas](kb/08-antennas/patch-antennas.md) |
| **pattern multiplication** | — | For identical elements, the array pattern is the element pattern multiplied by the array factor. | E(θ) = E_el(θ)·AF(θ) | [12/array-factor-linear-arrays](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-factor-linear-arrays.md) |
| **PCB** | printed circuit board | Laminated copper and dielectric layers that carry components and form RF transmission lines; at RF the stackup is part of the circuit. | — | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **PCBA** | printed circuit board assembly | A PCB with components soldered (e.g. JLCPCB's assembly service). | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **Pd** | probability of detection | Chance that a target present in a cell is declared detected. | design point often P_D = 0.9 | [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md) |
| **PDA** | probabilistic data association | Tracking method that updates a track with a weighted mix of all measurements in its gate when clutter makes the true one uncertain. | — | [11/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers](kb/11-radar/radar-tracking-monopulse-and-trackers.md) |
| **PDE** | partial differential equation | Equation in several variables (space and time); the wave equation and Maxwell's equations are PDEs. | — | [00/differential-equations-circuits-waves](kb/00-math-foundations/differential-equations-circuits-waves.md) |
| **PDN** | power distribution network | Everything between the regulator and the IC pins (planes, vias, capacitors); its impedance versus frequency decides supply noise. | target impedance Z = ΔV/ΔI | [13/power-distribution-decoupling-for-rf](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/power-distribution-decoupling-for-rf.md) |
| **PEC** | perfect electric conductor | Idealised conductor with zero resistance, used as a boundary in EM solvers; tangential E is zero on it. | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **PEP** | peak envelope power | Average power over one RF cycle at the peak of the modulation envelope; the US amateur power limit is written in it. | — | [19/amateur-radio-licensing](kb/19-regulations-safety/amateur-radio-licensing.md) |
| **PER** | packet error rate | Fraction of packets lost or failing CRC; LoRa sensitivities are specified at 1 % PER for a stated packet length. | PER ≈ 1 − (1 − BER)^bits (uncoded, independent errors) | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **permeability** | — | How strongly a material supports magnetic flux; relative permeability μr compares it with vacuum. Ferrites have high, lossy, frequency-dependent μ. | μ = μ′ − jμ″ | [02/magnetic-materials-permeability](kb/02-electromagnetics/magnetic-materials-permeability.md) |
| **permittivity** | — | How strongly a material stores electric energy (polarises); relative permittivity εr compares it with vacuum. Its imaginary part is dielectric loss. | εr = εr′ − jεr″; FR-4 εr ≈ 4.3–4.7 at 1 GHz | [02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md) |
| **Pfa** | probability of false alarm | Chance that noise alone crosses the detection threshold in a cell. | typical 10⁻⁶ | [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md) |
| **PFD** | phase-frequency detector | The PLL block that compares reference and divided VCO edges and drives the charge pump. | in-band PN ≈ FOM + 10 log f_PFD + 20 log N | [06/vco-and-pll-synthesizers](kb/06-rf-circuits/vco-and-pll-synthesizers.md) |
| **PGA** | programmable-gain amplifier | Amplifier with digitally selected gain steps, often in front of an ADC. (PGA-103 is also a Mini-Circuits LNA part.) | — | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **phase margin** | — | How far a feedback loop's phase is from −180° where its loop gain crosses 0 dB; small margins mean ringing or oscillation. | PM = 180° + ∠T(jω_c); aim ≥ 45–60° | [05/feedback-and-stability](kb/05-analog-electronics/feedback-and-stability.md) |
| **phase noise** | — | Random fluctuation of an oscillator's phase, seen as skirts around the carrier in the spectrum; it causes reciprocal mixing, jitter and constellation spread. | 𝓛(f) = S_φ(f)/2, in dBc/Hz | [07/phase-noise-and-jitter](kb/07-noise-linearity/phase-noise-and-jitter.md) |
| **phase quantisation** | — | Error from using few-bit phase shifters; periodic errors create quantisation lobes. | worst lobe 20 log tan(π/2^{P+1}) ≈ −6.02P + 4.4 dB | [12/phase-shifters-ttd-components](kb/12-phased-arrays/phase-shifters-ttd-components.md) |
| **phase shifter** | — | A component (switched lines, vector modulator, loaded line) that adds a controllable phase shift; digital ones have 4–7 bits. | — | [12/phase-shifters-ttd-components](kb/12-phased-arrays/phase-shifters-ttd-components.md) |
| **phase-noise measurement** | — | Methods to measure 𝓛(f): direct on a spectrum analyser, with a phase detector against a reference, or by cross-correlating two channels to beat down the instrument's own noise. | direct SA: 𝓛 = P(offset) − P_carrier − 10 log RBW + corrections | [17/phase-noise-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/phase-noise-measurement.md) |
| **phased array** | — | An antenna made of many elements whose relative phases (or delays) are set electronically to steer and shape the beam without moving parts. | — | [12/array-factor-linear-arrays](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-factor-linear-arrays.md) |
| **phasor** | — | A complex number whose magnitude and angle stand for the amplitude and phase of a sinusoid at one frequency, so that derivatives become multiplication by jω. The KB uses peak phasors with the e^{jωt} convention. | v(t) = Re{V e^{jωt}}; P = ½Re{VI*} with peak phasors | [00/complex-numbers-and-phasors](kb/00-math-foundations/complex-numbers-and-phasors.md) |
| **pHEMT** | pseudomorphic HEMT | GaAs HEMT with a strained channel; the device in most discrete LNAs (e.g. ATF-54143) and many switches. | NF_min ≈ 0.3–0.5 dB at 1–2 GHz | [05/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan](kb/05-analog-electronics/fets-mosfet-jfet-gaas-gan.md) |
| **PHY** | physical layer | The bottom protocol layer: modulation, coding and framing of bits on the radio channel (as opposed to MAC and higher layers). | — | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **PI loop** | proportional–integral loop filter | The standard second-order loop filter in digital PLLs, timing recovery and clock disciplining. | — | [10/synchronization-carrier-timing](kb/10-comms-modulation/synchronization-carrier-timing.md) |
| **pi network** | — | Three-element matching network (shunt–series–shunt) shaped like π; lets the designer choose Q and adds harmonic filtering. | — | [03/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed.md) |
| **Pierce oscillator** | — | The crystal-oscillator topology inside nearly every MCU and radio chip: an inverting amplifier with the crystal and two load capacitors in a π. | — | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **PIFA** | planar inverted-F antenna | A shorted quarter-wave patch folded over a ground plane; compact, used in phones and modules. | — | [08/pcb-and-chip-antennas](kb/08-antennas/pcb-and-chip-antennas.md) |
| **pigtail** | — | A short flexible coax lead with a connector, linking a board connector (U.FL) to a panel connector or antenna. | — | [13/connectors-and-launches](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/connectors-and-launches.md) |
| **PIM** | passive intermodulation | Intermodulation generated in "linear" passive parts (corroded or ferromagnetic connectors, loose contacts, nickel plating) when several strong carriers are present. | low-PIM: white bronze or silver finishes, no nickel | [07/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod](kb/07-noise-linearity/nonlinearity-p1db-ip3-intermod.md) |
| **PIN diode** | — | Diode with an intrinsic layer that acts as a current-controlled RF resistor; used in switches, attenuators and limiters. | series switch IL = 20 log(1 + R_S/2Z0) | [05/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor](kb/05-analog-electronics/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor.md) |
| **pipeline ADC** | — | ADC built from cascaded low-resolution stages; the usual architecture for 10–16 bits at tens to hundreds of MS/s. | — | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **plane wave** | — | A wave whose fields are uniform over flat planes perpendicular to its direction; the far field of any antenna looks locally like one. | E/H = η; η0 = 376.73 Ω in free space | [02/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization](kb/02-electromagnetics/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization.md) |
| **PLF** | polarization loss factor | Fraction of power received when the wave's polarization differs from the antenna's (e.g. a rolling rocket's linear antenna against a linear ground antenna). | linear–linear: cos²ψ; linear–CP: 0.5 (−3 dB) | [09/polarization-mismatch-loss](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/polarization-mismatch-loss.md) |
| **PLL** | phase-locked loop | A feedback loop that locks a VCO to a multiple of a reference by comparing phases; the basis of frequency synthesisers, carrier recovery and GNSS tracking. | f_out = N·f_PFD | [06/vco-and-pll-synthesizers](kb/06-rf-circuits/vco-and-pll-synthesizers.md) |
| **plume attenuation** | — | Signal loss when a rocket's exhaust plume (ionised, particle-laden) lies in the radio path, usually looking back from the ground at a rising rocket. | — | [08/antennas-on-rockets](kb/08-antennas/antennas-on-rockets.md) |
| **PlutoSDR** | ADALM-PLUTO | Analog Devices' learning SDR built on the AD936x transceiver (325 MHz–3.8 GHz, hackable to 70 MHz–6 GHz), full-duplex 12-bit. | — | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **PM** | phase modulation | Information carried in the carrier's phase. (PM also means phase margin in feedback loops.) | — | [10/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb](kb/10-comms-modulation/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb.md) |
| **PMC** | perfect magnetic conductor | Idealised boundary with zero tangential H; does not exist physically but is useful as a symmetry plane. | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **PML** | perfectly matched layer | An artificial absorbing boundary that soaks up outgoing waves in FDTD/FEM so the simulation behaves as if in open space. | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **PO** | physical optics | High-frequency approximation that estimates scattering from the currents induced on the lit side of a large object; used for RCS and reflectors. (GO = geometrical optics, ray-based; GTD adds edge diffraction.) | — | [11/radar-cross-section](kb/11-radar/radar-cross-section.md) |
| **polarization** | — | The direction in which a wave's electric field points and how it moves over a cycle: linear, circular or elliptical. Mismatched polarizations lose signal. | — | [02/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization](kb/02-electromagnetics/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization.md) |
| **pole** | — | A value of s (or z) where a transfer function becomes infinite. Poles in the right half-plane (or outside the unit circle for z) mean an unstable system. | each pole adds −20 dB/decade and up to −90° | [00/laplace-transfer-functions-bode](kb/00-math-foundations/laplace-transfer-functions-bode.md) |
| **polyphase filter** | — | An FIR split into M sub-filters so that only the samples that survive decimation are computed; also the basis of channelisers. | — | [14/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic](kb/14-sdr-dsp/multirate-dsp-decimation-cic.md) |
| **port extension** | — | Moving the reference plane forward by subtracting an ideal line's delay (and optionally loss) after calibration. | — | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **power density** | — | Power per unit area crossing a surface, in W/m²; the quantity RF-exposure limits are written in. | S = EIRP/(4πd²) | [02/poynting-vector-power-flow](kb/02-electromagnetics/poynting-vector-power-flow.md) |
| **power meter** | — | Instrument with a thermal or diode sensor that measures absolute RF power accurately (typically ±0.1–0.3 dB). | — | [17/rf-power-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/rf-power-measurement.md) |
| **Poynting vector** | — | The power flow per unit area carried by an electromagnetic field, pointing along the direction of energy flow. | S = ½Re{E×H*} (peak phasors); far field S = E²_rms/η0 | [02/poynting-vector-power-flow](kb/02-electromagnetics/poynting-vector-power-flow.md) |
| **PPI** | plan position indicator | Radar display showing echoes as a map: range outward from the centre and azimuth around it (the classic rotating sweep). B-scope shows range versus azimuth on rectangular axes. | — | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **PPK** | post-processed kinematic | RTK done afterwards on logged raw data from rover and base; avoids a live correction link and can process forwards and backwards. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **ppm** | parts per million | Fractional error of a frequency (or a component value) in millionths. | 1 ppm at 433 MHz = 433 Hz; at 915 MHz = 915 Hz | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **PPP** | precise point positioning | cm–dm positioning with a single receiver using precise satellite orbits and clocks from a service; slow to converge (tens of minutes). PPP-RTK adds atmospheric corrections to speed it up. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **pre-compliance** | — | In-house EMC checks (near-field probes, spectrum analyser, LISN, cheap antenna) before paying for formal certification tests. | — | [19/certification-fcc-ce-imda](kb/19-regulations-safety/certification-fcc-ce-imda.md) |
| **pre-emphasis** | — | Boosting high audio frequencies before FM transmission (and cutting them after, de-emphasis) to improve SNR, because FM noise rises with frequency. | 50 or 75 µs broadcast | [10/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb](kb/10-comms-modulation/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb.md) |
| **preamble** | — | Known symbols at the start of a packet that let the receiver detect it and settle gain, frequency and timing. LoRa default: 8 symbols. | — | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **prepreg** | — | Partly cured resin-impregnated glass cloth that bonds PCB layers; its thickness and glass style (7628, 2116, 3313, 1080) set the RF dielectric between outer layer and first plane. | 7628 ≈ 0.2 mm per ply | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **PRF** | pulse repetition frequency | Number of radar pulses (or chirps) per second; a trade between unambiguous range (low PRF) and unambiguous Doppler (high PRF). | PRF = 1/PRI | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **PRI** | pulse repetition interval | Time between radar pulses. | PRI = 1/PRF | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **PRN** | pseudo-random noise (code) | A deterministic sequence that looks random and has sharp autocorrelation; GNSS satellites are identified by their PRN code numbers. | GPS C/A: 1023-chip Gold codes | [10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md) |
| **processing gain** | — | SNR improvement gained by processing: despreading (DSSS chips per bit, LoRa 2^SF), coherent integration (radar, FFT), or oversampling an ADC. The meaning differs between pages — see the ambiguous-acronyms section. | DSSS G_proc = R_c/R_b; LoRa 10 log 2^SF = 3.01·SF dB; ADC 10 log(f_s/2B) real, 10 log(f_s/B) complex | [10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md), [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) |
| **propagation constant** | — | Complex number describing how a wave's amplitude decays (α) and phase advances (β) per metre. | γ = α + jβ; β = 2π/λ | [02/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization](kb/02-electromagnetics/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization.md) |
| **PSD** | power spectral density | How a signal's or noise's power is spread over frequency, in W/Hz or dBm/Hz. Integrate it over a band to get power. | thermal noise PSD kT = −174 dBm/Hz at 290 K | [00/probability-random-processes-and-psd](kb/00-math-foundations/probability-random-processes-and-psd.md) |
| **pseudorange** | — | Measured transmit-to-receive time times c, including the receiver clock error ("pseudo" because of that clock bias). Four satellites solve x, y, z and clock. | — | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **PSK** | phase-shift keying | Digital modulation by switching the carrier phase among M values. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **PSRR** | power-supply rejection ratio | How well a circuit (op-amp, LDO, oscillator) keeps supply ripple out of its output. | LDO PSRR often 60–70 dB at 1 kHz, falling at MHz | [05/power-supplies-for-rf-and-analog](kb/05-analog-electronics/power-supplies-for-rf-and-analog.md) |
| **PSS** | periodic steady-state analysis | Simulator analysis (Spectre-style) that finds an oscillator's or mixer's periodic operating point, used for phase-noise (pnoise) simulation. | — | [06/oscillator-phase-noise-ltv-isf](kb/06-rf-circuits/oscillator-phase-noise-ltv-isf.md) |
| **PTFE** | polytetrafluoroethylene (Teflon) | Very low-loss dielectric used in microwave laminates and coax. | εr ≈ 2.1; coax VF ≈ 0.69–0.70 | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **pulse compression** | — | Transmitting a long coded or chirped pulse for energy and compressing it with a matched filter to the resolution of a short pulse. | compression ratio = TB | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **pulse-Doppler radar** | — | Radar that measures Doppler with an FFT over many pulses at each range bin, separating moving targets from clutter. | Doppler resolution = 1/T_CPI | [11/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes](kb/11-radar/pulse-doppler-data-cube-and-prf-regimes.md) |
| **PVT** | position, velocity, time | A GNSS receiver's navigation solution output (u-blox UBX-NAV-PVT). In IC design PVT also means process–voltage–temperature corners. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox.md) |

## Q

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Q** | quality factor | How sharply a resonator or reactive part stores energy compared with what it loses each cycle. High Q means narrow bandwidth and low loss. | Q = 2π·(energy stored)/(energy lost per cycle) = f0/BW₃dB; inductor Q = X/R | [01/resonance-and-q](kb/01-circuit-theory/resonance-and-q.md) |
| **Q-function** | Gaussian tail probability | Probability that a standard normal variable exceeds x; it gives BER and false-alarm probabilities. | BPSK BER = Q(√(2Eb/N0)); Q(4.75) ≈ 10⁻⁶ | [00/probability-random-processes-and-psd](kb/00-math-foundations/probability-random-processes-and-psd.md) |
| **QAM** | quadrature amplitude modulation | Data in both amplitude and phase on a grid of constellation points (16-QAM, 64-QAM); bandwidth-efficient, needs linear PAs and high SNR. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **QFH** | quadrifilar helix | Four helical arms fed in quadrature; gives a broad CP pattern covering most of the sky (satellite and GNSS reception). | — | [08/helical-antennas](kb/08-antennas/helical-antennas.md) |
| **QFN** | quad flat no-lead package | Small leadless package with pads on the edges and an exposed pad underneath; low lead inductance, common for RF ICs. | — | [04/package-parasitics-bondwires](kb/04-real-components-hf/package-parasitics-bondwires.md) |
| **QPSK** | quadrature phase-shift keying | Four phases, 2 bits per symbol; same BER versus Eb/N0 as BPSK in half the bandwidth. | — | [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md) |
| **quarter-wave transformer** | — | A λ/4 line of impedance √(Z0·R_L) that matches a real load to Z0 at one frequency. | Z_in = Z1²/Z_L | [03/input-impedance-stubs-quarter-wave](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/input-impedance-stubs-quarter-wave.md) |
| **quasi-static** | — | Regime where a circuit is small compared with a wavelength, so lumped elements and Kirchhoff's laws work. | lumped if ℓ < λ/20 in the medium (λ/10 rough) | [02/quasi-static-vs-full-wave](kb/02-electromagnetics/quasi-static-vs-full-wave.md) |
| **Qucs-S** | — | Open-source schematic front end for ngspice/Xyce and the Qucsator-RF engine (S-parameters, harmonic balance). | — | [16/rf-circuit-simulators-harmonic-balance](kb/16-simulation-tools/rf-circuit-simulators-harmonic-balance.md) |
| **QZSS** | Quasi-Zenith Satellite System | Japan's regional GNSS augmentation, with GPS-compatible signals and the CLAS/MADOCA correction services. | — | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |

## R

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **radar range equation** | — | Received echo power from transmit power, antenna gains, wavelength, target RCS and range; echo power falls as 1/R⁴. | P_r = P_tG_tG_rλ²σ/((4π)³R⁴L); (4π)³ = 32.98 dB | [11/radar-range-equation](kb/11-radar/radar-range-equation.md) |
| **radiation efficiency** | — | Fraction of the power accepted by an antenna that is radiated rather than lost as heat in conductors, dielectrics or nearby objects. | η_rad = R_rad/(R_rad + R_loss) | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **radiation pattern** | — | Plot of an antenna's radiated field or power against direction, usually as E-plane and H-plane cuts. | — | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **radiation resistance** | — | The part of an antenna's input resistance that accounts for power radiated away (as opposed to lost as heat). | η_rad = R_rad/(R_rad + R_loss) | [02/radiation-from-currents-hertzian-dipole](kb/02-electromagnetics/radiation-from-currents-hertzian-dipole.md) |
| **radio horizon** | — | The distance to which a radio path stays above the earth's bulge, slightly beyond the visual horizon because the atmosphere bends waves downward. | d ≈ 4.12√h km (h in m, k = 4/3); two ends 4.12(√h₁ + √h₂) | [09/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon.md) |
| **RAE** | Radio Amateur Examination | Singapore's amateur licence exam, run by SARTS for IMDA. | — | [19/amateur-radio-licensing](kb/19-regulations-safety/amateur-radio-licensing.md) |
| **rain fade** | rain attenuation | Loss from raindrops absorbing and scattering the wave; negligible at 433 MHz–2.4 GHz, important above ~10 GHz. | specific attenuation γ = kR^α dB/km (ITU-R P.838) | [09/atmospheric-rain-attenuation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/atmospheric-rain-attenuation.md) |
| **raised cosine** | — | The standard zero-ISI pulse spectrum, with roll-off α setting the excess bandwidth. | occupied BW = R_s(1 + α) | [10/pulse-shaping-isi](kb/10-comms-modulation/pulse-shaping-isi.md) |
| **range migration** | — | A target's range changing across the synthetic aperture so its echo moves between range bins; must be corrected in SAR. | — | [11/sar-and-imaging](kb/11-radar/sar-and-imaging.md) |
| **range resolution** | — | Smallest separation at which two targets in range appear separate; set only by bandwidth. | ΔR = c/(2B): 4 GHz → 3.75 cm | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **range test** | — | Field test of a radio link at increasing distance (or with added attenuation) to check the link budget's prediction. | — | [17/antenna-range-ota-testing](kb/17-test-measurement/antenna-range-ota-testing.md) |
| **range–Doppler coupling** | — | A Doppler shift on an LFM pulse moves its compressed peak in range (the ambiguity "ridge"). | Δt = f_D·T/B | [11/ambiguity-function-waveforms](kb/11-radar/ambiguity-function-waveforms.md) |
| **range–Doppler map** | — | 2-D image of echo power versus range and radial velocity, from a range FFT and a Doppler FFT. | — | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **Rayleigh distribution** | — | Distribution of the envelope of complex Gaussian noise, and of a signal arriving by many paths with no dominant one (Rayleigh fading). | margin vs mean power: 10/20/30 dB for 10/1/0.1 % outage | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **RBW** | resolution bandwidth | The spectrum analyser's IF filter width; it sets how close two tones can be resolved and how much noise is displayed. | displayed noise rises 10 dB per 10× RBW | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **RC** | resistor–capacitor | First-order network (low-pass, high-pass, timing). (RC also abbreviates raised-cosine, resin content and relaxation oscillators — see ambiguous acronyms.) | τ = RC; f_3dB = 1/(2πRC) | [01/transient-and-step-response](kb/01-circuit-theory/transient-and-step-response.md) |
| **RCS** | radar cross-section | The area a target would need, re-radiating isotropically, to return the echo it actually returns; depends strongly on angle and frequency. | sphere (optical) πa²; flat plate 4πA²/λ²; trihedral 4πa⁴/(3λ²) | [11/radar-cross-section](kb/11-radar/radar-cross-section.md) |
| **reactance** | — | The imaginary part of impedance: energy is stored and returned rather than dissipated. Inductors have positive, capacitors negative reactance. | X_L = ωL, X_C = −1/(ωC) | [01/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance](kb/01-circuit-theory/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance.md) |
| **reactive power** | — | Power that sloshes back and forth between source and reactive elements without being consumed; measured in volt-amperes reactive. | Q = ½Im{VI*} (peak phasors) | [01/energy-storage-and-reactive-power](kb/01-circuit-theory/energy-storage-and-reactive-power.md) |
| **realized gain** | — | Antenna gain including its impedance-mismatch loss; what a link budget should use. | G_r = G(1 − \|Γ\|²) | [08/antenna-fundamentals](kb/08-antennas/antenna-fundamentals.md) |
| **reciprocal mixing** | — | A strong nearby signal mixing with the LO's phase-noise skirt and landing in the IF as noise; it limits close-in blocking. | noise = P_blocker + 𝓛(Δf) + 10 log B | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **RED** | Radio Equipment Directive (2014/53/EU) | EU law governing radio equipment placed on the market. | — | [19/certification-fcc-ce-imda](kb/19-regulations-safety/certification-fcc-ce-imda.md) |
| **Reed–Solomon code** | — | Block code over bytes that corrects burst errors; often the outer code around a convolutional inner code. | RS(255,223) corrects 16 byte errors | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **reference plane** | — | The point where calibration places the measurement; moving it to the DUT pins needs port extension or de-embedding. | — | [17/fixtures-and-deembedding](kb/17-test-measurement/fixtures-and-deembedding.md) |
| **reflectarray** | — | A flat array of printed reflecting elements whose phases imitate a curved reflector. | — | [08/lens-and-reflectarray-antennas](kb/08-antennas/lens-and-reflectarray-antennas.md) |
| **reflector antenna** | dish | A parabolic reflector illuminated by a feed; high gain set by its aperture and illumination efficiency. | G = η_ap(πD/λ)²; η_ap ≈ 0.5–0.7 | [08/horn-reflector-aperture-antennas](kb/08-antennas/horn-reflector-aperture-antennas.md) |
| **resonance** | — | The frequency where inductive and capacitive reactances cancel, so a series circuit has minimum and a parallel circuit maximum impedance. | f0 = 1/(2π√(LC)) | [01/resonance-and-q](kb/01-circuit-theory/resonance-and-q.md) |
| **return path** | — | The route a signal's return current takes back to its source; at RF it flows in the plane directly under the trace, so gaps or splits under a trace cause radiation and crosstalk. | — | [13/grounding-and-return-paths](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/grounding-and-return-paths.md) |
| **return-loss bridge** | — | A resistive bridge that separates reflected from incident signal so a generator and detector (or SA) can measure return loss. | — | [17/bench-measurement-recipes](kb/17-test-measurement/bench-measurement-recipes.md) |
| **RF** | radio frequency | Loosely, any frequency used for radio (kHz to hundreds of GHz); in a receiver chain "RF" also names the antenna-side signal before mixing, as opposed to IF and baseband. | — | [00/rf-concepts-for-beginners](kb/00-math-foundations/rf-concepts-for-beginners.md) |
| **rfcalc** | — | The KB's own tested Python calculator package (tools/rfcalc): dB/link/noise/matching/line/radar/array/LoRa formulas with a CLI. | python3 -m rfcalc … | [16/python-rf-toolkit](kb/16-simulation-tools/python-rf-toolkit.md) |
| **RFID** | radio-frequency identification | Tags read by a reader's field: inductively coupled at LF (125 kHz) and HF (13.56 MHz), backscatter at UHF (860–960 MHz). | — | [08/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt](kb/08-antennas/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt.md) |
| **RFM95** | HopeRF RFM95/96/98 | SX127x-based LoRa modules with an SPI interface. | — | [15/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec.md) |
| **RFSoC** | RF system-on-chip | AMD/Xilinx FPGAs with multi-GS/s ADCs and DACs on die, for direct-RF sampling. | — | [14/fpga-for-sdr](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fpga-for-sdr.md) |
| **RG-174** | — | Thin (2.8 mm) PE 50 Ω coax for short jumpers; lossy. | ≈ 0.64 dB/m at 433 MHz | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **RG-316** | — | Thin (2.5 mm) PTFE 50 Ω coax for pigtails and inside avionics bays; high temperature rating, high loss per metre. | ≈ 0.72 dB/m at 433 MHz | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **RG-58** | — | Common 5 mm 50 Ω coax with solid PE dielectric; moderate loss. | ≈ 0.29 dB/m at 433 MHz | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **RHCP** | right-hand circular polarization | CP whose field rotates clockwise as seen from behind, looking in the direction of travel (IEEE convention). GNSS signals are RHCP. | IEEE: (x̂ − jŷ)e^{−jkz}; optics uses the opposite naming | [08/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization](kb/08-antennas/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization.md) |
| **Rician distribution** | — | Envelope distribution of a steady (line-of-sight) component plus random scattered paths; the K-factor is the ratio of steady to scattered power. | K = 0 → Rayleigh; large K → nearly Gaussian about the LOS level | [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md) |
| **RINEX** | Receiver Independent Exchange Format | Standard text format for raw GNSS observations and navigation data, used for post-processing. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **rise time** | — | Time for a step to go from 10 % to 90 %; tied to bandwidth for a first-order system. | t_r ≈ 0.35/BW | [01/transient-and-step-response](kb/01-circuit-theory/transient-and-step-response.md) |
| **RL** | return loss | How far below the incident power the reflected power is, as a positive dB number; bigger is better. (RL is also used for a load resistor R_L and the RL circuit — see the ambiguous-acronyms section.) | RL = −20 log₁₀\|Γ\|; RL 10 dB ↔ VSWR 1.92 | [03/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch.md) |
| **RLC** | resistor–inductor–capacitor | The basic second-order circuit; its damping ratio and Q set ringing and bandwidth. | ζ = 1/(2Q) | [01/transient-and-step-response](kb/01-circuit-theory/transient-and-step-response.md) |
| **RLGC** | per-unit-length resistance, inductance, conductance, capacitance | The four line constants of the telegrapher model; field solvers output them. | Z0 = √((R′+jωL′)/(G′+jωC′)) | [03/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance.md) |
| **RMB** | Reed–Mallett–Brennan rule | Adaptive-filter rule: using K training samples to estimate an N-dimensional covariance loses SNR by (K+2−N)/(K+1); K ≈ 2N keeps the loss to about 3 dB. | loss = (K + 2 − N)/(K + 1) | [11/clutter-and-mti](kb/11-radar/clutter-and-mti.md) |
| **RMS** | root mean square | The DC-equivalent heating value of a varying signal; a sine's RMS is its peak divided by √2. The KB's phasors are peak values. | V_rms = V_pk/√2; P = V_rms²/R | [00/complex-numbers-and-phasors](kb/00-math-foundations/complex-numbers-and-phasors.md) |
| **RMS detector** | — | Detector that reads true average power regardless of waveform crest factor; needed for OFDM and noise-like signals. | — | [06/detectors-and-power-meters](kb/06-rf-circuits/detectors-and-power-meters.md) |
| **RNSS** | radionavigation-satellite service | ITU allocation category for GNSS signals (the bands GPS, Galileo, BeiDou use). | — | [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md) |
| **RO4350B** | Rogers RO4350B laminate | Low-loss hydrocarbon-ceramic laminate processed like FR-4; a common upgrade for RF boards (JLCPCB offers it). | design Dk 3.66 (process 3.48 ± 0.05), Df 0.0037 @ 10 GHz | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **ROC** | region of convergence | The set of s or z values for which a Laplace or z-transform sum converges; together with the poles it decides causality and stability. (On 21's sources page "ROC" is a page key for the rockets page.) | causal + stable discrete system: ROC includes the unit circle | [00/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems](kb/00-math-foundations/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems.md) |
| **Rotman lens** | — | A planar true-time-delay lens that forms multiple wideband beams. | — | [12/beamforming-architectures](kb/12-phased-arrays/beamforming-architectures.md) |
| **RP002** | LoRaWAN Regional Parameters (RP002-1.0.x) | LoRa Alliance document giving the channel plans and power/duty limits for each region. | — | [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **RR** | ITU Radio Regulations | The international treaty that allocates bands; footnotes such as RR 5.138 and 5.150 define the ISM bands. | — | [19/spectrum-allocation-ism-bands](kb/19-regulations-safety/spectrum-allocation-ism-bands.md) |
| **RRC** | root-raised-cosine | The square root of a raised-cosine spectrum, split between transmitter and receiver so that together they are zero-ISI and the receiver is matched. | — | [10/pulse-shaping-isi](kb/10-comms-modulation/pulse-shaping-isi.md) |
| **RSSI** | received signal strength indicator | The radio's estimate of received power in dBm; on LoRa chips it includes noise, so below the noise floor the packet SNR is more informative. | SX127x LoRa: RSSI = −164 + Rssi (LF port) or −157 + Rssi (HF port), + SNR/4 when SNR < 0 | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **RTC** | real-time clock | Low-power clock (often 32.768 kHz) that keeps time while the system sleeps; lets a GNSS receiver warm- or hot-start. | — | [21/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris](kb/21-gnss/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris.md) |
| **RTCM** | Radio Technical Commission for Maritime Services (RTCM 3 messages) | The standard format for GNSS correction messages (1005 base position, MSM4/MSM7 observations, 1230 GLONASS biases). | 4-constellation dual-frequency MSM4 at 1 Hz ≈ 710 B/s (MSM7 ≈ 1130 B/s) | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **RTK** | real-time kinematic | Centimetre-level positioning by differencing the rover's carrier phase against a nearby base station in real time and fixing integer ambiguities. | ZED-F9P: 0.01 m + 1 ppm CEP | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **RTKLIB** | — | Open-source GNSS post-processing and real-time RTK software. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **RTL-SDR** | — | Cheap USB SDR built from a DVB-T TV dongle (RTL2832U demodulator + R820T2/R828D tuner); 8-bit, ≈ 2.4 MS/s, ≈ 24–1766 MHz. | NF ≈ 5.4 dB at 437 MHz (V3, measured) | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **rubber duck** | — | A short, helically wound flexible whip; convenient but less efficient than a full-size λ/4. | — | [08/helical-antennas](kb/08-antennas/helical-antennas.md) |
| **Ruthroff balun** | voltage balun | Transmission-line balun/transformer that forces equal and opposite voltages; gives a 4:1 impedance ratio easily but balances current less well. | — | [06/baluns-and-rf-transformers](kb/06-rf-circuits/baluns-and-rf-transformers.md) |
| **RVP** | residual video phase | An extra phase term left after dechirping, proportional to the square of delay; it matters in SAR and long-range stretch processing. | φ = πSτ² | [11/fmcw-radar](kb/11-radar/fmcw-radar.md) |
| **RX** | receive / receiver | The receiving side of a radio link. | — | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |

## S

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **S-parameters** | scattering parameters | Two-port (or N-port) description in terms of incident and reflected waves at matched ports — what a VNA measures. S11 is input reflection, S21 forward gain, S12 reverse isolation, S22 output reflection. | \|S21\|² = transducer gain with 50 Ω source and load | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **S11** | input reflection coefficient | The S-parameter for wave reflected back out of port 1; its magnitude in dB is minus the return loss. | S11 = −10 dB ↔ RL 10 dB | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **S12** | reverse transmission coefficient | Wave leaking backwards from port 2 to port 1; the reverse isolation of an amplifier. Non-zero S12 is what makes amplifiers potentially unstable. | — | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **S21** | forward transmission coefficient | The S-parameter for wave delivered from port 1 to port 2: gain of an amplifier or insertion loss of a filter or cable. | IL = −20 log\|S21\| | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **S22** | output reflection coefficient | Reflection looking back into port 2. | — | [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md) |
| **safe distance** | — | Distance from an antenna beyond which power density is below the exposure limit (OET-65 far-field estimate, optionally ×2.56 for ground reflection and × duty factor). | R = √(k·EIRP·D/(4πS_lim)) | [19/rf-exposure-safety](kb/19-regulations-safety/rf-exposure-safety.md) |
| **SAR** | synthetic-aperture radar | Imaging radar that moves a small antenna along a path and coherently combines echoes to synthesise a long aperture, giving fine cross-range resolution. (Also specific absorption rate and SAR ADC.) | stripmap cross-range resolution ≈ D/2 | [11/sar-and-imaging](kb/11-radar/sar-and-imaging.md) |
| **SAR (exposure)** | specific absorption rate | RF power absorbed per kilogram of tissue; the basic exposure restriction below a few GHz. | whole-body 0.08 W/kg public, 0.4 W/kg occupational; local 2/10 W/kg | [19/rf-exposure-safety](kb/19-regulations-safety/rf-exposure-safety.md) |
| **SAR ADC** | successive-approximation-register ADC | ADC that finds each sample by binary search with a comparator and internal DAC; 8–20 bits at kS/s–tens of MS/s, low power. (SAR also means synthetic-aperture radar and specific absorption rate.) | — | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **SAW** | surface acoustic wave | Filter (or resonator) that converts RF to sound waves on a piezoelectric chip; small, sharp, cheap filters up to ≈ 2.5–3 GHz. | 1.5–3 dB IL; TCF ≈ −45 ppm/°C (TC-SAW −15 to −25) | [06/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-filters-lc-saw-baw-cavity-microstrip.md) |
| **SBAS** | satellite-based augmentation system | Geostationary satellites broadcasting GPS corrections and integrity data (WAAS, EGNOS, MSAS, GAGAN); metre-level improvement. | — | [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md) |
| **scalloping loss** | — | Drop in an FFT's reading of a tone that falls midway between bins. | Hann 1.42 dB; rectangular 3.92 dB | [14/fft-spectral-analysis-windows](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fft-spectral-analysis-windows.md) |
| **scan loss** | — | Fall in array gain as the beam is steered away from broadside, from the shrinking projected aperture and the element pattern. | ≈ cos θ (or cos^1.5 θ) | [12/beam-steering-grating-lobes](kb/12-phased-arrays/beam-steering-grating-lobes.md) |
| **Schottky diode** | — | Metal–semiconductor diode with low forward voltage and almost no stored charge; used for RF detectors, mixers and clamps. | — | [05/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor](kb/05-analog-electronics/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor.md) |
| **scikit-rf** | — | Python library for networks and S-parameters: Touchstone I/O, cascading, de-embedding, VNA calibration and plotting. | — | [16/python-rf-toolkit](kb/16-simulation-tools/python-rf-toolkit.md) |
| **scintillation** | — | Rapid fluctuation of signal amplitude and phase caused by ionospheric irregularities; strong near the geomagnetic equator after sunset, a GNSS issue in Singapore. | — | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **SDR** | software-defined radio | A radio whose channel filtering, demodulation and often mixing are done in software on digitised samples; the hardware is a tunable front end plus ADC/DAC. | — | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **SDR software** | — | Receiver applications for SDRs: GQRX, SDR++, SDRangel (general receivers), URH (protocol reverse-engineering), SatDump (satellite decoding), inspectrum (offline IQ analysis). | — | [14/gnu-radio-and-software-stack](kb/14-sdr-dsp/gnu-radio-and-software-stack.md) |
| **selectivity** | — | A receiver's ability to reject signals on nearby channels. | — | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **semi-rigid coax** | — | Coax with a solid copper tube shield; excellent shielding and phase stability, bent once to shape. | — | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **sensitivity** | — | The weakest input signal that a receiver demodulates at a stated error rate (BER or PER) and packet length. | S = −174 + NF + 10 log₁₀B + SNR_req dBm; SX1262 BW125: −124 (SF7) … −137 dBm (SF12) | [07/sensitivity-and-mds](kb/07-noise-linearity/sensitivity-and-mds.md) |
| **SER** | symbol error rate | Fraction of symbols received wrongly; for M-ary schemes (LoRa, M-FSK) it is computed first, then converted to BER. | — | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **SF** | spreading factor | LoRa's chips-per-symbol exponent (SF5–SF12): each symbol is a chirp of 2^SF chips carrying SF bits. One SF step doubles time on air for ≈ 2.5 dB more sensitivity. (SF is also a page key and a VNA error term — see ambiguous acronyms.) | T_sym = 2^SF/BW; SNR_req −7.5 dB (SF7) … −20 dB (SF12) | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **SFDR** | spurious-free dynamic range | Range of input levels over which the receiver's own spurs (IM3, or ADC spurs) stay below the noise floor. | SFDR₃ = ⅔(IIP3 − N) | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **SFI** | solar flux index | The 10.7 cm (2.8 GHz) solar radio flux in solar flux units; a daily proxy for solar activity and HF conditions. | in solar flux units (sfu); ≈ 92 sfu on 1 Oct 2026, NOAA forecast falling toward ≈ 85 by late 2028 | [09/solar-activity-and-space-weather](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/solar-activity-and-space-weather.md) |
| **Shannon limit** | Shannon–Hartley capacity | The maximum error-free data rate a channel of bandwidth B and SNR can carry; as bandwidth grows the required Eb/N0 falls to −1.59 dB. | C = B log₂(1 + S/N) | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **shield can** | — | A soldered metal box over an RF section; it can resonate as a cavity, so its size and absorbers matter. | — | [13/shielding-and-isolation](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/shielding-and-isolation.md) |
| **shielding effectiveness** | — | How much a shield attenuates fields passing through it, in dB; for real enclosures it is set by slots and seams, not by the metal. | slot of length L < λ/2: SE ≈ 20 log(λ/2L) | [13/shielding-and-isolation](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/shielding-and-isolation.md) |
| **shot noise** | — | Noise from the discrete arrival of charges in a current crossing a barrier (diode, transistor junction). | i_n = √(2qI·B) | [07/shot-and-flicker-noise](kb/07-noise-linearity/shot-and-flicker-noise.md) |
| **SI** | Système international d'unités (International System of Units) | The metric unit system; since 2019 its constants (c, h, e, k) are exact by definition, and μ0 became a measured quantity. Also used as "signal integrity" on some pages — see the ambiguous-acronyms section. | k = 1.380649×10⁻²³ J/K exact; c = 299 792 458 m/s | [02/electromagnetic-units-and-constants](kb/02-electromagnetics/electromagnetic-units-and-constants.md) |
| **sidelobe** | — | Any lobe of a radiation pattern other than the main beam; sidelobes waste power and let interference in. | uniform aperture first sidelobe −13.26 dB | [12/tapering-sidelobe-control](kb/12-phased-arrays/tapering-sidelobe-control.md) |
| **SiGe** | silicon–germanium | Silicon process with germanium-doped bases for fast HBTs; cheap, integrable LNAs and transceivers to mm-wave. | — | [05/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal](kb/05-analog-electronics/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal.md) |
| **sigma-delta ADC** | — | Oversampling ADC with noise shaping and a digital decimation filter; very high resolution at modest bandwidth. | — | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **SigMF** | Signal Metadata Format | Open format pairing a raw IQ recording with a JSON file describing sample rate, centre frequency and annotations. | — | [14/gnu-radio-and-software-stack](kb/14-sdr-dsp/gnu-radio-and-software-stack.md) |
| **signal generator** | — | Instrument producing a calibrated RF signal (CW or modulated) for receiver and component testing. | — | [17/signal-generators-and-sources](kb/17-test-measurement/signal-generators-and-sources.md) |
| **signal-flow graph** | — | Diagram of nodes (waves) and branches (S-parameters); Mason's rule then gives any transfer ratio. Used to derive VNA error models. | — | [03/signal-flow-graphs-mason](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/signal-flow-graphs-mason.md) |
| **SiK radio** | — | Open firmware for Si1000-based 433/915 MHz FHSS telemetry radios (3DR, RFD900x) used by drones and some rockets. | — | [15/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4.md) |
| **SimSmith** | — | Free interactive Smith-chart matching tool. | — | [16/impedance-and-rf-calculators](kb/16-simulation-tools/impedance-and-rf-calculators.md) |
| **SINAD** | signal-to-noise-and-distortion ratio | (Signal + noise + distortion)/(noise + distortion); used for FM receiver sensitivity (12 dB SINAD) and ADC dynamic performance. | ENOB = (SINAD − 1.76)/6.02 | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **sinc** | — | The function sin(πx)/(πx): the spectrum of a rectangular pulse, the pattern of a uniform aperture and the response of a DFT bin. | first sidelobe −13.26 dB | [00/special-functions-bessel-sinc](kb/00-math-foundations/special-functions-bessel-sinc.md) |
| **SINR** | signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio | Like SNR but counting interference as noise; what matters in crowded bands and arrays. | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **SIR** | signal-to-interference ratio | Wanted signal power divided by interference power (noise excluded). | — | [10/ofdm](kb/10-comms-modulation/ofdm.md) |
| **SISRE** | signal-in-space range error | The part of the ranging error due to broadcast orbit and clock errors; differs by constellation. | — | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **SIW** | substrate-integrated waveguide | A waveguide made inside a PCB by two rows of plated vias between ground planes; brings waveguide-like low loss and shielding to planar boards. | via pitch ≤ 2× drill, ≤ λg/10 | [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md) |
| **skin depth** | — | Depth at which current density has fallen to 1/e (37 %) of its surface value. | copper (IACS): δ = 66.1 µm/√(f/MHz): 3.18 µm @ 433 MHz, 2.19 µm @ 915 MHz, 1.67 µm @ 1575 MHz | [02/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness](kb/02-electromagnetics/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness.md) |
| **skin effect** | — | At high frequency, current crowds into a thin layer at a conductor's surface, raising its resistance. | δ = 1/√(πfμσ) | [02/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness](kb/02-electromagnetics/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness.md) |
| **skywave** | — | HF signal returned to earth by the ionosphere; it allows beyond-horizon contacts with a skip zone in between. | — | [09/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction.md) |
| **slew rate** | — | Maximum rate of change of an amplifier's output voltage; large fast signals are distorted if they need more. | need SR > 2πf·V_pk; FPBW = SR/(2πV_pk) | [05/op-amps-real-limitations](kb/05-analog-electronics/op-amps-real-limitations.md) |
| **slot antenna** | — | A narrow slot cut in a metal sheet; the complement of a dipole. Also unintended slots radiate or leak through shields. | Babinet: Z_slot·Z_dipole = η0²/4 | [08/slot-antennas](kb/08-antennas/slot-antennas.md) |
| **SMA** | SubMiniature version A connector | The standard small threaded 50 Ω RF connector (DC–18 GHz). RP-SMA reverses the centre-pin gender (Wi-Fi gear), a common mismatch trap. | torque: brass 0.57 N·m (5 in-lb), stainless 0.9 N·m (8 in-lb) | [18/connectors-and-cables-parts](kb/18-components-parts/connectors-and-cables-parts.md), [13/connectors-and-launches](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/connectors-and-launches.md) |
| **SMD** | surface-mount device | Component soldered onto pads on the board surface (0402, QFN …), as opposed to through-hole. | sizes 0201, 0402, 0603 (imperial) | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **SMI** | sample-matrix inversion | Adaptive beamforming that inverts a covariance matrix estimated from data snapshots; its SNR loss follows the RMB rule. | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **Smith chart** | — | A polar plot of reflection coefficient overlaid with circles of constant resistance and reactance; the standard tool for seeing and designing matching networks. | centre = Z0; edge = \|Γ\| = 1 | [03/smith-chart](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/smith-chart.md) |
