| **SMPS** | switched-mode power supply | Efficient regulator (buck, boost) that switches at hundreds of kHz to MHz; its switching harmonics and ripple must be kept out of RF. | — | [05/power-supplies-for-rf-and-analog](kb/05-analog-electronics/power-supplies-for-rf-and-analog.md) |
| **Snoek's limit** | — | Trade-off in ferrites: the higher the initial permeability, the lower the frequency at which it rolls off. | (μ_i − 1)·f_r ≈ constant (≈ (2/3)(γ/2π)μ0M_s) | [02/magnetic-materials-permeability](kb/02-electromagnetics/magnetic-materials-permeability.md) |
| **SNR** | signal-to-noise ratio | Signal power divided by noise power in a stated bandwidth. LoRa quotes SNR over the whole channel bandwidth, which is why it can be negative; detection budgets in the KB use E/N0. | LoRa SNR_req −7.5 dB (SF7) … −20 dB (SF12) | [07/sensitivity-and-mds](kb/07-noise-linearity/sensitivity-and-mds.md) |
| **SoapySDR** | — | Vendor-neutral C++/Python API for SDR hardware, so the same program can drive RTL-SDR, HackRF, Pluto and others. | — | [14/gnu-radio-and-software-stack](kb/14-sdr-dsp/gnu-radio-and-software-stack.md) |
| **soft decision** | — | Passing the decoder reliability information (not just 0/1) for each bit; gains about 2 dB over hard decisions. | — | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **SOI** | silicon-on-insulator | CMOS built on an insulating layer; low parasitic capacitance, the modern RF-switch process. | — | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **SOL** | short–open–load | One-port calibration with three known standards. | — | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **soldermask** | solder mask | The thin polymer coating over a PCB's copper; it lowers a microstrip's impedance a few ohms and adds a little loss. | JLC 50 Ω: 0.359 mm masked vs 0.377 mm bare | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **SOLT** | short–open–load–thru | Two-port calibration adding a thru connection; standard for coaxial measurements. (SOLR uses an unknown reciprocal thru.) | — | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **SPDT** | single-pole double-throw | Switch connecting one common port to either of two others. (SP4T: four.) | — | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **spectral efficiency** | — | Data rate per hertz of bandwidth, in b/s/Hz. | — | [10/ber-ebn0-and-shannon](kb/10-comms-modulation/ber-ebn0-and-shannon.md) |
| **spectral leakage** | — | Energy of a tone spreading into neighbouring FFT bins because the block does not hold a whole number of cycles. | — | [14/fft-spectral-analysis-windows](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fft-spectral-analysis-windows.md) |
| **spectrogram** | waterfall | Image of successive FFT spectra against time; the standard view for finding and identifying signals. | — | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |
| **spectrum analyser** | — | Instrument that displays power versus frequency; swept-tuned or FFT-based. | — | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **SPI** | serial peripheral interface | Fast four-wire synchronous serial bus used by bare LoRa chips (SX126x), PLLs and phased-array ICs. | — | [18/rf-transceiver-ics](kb/18-components-parts/rf-transceiver-ics.md) |
| **SPICE** | Simulation Program with Integrated Circuit Emphasis | The standard circuit simulator family (ngspice, LTspice, Xyce): DC, AC, transient and noise analysis of netlists. | — | [16/spice-circuit-simulation](kb/16-simulation-tools/spice-circuit-simulation.md) |
| **SPICE model** | — | A circuit-simulator description of a component (a netlist of ideal elements, or a device model card). Vendor RF models often come as S-parameter files instead. | — | [04/component-models-s2p-spice](kb/04-real-components-hf/component-models-s2p-spice.md) |
| **split plane** | — | A ground or power plane cut into separate regions; traces crossing a split lose their return path. Usually a mistake on RF boards. | — | [13/grounding-and-return-paths](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/grounding-and-return-paths.md) |
| **spoofing** | — | Transmitting counterfeit GNSS signals so a receiver computes a false position or time. | — | [21/gnss-interference-jamming-spoofing](kb/21-gnss/gnss-interference-jamming-spoofing.md) |
| **sporadic E** | — | Patches of dense ionisation in the E layer that briefly reflect VHF (6 m, 2 m, FM broadcast) over 1000–2000 km. | — | [09/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation.md) |
| **spur** | spurious signal | An unwanted discrete tone (from clock harmonics, PLL fractional patterns, mixer products) in a spectrum. | quoted in dBc | [07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md) |
| **spurious emission** | — | Emission outside the necessary bandwidth (harmonics, mixing products) that regulations cap. | SG: −36 dBm below 1 GHz, −30 dBm above | [19/singapore-imda-rules](kb/19-regulations-safety/singapore-imda-rules.md) |
| **SQNR** | signal-to-quantisation-noise ratio | Ratio of a full-scale sine to the quantisation noise of an ideal N-bit converter. | SQNR = 6.02N + 1.76 dB (8 bit: 49.9 dB; 12 bit: 74 dB) | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |
| **square-law detector** | — | A diode detector at small signal, whose output voltage is proportional to input power. | — | [06/detectors-and-power-meters](kb/06-rf-circuits/detectors-and-power-meters.md) |
| **squegging** | — | An oscillator turning itself on and off at a low rate because its bias network recovers too slowly; shows as spurious sidebands. | — | [05/oscillators](kb/05-analog-electronics/oscillators.md) |
| **SRAD** | student researched and designed | IREC term for avionics and hardware the team built itself (as opposed to commercial units). | — | [19/international-rules-for-competitions](kb/19-regulations-safety/international-rules-for-competitions.md) |
| **SRD** | short-range device | Regulatory class of low-power, licence-exempt transmitters (EU, Singapore), with power, duty-cycle and bandwidth limits per sub-band. | SG 433.05–434.79 MHz: 10 mW e.r.p., ≤ 10 % duty | [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md) |
| **SRF** | self-resonant frequency | Frequency where a component's parasitic L and C resonate. Capacitors are inductive above it, inductors capacitive; use parts well below (inductors) or near (DC blocks) their SRF. | f_SR = 1/(2π√(ESL·C)) | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **SS (amateur)** | spread spectrum (FCC Part 97) | US amateur rules treat bandwidth-expansion modulations such as LoRa as "SS" emissions with their own power limit. | ≤ 10 W PEP (97.313(j)) | [19/amateur-radio-licensing](kb/19-regulations-safety/amateur-radio-licensing.md) |
| **SSB** | single sideband | AM with the carrier and one sideband removed; half the bandwidth and much better power efficiency. Also "single-sideband" phase noise 𝓛(f). | — | [10/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb](kb/10-comms-modulation/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb.md) |
| **SSC** | spread-spectrum clocking | Slowly modulating a digital clock's frequency to spread its harmonics and lower peak emissions. | peak reduction a few dB | [13/mixed-signal-layout](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/mixed-signal-layout.md) |
| **SSN** | sunspot number | Monthly count-based index of solar activity; correlates with F10.7 and HF MUFs. | ≈ 60 (Sep 2026) | [09/solar-activity-and-space-weather](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/solar-activity-and-space-weather.md) |
| **stability (amplifier)** | — | An amplifier is unconditionally stable if no passive source or load can make it oscillate; otherwise some impedances (inside its stability circles) cause oscillation. | Rollett K > 1 and \|Δ\| < 1, or μ > 1 | [06/amplifier-stability-and-biasing](kb/06-rf-circuits/amplifier-stability-and-biasing.md) |
| **stackup** | — | The sequence and thickness of copper and dielectric layers in a PCB; it fixes trace impedances and which plane each trace returns on. | JLC04161H-7628: L1–L2 prepreg 0.2104 mm | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **standing wave** | — | The stationary interference pattern of incident and reflected waves on a mismatched line, with voltage maxima and minima λ/2 apart. | — | [03/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch.md) |
| **STAP** | space–time adaptive processing | Joint adaptive filtering across antenna channels and pulses to cancel clutter and jamming that depend on angle and Doppler (airborne radar). | needs ≈ 2× degrees of freedom of training samples (RMB) | [11/clutter-and-mti](kb/11-radar/clutter-and-mti.md) |
| **steering vector** | — | The vector of phases an array's elements see for a plane wave from a given direction; beamforming weights are built from it. | v_n = e^{+jkx_n u} (KB convention); weights e^{−jkx_n u₀} | [12/beam-steering-grating-lobes](kb/12-phased-arrays/beam-steering-grating-lobes.md) |
| **STFT** | short-time Fourier transform | FFTs of successive short windows of a signal; the maths behind a spectrogram or waterfall. | time resolution × frequency resolution ≈ 1 | [14/fft-spectral-analysis-windows](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fft-spectral-analysis-windows.md) |
| **STM32WL** | — | ST microcontroller with an SX126x-class LoRa radio on the same die. | — | [18/rf-transceiver-ics](kb/18-components-parts/rf-transceiver-ics.md) |
| **stripline** | — | A trace buried between two ground planes in a PCB; true TEM, well shielded, slower and narrower than microstrip for 50 Ω. | — | [03/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/planar-lines-microstrip-stripline-cpwg.md) |
| **stub** | — | A short length of line, open or shorted, used as a reactance in matching networks and filters. | shorted λ/4 stub = open circuit | [03/input-impedance-stubs-quarter-wave](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/input-impedance-stubs-quarter-wave.md) |
| **superheterodyne** | — | Receiver that converts the RF to a fixed IF, filters and amplifies there, then demodulates; the classic architecture. | — | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **surface resistance** | — | The resistance of a square of conductor at high frequency, as if all current flowed in one skin depth. | R_s = 1/(σδ) = √(πfμ/σ) | [02/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness](kb/02-electromagnetics/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness.md) |
| **surface roughness** | — | Copper foil teeth comparable to the skin depth lengthen the current path and add loss (up to about 2× at high frequency). | Hammerstad: K = 1 + (2/π)atan(1.4(Δ/δ)²) | [02/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness](kb/02-electromagnetics/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness.md) |
| **susceptance** | — | The imaginary part of admittance. | B_C = ωC | [01/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance](kb/01-circuit-theory/lumped-elements-impedance-admittance.md) |
| **SVD** | singular value decomposition | Factorisation of any matrix into rotations and scalings; reveals rank and conditioning, and underlies subspace methods such as MUSIC. | A = UΣVᴴ | [00/matrices-and-linear-algebra-for-networks](kb/00-math-foundations/matrices-and-linear-algebra-for-networks.md) |
| **Swerling models** | — | Five standard models (0–4) of how a target's RCS fluctuates from scan to scan or pulse to pulse; fluctuating targets need more SNR. | P_D 0.9, P_FA 10⁻⁶: Swerling 0 χ = 13.2 dB (non-coherent) | [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md) |
| **SX126x** | Semtech SX1261/SX1262/SX1268 family | Second-generation Semtech LoRa/FSK transceivers (sub-GHz); SX1262 is the +22 dBm part inside EBYTE E22 modules. | SX1262 sensitivity −124 (SF7) … −137 dBm (SF12) at BW125 | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **SX127x** | Semtech SX1272/SX1276/SX1278 family | First-generation Semtech LoRa transceivers (RFM95, many older modules); +20 dBm PA_BOOST, SF6–SF12. | — | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **SX128x** | Semtech SX1280/SX1281 | 2.4 GHz LoRa/FLRC/GFSK transceivers; LoRa at 2.4 GHz is legal worldwide but, with the same antenna gains, has ≈ 8.5 dB more free-space loss than 915 MHz (15 dB more than 433 MHz). | — | [15/semtech-chip-family](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/semtech-chip-family.md) |
| **symbol timing recovery** | — | Finding the best sampling instant of each symbol from the received signal (Gardner, Mueller–Müller, early–late). | — | [10/synchronization-carrier-timing](kb/10-comms-modulation/synchronization-carrier-timing.md) |
| **sync word** | — | A fixed bit (or symbol) pattern after the preamble that marks the frame start and separates networks. | LoRa private/public: 0x12/0x34 (SX127x), 0x1424/0x3444 (SX126x) | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **system noise temperature** | — | Antenna temperature plus receiver noise temperature referred to the antenna terminals; it sets the true noise floor. | T_sys = T_a + T_e | [07/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature](kb/07-noise-linearity/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature.md) |

## T

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **T network** | — | Three-element matching network (series–shunt–series) shaped like T. | — | [03/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/impedance-matching-lumped-and-distributed.md) |
| **T-parameters** | transfer (scattering-transfer) parameters | Rearranged S-parameters that, like ABCD, multiply for cascades; used in de-embedding. | — | [01/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd](kb/01-circuit-theory/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd.md) |
| **T/R module** | transmit/receive module | Per-element module of an active array containing PA, LNA, switch or circulator, phase shifter and attenuator. | — | [12/beamforming-architectures](kb/12-phased-arrays/beamforming-architectures.md) |
| **T/R switch** | transmit/receive switch | Switch that connects one antenna to the PA when transmitting and to the LNA when receiving; must protect the LNA from PA power. | — | [06/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters](kb/06-rf-circuits/rf-switches-attenuators-limiters.md) |
| **T0** | standard noise temperature | The reference temperature for noise figure, 290 K by IEEE convention; state any other temperature used (some analog pages use 300 K). | 290 K | [07/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature](kb/07-noise-linearity/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature.md) |
| **tapering** | amplitude taper | Weighting element amplitudes (largest at the centre) to lower sidelobes, at the cost of a wider beam and some gain (taper loss). | Taylor, Dolph–Chebyshev, Hamming, Hann tapers | [12/tapering-sidelobe-control](kb/12-phased-arrays/tapering-sidelobe-control.md) |
| **TCDk** | thermal coefficient of dielectric constant | How much a laminate's Dk drifts with temperature, in ppm/°C. | RO4003C +40, RO4350B +50 ppm/°C; FR-4 is worse and less specified | [02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md) |
| **TCXO** | temperature-compensated crystal oscillator | Crystal oscillator with a circuit that cancels most of the quartz's temperature drift; needed for narrow LoRa bandwidths and fast GNSS acquisition. | ±0.5–2.5 ppm | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **TDD** | time-division duplex | Transmit and receive alternate in time on one frequency (all half-duplex LoRa links). | — | [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md) |
| **TDM** | time-division multiplexing | Sharing a channel or hardware by taking turns in time (TDM-MIMO radar, TDMA). | — | [11/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays](kb/11-radar/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays.md) |
| **TDM-MIMO** | time-division multiplexed MIMO | MIMO radar whose transmitters take turns chirp by chirp; simple, but motion between slots must be compensated. (BPM: binary phase coding; DDM: Doppler-division.) | — | [11/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays](kb/11-radar/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays.md) |
| **TDMA** | time-division multiple access | Users take turns in assigned time slots on one channel. | — | [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md) |
| **TDR** | time-domain reflectometry | Sending a fast step or pulse down a line and timing and sizing the reflections to locate impedance changes; NanoVNAs do it by inverse-FFT of S11 (the "time-domain transform"). | distance = v·t/2 | [03/time-domain-reflectometry](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/time-domain-reflectometry.md) |
| **TE** | transverse electric | Waveguide mode (or polarization) with no electric field along the propagation direction. Also "s" polarization in reflection. | TE10 is the dominant rectangular mode | [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md) |
| **TEC** | total electron content | Number of free electrons in a column through the ionosphere; it sets GNSS ionospheric delay and Faraday rotation. | 1 TECU = 10¹⁶ e/m² ≈ 0.162 m of L1 delay | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **TED** | timing-error detector | The part of a timing-recovery loop that measures how early or late the sampling is (e.g. Gardner TED, 2 samples/symbol). | — | [10/synchronization-carrier-timing](kb/10-comms-modulation/synchronization-carrier-timing.md) |
| **telegrapher's equations** | — | The pair of differential equations for voltage and current on a transmission line, built from per-metre R, L, G and C. | dV/dz = −(R′+jωL′)I; dI/dz = −(G′+jωC′)V | [03/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance.md) |
| **TEM** | transverse electromagnetic | Mode with both E and H perpendicular to the direction of travel and no cut-off frequency; the normal mode of coax and stripline (microstrip is quasi-TEM). | v = c/√εr | [03/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance.md) |
| **TEP** | trans-equatorial propagation | Evening VHF propagation across the geomagnetic equator via ionospheric irregularities; relevant to Singapore's latitude. | — | [09/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation.md) |
| **TETRA** | Terrestrial Trunked Radio | Digital professional mobile radio standard (π/4-DQPSK, TDMA) used by public-safety services. | — | [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md) |
| **Tg** | glass-transition temperature | Temperature at which a laminate's resin softens and its expansion jumps; higher Tg laminates survive lead-free reflow and heat better. | standard FR-4 Tg ≈ 130–140 °C; high-Tg ≥ 170 °C | [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md) |
| **thermal noise** | Johnson–Nyquist noise | Random voltage from the thermal motion of electrons in any resistance; its power in a bandwidth depends only on temperature, not on the resistor value. | P = kTB; kT0 = −174 dBm/Hz; v_n = √(4kTRB): 0.895 nV/√Hz at 50 Ω | [07/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature](kb/07-noise-linearity/thermal-noise-and-noise-temperature.md) |
| **thermal via** | — | Vias under a hot part's exposed pad that conduct heat to inner and bottom copper. | — | [13/thermal-management-rf](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/thermal-management-rf.md) |
| **thin-film resistor** | — | Resistor made of a thin metal film; low parasitics and tight tolerance, used for RF terminations and pads. | — | [04/resistors-at-rf](kb/04-real-components-hf/resistors-at-rf.md) |
| **Thévenin equivalent** | — | Any linear two-terminal network behaves like one voltage source in series with one impedance (Norton: a current source in parallel). | — | [01/network-theorems-max-power-transfer](kb/01-circuit-theory/network-theorems-max-power-transfer.md) |
| **TI** | Texas Instruments | Maker of mm-wave radar SoCs (IWR/AWR), CC1xxx radios, data converters and op-amps. | — | [18/sourcing-and-eval-boards](kb/18-components-parts/sourcing-and-eval-boards.md) |
| **TIA** | transimpedance amplifier | Amplifier that turns an input current into an output voltage (photodiodes, detectors). Also the Telecommunications Industry Association (TIA-603) — see ambiguous acronyms. | V_out = −I_in R_F | [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md) |
| **time constant** | — | The time a first-order circuit takes to cover 63 % of a step change. | RC: τ = RC; RL: τ = L/R | [01/transient-and-step-response](kb/01-circuit-theory/transient-and-step-response.md) |
| **time–bandwidth product** | — | Pulse duration × bandwidth; it equals the pulse-compression gain and, for LoRa, the 2^SF processing gain. | TB = 2^SF (LoRa) | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **tinySA** | — | Low-cost hand-held spectrum analyser and signal generator. | — | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **TIS** | total isotropic sensitivity | A receiver's sensitivity averaged over all directions of arrival (OTA). | — | [17/antenna-range-ota-testing](kb/17-test-measurement/antenna-range-ota-testing.md) |
| **TM** | transverse magnetic | Waveguide mode (or polarization) with no magnetic field along the propagation direction; "p" polarization in reflection. Also CCSDS "TM" = telemetry. | — | [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md) |
| **ToA** | time on air | How long one packet occupies the channel; it sets duty-cycle use, collision risk and how fast a rocket's position updates. | 32 B SF7/125 = 71.9 ms; 32 B SF12/125 = 1810 ms (CR 4/5, 8-symbol preamble, explicit header, CRC) | [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md) |
| **Touchstone** | — | The plain-text file format (.s1p, .s2p, .snp) for S-parameter data; the option line (e.g. "# MHz S MA R 50") gives units, format and reference impedance. | — | [04/component-models-s2p-spice](kb/04-real-components-hf/component-models-s2p-spice.md) |
| **tracking loops** | — | The DLL (code), PLL (carrier phase) and FLL (frequency) feedback loops that follow each satellite after acquisition. Constant acceleration costs nothing in steady state; jerk breaks lock. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-architecture-acquisition-tracking.md) |
| **transconductance** | — | Change in output current per change in input voltage of a transistor. | BJT g_m = I_C/V_T | [05/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal](kb/05-analog-electronics/bjt-fundamentals-small-signal.md) |
| **transducer gain** | — | Power delivered to the load divided by the power available from the source; the gain that actually matters in a system. | = \|S21\|² for 50 Ω source and load | [06/lna-design](kb/06-rf-circuits/lna-design.md) |
| **transfer function** | — | Ratio of a linear system's output to its input as a function of complex frequency. Its poles and zeros set the frequency response and stability. | H(s) = Y(s)/X(s) | [00/laplace-transfer-functions-bode](kb/00-math-foundations/laplace-transfer-functions-bode.md) |
| **transformer** | — | Coupled windings that transform voltage, current and impedance by the turns ratio (and can isolate or balance). | Z_in = n²Z_L for turns ratio n | [01/transformers-coupled-inductors](kb/01-circuit-theory/transformers-coupled-inductors.md) |
| **transmission line** | — | A pair of conductors (coax, microstrip, twisted pair) treated as distributed inductance and capacitance, so signals travel as waves with a characteristic impedance. | v = 1/√(L′C′) | [03/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance.md) |
| **TRL** | thru–reflect–line | Two-port calibration using a thru, a reflect and a known-length line; accurate for PCB fixtures because the standards can be built on the same board. (LRM: line–reflect–match.) | line length ≈ λ/4 at band centre (20°–160° usable) | [17/fixtures-and-deembedding](kb/17-test-measurement/fixtures-and-deembedding.md) |
| **tropospheric delay** | — | GNSS delay from the neutral atmosphere, ≈ 2.4 m at zenith, larger at low elevation. | — | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **tropospheric ducting** | — | Trapping of VHF–microwave signals in a layer of the lower atmosphere with a steep refractivity gradient, giving very long ranges. | ducting when dN/dh < −157 N-units/km | [09/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/vhf-uhf-anomalous-propagation.md) |
| **TRP** | total radiated power | Power radiated by a device integrated over all directions; the OTA figure that captures antenna efficiency and mismatch together. | TRP = P_accepted·η | [17/antenna-range-ota-testing](kb/17-test-measurement/antenna-range-ota-testing.md) |
| **TS SRD** | IMDA Technical Specification for Short Range Devices | The Singapore document listing licence-exempt bands with power, duty-cycle and spurious limits. | 866–869 MHz 500 mW e.r.p. (29); 917–925 MHz LPWAN end device 100 mW e.r.p., ≤ 1 % (30c); spurious −36 dBm < 1 GHz, −30 dBm > 1 GHz | [19/singapore-imda-rules](kb/19-regulations-safety/singapore-imda-rules.md) |
| **TSS** | tangential signal sensitivity | An old detector/receiver sensitivity figure: the pulse level at which the noise band on a scope just lifts by its own width (≈ 8 dB SNR). | — | [06/detectors-and-power-meters](kb/06-rf-circuits/detectors-and-power-meters.md) |
| **TTD** | true time delay | Steering with actual delay lines instead of phase shifters, so the beam direction does not change with frequency; needed for wideband arrays. | τ_n = x_n sin θ₀/c | [12/phase-shifters-ttd-components](kb/12-phased-arrays/phase-shifters-ttd-components.md) |
| **TTFF** | time to first fix | Time from power-on (or reset) to the first position solution: cold start (no data), warm (almanac and time), hot (valid ephemeris). | ZED-F9P cold 25–30 s, hot 2 s; MAX-M10S cold 23–28 s (multi-GNSS), hot 1 s | [21/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris](kb/21-gnss/ttff-agps-almanac-ephemeris.md) |
| **TTN** | The Things Network | Community LoRaWAN network and server. | — | [15/lorawan-vs-raw-lora](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lorawan-vs-raw-lora.md) |
| **turbo code** | — | Parallel concatenated convolutional codes decoded iteratively; near-capacity performance (3G/4G, CCSDS). | — | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **TVS** | transient-voltage-suppressor diode | A fast avalanche diode that clamps ESD and surge spikes on I/O and supply lines; its capacitance matters on RF lines. | — | [05/analog-signal-conditioning](kb/05-analog-electronics/analog-signal-conditioning.md) |
| **two-port** | — | A network with an input and an output port, described by a 2×2 matrix (Z, Y, h, ABCD, S or T parameters). | — | [01/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd](kb/01-circuit-theory/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd.md) |
| **two-ray model** | plane-earth model | Received field as the sum of a direct ray and a ground-reflected ray; far out the loss rises 40 dB/decade and becomes independent of frequency. | L ≈ 40 log d − 20 log(h_t h_r) dB | [09/two-ray-ground-reflection](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/two-ray-ground-reflection.md) |
| **TX** | transmit / transmitter | The sending side of a radio link (TX power, TX chain). | — | [06/transmitter-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/transmitter-architectures.md) |

## U

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **u-blox** | — | Swiss GNSS module maker; the Space Bar boards use the ZED-F9P (multi-band RTK) and MAX-M10S (single-band, low power). | — | [21/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox.md) |
| **U.FL** | Hirose U.FL (also IPEX/MHF) | Tiny snap-on board connector for micro-coax pigtails; fragile and rated for only ≈ 30 mating cycles. | DC–6 GHz, VSWR ≤ 1.3 to 3 GHz | [13/connectors-and-launches](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/connectors-and-launches.md) |
| **UART** | universal asynchronous receiver–transmitter | Simple two-wire serial link (TX/RX); EBYTE LoRa modules and u-blox receivers talk to the MCU over it. | — | [15/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-modules-ebyte-rfm-heltec.md) |
| **UBX** | u-blox binary protocol | u-blox's binary configuration and output protocol (UBX-NAV-PVT, UBX-RXM-RAWX, UBX-MON-RF …); more complete than NMEA. | — | [21/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox](kb/21-gnss/gnss-receiver-integration-u-blox.md) |
| **UCA** | uniform circular array | Elements equally spaced on a circle; gives 360° direction finding (e.g. KrakenSDR's five-element ring). | — | [12/adaptive-beamforming-doa](kb/12-phased-arrays/adaptive-beamforming-doa.md) |
| **UERE** | user-equivalent range error | Total one-sigma ranging error from all sources (satellite clock/orbit, ionosphere, troposphere, multipath, receiver noise). | — | [21/gnss-error-sources-dop](kb/21-gnss/gnss-error-sources-dop.md) |
| **UHD** | USRP Hardware Driver | Ettus/NI driver library for USRP radios; RFNoC is their FPGA processing framework. | — | [14/gnu-radio-and-software-stack](kb/14-sdr-dsp/gnu-radio-and-software-stack.md) |
| **UKF** | unscented Kalman filter | Non-linear Kalman filter that propagates a set of sigma points through the model instead of linearising it. | — | [00/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation](kb/00-math-foundations/kalman-filter-and-state-estimation.md) |
| **ULA** | uniform linear array | Elements equally spaced along a line; the basic array for analysis and DOA. | — | [12/array-factor-linear-arrays](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-factor-linear-arrays.md) |
| **unambiguous range** | — | Greatest range a pulse radar can measure before echoes from one pulse arrive after the next pulse is sent. | R_u = c/(2·PRF) | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **unloaded Q** | — | Q of a resonator counting only its own losses, before the source and load are attached. | 1/Q_L = 1/Q_u + 1/Q_e | [01/resonance-and-q](kb/01-circuit-theory/resonance-and-q.md) |
| **URA** | uniform rectangular array | Planar grid of elements; its array factor is the product of two linear ones. | AF = AF_x(u − u₀)·AF_y(v − v₀) | [12/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling](kb/12-phased-arrays/planar-arrays-element-pattern-coupling.md) |
| **USRP** | Universal Software Radio Peripheral | Ettus/NI family of research SDRs (B200/B210 to X410). | — | [14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md) |
| **UTC** | Coordinated Universal Time | The civil time standard, kept within 0.9 s of earth rotation by leap seconds. | — | [21/gnss-timing-1pps](kb/21-gnss/gnss-timing-1pps.md) |
| **UWB** | ultra-wideband | Signals or devices with very large bandwidth (≥ 500 MHz or ≥ 20 % fractional) at very low power density; also the regulatory class used for some radar exemptions. | — | [19/singapore-imda-rules](kb/19-regulations-safety/singapore-imda-rules.md) |

## V

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **varactor** | — | Reverse-biased diode used as a voltage-controlled capacitor in VCOs and tunable filters. | — | [05/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor](kb/05-analog-electronics/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor.md) |
| **VBW** | video bandwidth | Post-detection smoothing filter of a spectrum analyser; it reduces trace noise without changing the average noise level. | — | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **VCO** | voltage-controlled oscillator | Oscillator whose frequency is tuned by a control voltage (usually through a varactor); the "engine" of a PLL. | tuning gain K_VCO in MHz/V | [06/vco-and-pll-synthesizers](kb/06-rf-circuits/vco-and-pll-synthesizers.md) |
| **VCTCXO** | voltage-controlled TCXO | A TCXO whose frequency can be trimmed by a control voltage (used in GPS-disciplined loops). | — | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |
| **velocity factor** | — | Speed of a wave on a cable as a fraction of the speed of light; it shortens the physical length of λ/4 stubs and baluns. | VF = 1/√εr: solid PE 0.66, PTFE ≈ 0.69–0.70, foam ≈ 0.8 | [03/coax-and-cables](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/coax-and-cables.md) |
| **VFA** | voltage-feedback amplifier | Conventional op-amp architecture with a constant gain–bandwidth product (contrast current-feedback amplifiers). | — | [05/op-amps-real-limitations](kb/05-analog-electronics/op-amps-real-limitations.md) |
| **VGA** | variable-gain amplifier | Amplifier whose gain is set by a control voltage or digital word; part of AGC loops and phased-array channels. (Not the video standard.) | — | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **via** | — | A plated hole connecting layers; at RF it is an inductor and, if it continues past the layer used, a resonant stub. | 1.6 mm × 0.3 mm ground via ≈ 0.54 nH | [13/vias-stitching-fencing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/vias-stitching-fencing.md) |
| **via fence** | — | A line of ground vias beside an RF trace (as in CPWG) to stop coupling and parallel-plate modes. | — | [13/vias-stitching-fencing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/vias-stitching-fencing.md) |
| **via inductance** | — | Inductance of a plated hole connecting layers; it limits how well a ground via really grounds at RF. | 1.6 mm × 0.3 mm via to a plane ≈ 0.54 nH; L1→L2 through 0.21 mm prepreg ≈ 0.02 nH | [04/pcb-parasitics-traces-vias-pads](kb/04-real-components-hf/pcb-parasitics-traces-vias-pads.md) |
| **via stitching** | — | Rows or grids of ground vias tying ground pours and planes together so they act as one ground at RF. | pitch λ/10–λ/20 at the highest frequency of concern | [13/vias-stitching-fencing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/vias-stitching-fencing.md) |
| **via-in-pad** | — | A via placed inside a component pad; must be filled and capped (POFV) so solder does not wick away. | — | [13/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/jlcpcb-rf-manufacturing.md) |
| **video** | radar video | Old radar term for the detected (envelope or baseband) receiver output that drove the display; "video bandwidth" in spectrum analysers has the same origin. | — | [11/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression](kb/11-radar/pulsed-radar-and-pulse-compression.md) |
| **virtual array** | — | The synthetic array formed by all TX–RX pairs of a MIMO radar; its element positions are sums of the TX and RX positions. | AWR1843 3 TX × 4 RX = 12 virtual | [11/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays](kb/11-radar/mimo-radar-virtual-arrays.md) |
| **VNA** | vector network analyser | Instrument that measures S-parameters (magnitude and phase) of a device by sweeping a source and measuring incident and reflected waves at each port. | — | [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md) |
| **VOACAP** | Voice of America Coverage Analysis Program | HF propagation prediction program based on ionospheric models (ITURHFProp is the ITU-R P.533 equivalent). | — | [09/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/hf-propagation-modes-and-prediction.md) |
| **VSWR** | voltage standing-wave ratio | Ratio of maximum to minimum voltage along a mismatched line; a simple scalar measure of match. | VSWR = (1+\|Γ\|)/(1−\|Γ\|); 2:1 ↔ \|Γ\| = 0.33, RL 9.5 dB, 0.51 dB mismatch loss | [03/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch.md) |
| **VSWR-2 bandwidth** | — | Frequency range over which an antenna's VSWR stays below 2:1 (return loss ≥ 9.5 dB); a common bandwidth definition. | single-tuned FBW ≤ 1/(√2·Q) | [08/small-antenna-limits](kb/08-antennas/small-antenna-limits.md) |

## W

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **waveguide** | — | A hollow (or dielectric-filled) conductor that guides waves above a cut-off frequency. | rectangular TE10 cut-off f_c = c/(2a): WR-90 6.557 GHz | [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md) |
| **waveguide below cut-off** | — | A tube or hole too small to support a propagating mode at the frequency of interest; fields decay along it, so long narrow holes pass air or cables but not RF. | — | [13/shielding-and-isolation](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/shielding-and-isolation.md) |
| **wavelength** | — | Distance a wave travels in one cycle; in a medium it shrinks by √εr. | λ = c/f; 433 MHz → 69.2 cm; 915 MHz → 32.8 cm; 1575.42 MHz → 19.0 cm | [02/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization](kb/02-electromagnetics/wave-equation-plane-waves-polarization.md) |
| **Welch method** | — | PSD estimate from averaging the FFTs of overlapping windowed segments; trades resolution for lower variance. | — | [14/fft-spectral-analysis-windows](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fft-spectral-analysis-windows.md) |
| **WER** | word error rate | Fraction of codewords decoded wrongly. | — | [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md) |
| **Wheeler cap** | — | A metal can placed over a small antenna to suppress radiation; comparing input resistance with and without it gives radiation efficiency. | η = 1 − R_cap/R_free (series-type) | [08/antenna-measurement](kb/08-antennas/antenna-measurement.md) |
| **whitening** | data whitening | XORing data with a pseudo-random sequence to avoid long runs of 0s or 1s that upset receiver loops. | PN9: x⁹ + x⁵ + 1 | [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md) |
| **Wiener–Khinchin theorem** | — | The PSD of a stationary random process is the Fourier transform of its autocorrelation. | S(f) = F{R(τ)} | [00/probability-random-processes-and-psd](kb/00-math-foundations/probability-random-processes-and-psd.md) |
| **Wilkinson divider** | — | Power splitter with two λ/4 lines of √2·Z0 and an isolation resistor of 2Z0; matched at all ports with isolated outputs at the design frequency. | 3.01 dB split; RL and isolation > 20 dB over ≈ 36 % bandwidth (single section) | [06/couplers-dividers-combiners](kb/06-rf-circuits/couplers-dividers-combiners.md) |
| **window function** | — | Taper applied to a block of samples before an FFT to reduce spectral leakage, trading resolution and noise bandwidth. | Hann ENBW 1.50 bins, scallop 1.42 dB; Hamming 1.36 bins, 1.75 dB; Blackman 1.73 bins | [14/fft-spectral-analysis-windows](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fft-spectral-analysis-windows.md) |
| **WPT** | wireless power transfer | Delivering power through coupled coils (Qi) or beams; efficiency is set by coupling and coil Q. | max efficiency depends on k²Q₁Q₂ | [08/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt](kb/08-antennas/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt.md) |
| **WR-90** | — | Standard rectangular waveguide size for X-band (8.2–12.4 GHz); inside 22.86 × 10.16 mm. | TE10 cut-off 6.557 GHz | [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md) |
| **wraparound antenna** | — | A conformal patch array wrapped around a rocket body tube to give a near-omnidirectional pattern with no protrusion. | — | [08/antennas-on-rockets](kb/08-antennas/antennas-on-rockets.md) |

## X

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **X7R** | class-2 ceramic dielectric | High-capacitance-density MLCC dielectric: ±15 % over −55…125 °C, but it loses much of its capacitance under DC bias and ages. Fine for decoupling, not for tuning. | ageing ≈ −2.5 %/decade-hour (typical) | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **XBee** | — | Digi's family of packaged radio modules (Zigbee, 900 MHz proprietary). | — | [15/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/other-ism-radios-fsk-2g4.md) |
| **XO** | crystal oscillator | A packaged crystal oscillator with no temperature compensation. | 10⁻⁵–10⁻⁴ (10–100 ppm) incl. temperature and 1 year ageing (Vig) | [04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md) |

## Y

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Y-factor method** | — | Noise-figure measurement from the ratio of output noise powers with a noise source on (hot) and off (cold). | F = ENR_lin/(Y − 1) | [17/noise-figure-measurement](kb/17-test-measurement/noise-figure-measurement.md) |
| **Y-parameters** | admittance parameters | Two-port description giving port currents from port voltages (short-circuit measurements); natural for transistor models. | I = Y·V | [01/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd](kb/01-circuit-theory/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd.md) |
| **Y5V** | class-2 ceramic dielectric (poor) | Very high-K MLCC dielectric with huge temperature and bias variation (+22/−82 %); avoid in RF and analog circuits. | — | [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md) |
| **Yagi–Uda antenna** | Yagi | A driven dipole with a reflector behind and directors in front; a cheap, directive ground-station antenna. | G ≈ 9.2 + 3.39 ln(B/λ) dBd (DL6WU): ≈ +2.35 dB per doubling of boom length | [08/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic](kb/08-antennas/yagi-uda-and-log-periodic.md) |
| **Yee cell** | — | The staggered grid of E and H field components used by FDTD. | — | [16/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem](kb/16-simulation-tools/em-solvers-fdtd-mom-fem.md) |
| **YIG** | yttrium iron garnet | Ferrimagnetic crystal used in very wideband, magnetically tuned oscillators and filters (and in circulators). | — | [02/magnetic-materials-permeability](kb/02-electromagnetics/magnetic-materials-permeability.md) |

## Z

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Z-parameters** | impedance parameters | Two-port description giving port voltages from port currents (open-circuit measurements). | V = Z·I | [01/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd](kb/01-circuit-theory/two-port-networks-zyh-abcd.md) |
| **z-transform** | — | The discrete-time counterpart of the Laplace transform; turns difference equations (digital filters, digital loops) into algebra in z. | X(z) = Σ x[n]z^{−n}; stable if all poles have \|z\| < 1 | [00/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems](kb/00-math-foundations/z-transform-and-discrete-time-systems.md) |
| **Z0** | characteristic impedance | The voltage-to-current ratio of a wave travelling one way on a line; a line terminated in Z0 shows no reflection. | lossless Z0 = √(L′/C′); RF default 50 Ω | [03/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/telegrapher-equations-characteristic-impedance.md) |
| **zero** | — | A value of s (or z) where a transfer function is zero. A right-half-plane zero (RHP zero) adds lag like a pole and makes feedback loops harder to stabilise. | each zero adds +20 dB/decade | [00/laplace-transfer-functions-bode](kb/00-math-foundations/laplace-transfer-functions-bode.md) |
| **zero span** | — | Spectrum-analyser mode that stays at one frequency and shows power versus time, like an oscilloscope; used for pulsed and bursty signals. | — | [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) |
| **zero-IF** | direct-conversion receiver | Receiver that mixes the RF straight to 0 Hz with an I/Q mixer; simple but suffers from DC offset, LO leakage, flicker noise and I/Q imbalance. | — | [06/receiver-architectures](kb/06-rf-circuits/receiver-architectures.md) |
| **ZOH** | zero-order hold | The staircase output of a DAC, which multiplies its spectrum by a sinc and needs a reconstruction filter. | \|H\| = sinc(f/fs) | [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md) |

## Symbols

| Term | Expansion | Meaning (plain English) | Formula / typical value | See |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Γ** | reflection coefficient | Ratio of reflected to incident wave voltage at a port or load; 0 for a perfect match, magnitude 1 for an open or short. | Γ = (Z_L − Z0)/(Z_L + Z0) | [03/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch.md) |
| **η0** | intrinsic impedance of free space | The ratio of electric to magnetic field in a plane wave in vacuum. | η0 = √(μ0/ε0) = 376.730 Ω (120π = 376.99 Ω is an acceptable hand value) | [02/electromagnetic-units-and-constants](kb/02-electromagnetics/electromagnetic-units-and-constants.md) |
| **θJA** | junction-to-ambient thermal resistance | Temperature rise of a chip's junction per watt dissipated, for a stated board; the starting point for PA heating checks. | T_J = T_A + P·θ_JA | [13/thermal-management-rf](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/thermal-management-rf.md) |
| **μ-factor** | Edwards–Sinsky stability factor | Single-number stability test: μ > 1 alone guarantees unconditional stability, and larger μ means more margin. | μ = (1 − \|S11\|²)/(\|S22 − ΔS11*\| + \|S12S21\|) | [06/amplifier-stability-and-biasing](kb/06-rf-circuits/amplifier-stability-and-biasing.md) |
| **𝓛(f)** | script-L, single-sideband phase noise | IEEE definition of phase noise: half the one-sided phase-fluctuation spectrum, in dBc/Hz at offset f. | 𝓛(f) = S_φ(f)/2 | [07/phase-noise-and-jitter](kb/07-noise-linearity/phase-noise-and-jitter.md), [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) |

## Ambiguous acronyms in this KB

These acronyms (and a few symbols) mean different things on different pages. Each page is correct in its own context, so read the surrounding text before you assume a meaning. The list comes from grepping every use of each acronym on 2026-10-03 (harvest: `research-logs/raw-harvest/glossary/ambiguous.tsv`, plus manual checks of the contexts). Where a convention choice is involved, [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md) gives the KB's canonical choice.

| Acronym | Meanings, and where each meaning is used |
|---|---|
| **AF** | (1) **array factor**: [12/array-factor-linear-arrays](kb/12-phased-arrays/array-factor-linear-arrays.md), [12/beam-steering-grating-lobes](kb/12-phased-arrays/beam-steering-grating-lobes.md). (2) **ambiguity function**: [11/ambiguity-function-waveforms](kb/11-radar/ambiguity-function-waveforms.md). (3) **antenna factor** (EMC, dB/m): [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md), [02/electromagnetic-units-and-constants](kb/02-electromagnetics/electromagnetic-units-and-constants.md). (4) the SPICE flicker-noise exponent `AF` (with `KF`): [05/low-noise-analog-design](kb/05-analog-electronics/low-noise-analog-design.md), [07/shot-and-flicker-noise](kb/07-noise-linearity/shot-and-flicker-noise.md). |
| **AR** | (1) **axial ratio** of a polarisation ellipse: [08/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization](kb/08-antennas/antenna-polarization-and-circular-polarization.md), [09/polarization-mismatch-loss](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/polarization-mismatch-loss.md). (2) **autoregressive** process, AR(1): [00/probability-random-processes-and-psd](kb/00-math-foundations/probability-random-processes-and-psd.md). (3) **ambiguity resolution** (PPP-AR): [21/rtk-ppp-and-corrections](kb/21-gnss/rtk-ppp-and-corrections.md). |
| **BT** | (1) Gaussian-filter **bandwidth × symbol time** in GFSK/GMSK: [10/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk](kb/10-comms-modulation/digital-modulation-psk-qam-fsk.md), [14/nco-ddc-duc](kb/14-sdr-dsp/nco-ddc-duc.md). (2) **time–bandwidth product** (coherent gain BT in passive radar; also written TB): [11/diy-radar-hardware](kb/11-radar/diy-radar-hardware.md), [11/ambiguity-function-waveforms](kb/11-radar/ambiguity-function-waveforms.md). (3) **Bluetooth**: [19/singapore-imda-rules](kb/19-regulations-safety/singapore-imda-rules.md). |
| **CA** | (1) **cell-averaging** CFAR: [11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md). (2) collision avoidance in **CSMA/CA**: [14/fpga-for-sdr](kb/14-sdr-dsp/fpga-for-sdr.md). (3) GPS **C/A** (coarse/acquisition) code: [21/gnss-signals-and-codes](kb/21-gnss/gnss-signals-and-codes.md). |

| **CR** | (1) LoRa **coding rate**. Written as a ratio "CR 4/5 … 4/8" on most pages ([15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md), [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md)) but as an **integer CR = 1…4** inside Semtech's bit-rate and time-on-air formulas ([07/sensitivity-and-mds](kb/07-noise-linearity/sensitivity-and-mds.md)). "CR 1" and "CR 4/5" are the same setting. (2) the **RC–CR** (low-pass/high-pass) phase-shift network in image-reject mixers: [06/mixers](kb/06-rf-circuits/mixers.md). |

(3) **differential encoding** (DE-QPSK): [14/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/signal-identification-and-spectrum-survey.md). (2) **European Commission / Community** (decisions such as 2006/771/EC): [15/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/ism-band-plans-duty-cycle.md). |
| **G_p / G_P (PG)** | Two quantities whose textbook symbols differ only in the case of the subscript. Since 2026-10-14 the KB writes them as **G_proc** and **G_power**. (1) **Processing gain** G_proc (often G_p in texts): [10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md), [10/multiple-access-and-duplexing](kb/10-comms-modulation/multiple-access-and-duplexing.md), [15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md). (2) **Operating power gain** G_power = P_L/P_in (textbook G_P): [03/s-parameters](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/s-parameters.md), [06/lna-design](kb/06-rf-circuits/lna-design.md); PA datasheets also print a power gain "Gp" ([18/lna-pa-gain-block-mmics](kb/18-components-parts/lna-pa-gain-block-mmics.md), written G_power there). G_p is still used for a tank's parallel conductance in [05/oscillators](kb/05-analog-electronics/oscillators.md) (m = G_c/G_p). "PG" itself is not used as an acronym in the KB. |

The harvester also mis-paired IL with "isolators" in [06/circulators-isolators](kb/06-rf-circuits/circulators-isolators.md); there it is insertion loss. |

(4) **linearly polarised** Q bound: [08/small-antenna-limits](kb/08-antennas/small-antenna-limits.md). |

(2) **near field**, in NF2FF ([16/openems-workflow](kb/16-simulation-tools/openems-workflow.md)) and as a page key in the 02 sources file ([02/near-field-far-field](kb/02-electromagnetics/near-field-far-field.md)). |

(3) **relaxation oscillators**: [05/oscillators](kb/05-analog-electronics/oscillators.md). (4) laminate **resin content**: [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md). |
| **RL** | (1) **return loss** (positive dB): [03/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch.md) and most pages. (2) a **load resistor R_L** ("RL 100 kΩ", SPICE netlists): [06/detectors-and-power-meters](kb/06-rf-circuits/detectors-and-power-meters.md), [05/oscillators](kb/05-analog-electronics/oscillators.md). (3) the **RL circuit**: [01/transient-and-step-response](kb/01-circuit-theory/transient-and-step-response.md). Watch the sign: some datasheets quote return loss as a negative S11 in dB. |
| **RS** | (1) **Reed–Solomon** code: [10/channel-coding-fec](kb/10-comms-modulation/channel-coding-fec.md). (2) EIA/TIA standards **RS-198**, **RS-485**: [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md), [13/grounding-and-return-paths](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/grounding-and-return-paths.md). (3) the SPICE diode **series resistance** parameter: [05/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor](kb/05-analog-electronics/diodes-schottky-pin-varactor.md). (4) RS Components (a distributor): [18/sourcing-and-eval-boards](kb/18-components-parts/sourcing-and-eval-boards.md). |
| **SA** | (1) **spectrum analyser**: [17/spectrum-analyzers](kb/17-test-measurement/spectrum-analyzers.md) and the rest of domain 17. (2) page key for the sampling page in the 14 sources file ([14/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sampling-aliasing-bandpass-sampling.md)). |
| **SAR** | (1) **synthetic-aperture radar**: [11/sar-and-imaging](kb/11-radar/sar-and-imaging.md). (2) **successive-approximation-register** ADC: [05/data-converters-adc-dac](kb/05-analog-electronics/data-converters-adc-dac.md), [05/analog-signal-conditioning](kb/05-analog-electronics/analog-signal-conditioning.md). (3) **specific absorption rate** (RF exposure): [19/rf-exposure-safety](kb/19-regulations-safety/rf-exposure-safety.md), [08/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt](kb/08-antennas/inductive-coupling-rfid-nfc-wpt.md). |
| **SE** | (1) **shielding effectiveness**: [13/shielding-and-isolation](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/shielding-and-isolation.md), [17/bench-measurement-recipes](kb/17-test-measurement/bench-measurement-recipes.md), [08/slot-antennas](kb/08-antennas/slot-antennas.md). (2) **single-ended** (traces, op-amp inputs): [13/stackups-and-materials](kb/13-pcb-rf-layout-emc/stackups-and-materials.md), [05/op-amps-real-limitations](kb/05-analog-electronics/op-amps-real-limitations.md). |
| **SF** | (1) LoRa **spreading factor** (domains 07, 10, 14, 15, 20). (2) the VNA **source-match** error term E_SF: [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md). (3) page key for the special-functions page in the 00 sources file ([00/special-functions-bessel-sinc](kb/00-math-foundations/special-functions-bessel-sinc.md)). |
| **SI** | (1) the **International System of Units**: [02/electromagnetic-units-and-constants](kb/02-electromagnetics/electromagnetic-units-and-constants.md), [00/decibels-and-log-units](kb/00-math-foundations/decibels-and-log-units.md). (2) **signal integrity**: [00/linear-systems-convolution](kb/00-math-foundations/linear-systems-convolution.md), [00/rf-learning-roadmap](kb/00-math-foundations/rf-learning-roadmap.md), [02/quasi-static-vs-full-wave](kb/02-electromagnetics/quasi-static-vs-full-wave.md). |
| **SNR** | One acronym, four bookkeeping conventions. (1) Ratio in a stated bandwidth ([07/sensitivity-and-mds](kb/07-noise-linearity/sensitivity-and-mds.md)). (2) LoRa's SNR over the **whole channel bandwidth**, which is why it is negative ([15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md)). (3) **E/N0**, used in KB link and radar budgets (the radar χ). (4) **2E/N0**, the real-passband matched-filter peak ([10/matched-filter-detection](kb/10-comms-modulation/matched-filter-detection.md)). (3) and (4) differ by 3 dB; see [00/canonical-values-and-conventions](kb/00-math-foundations/canonical-values-and-conventions.md). |
| **SR** | (1) op-amp **slew rate**: [05/op-amps-real-limitations](kb/05-analog-electronics/op-amps-real-limitations.md). (2) the VNA **reverse source-match** term E_SR: [17/vna-fundamentals-calibration](kb/17-test-measurement/vna-fundamentals-calibration.md). (3) **f_SR**, the self-resonant frequency: [04/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc](kb/04-real-components-hf/capacitors-at-rf-mlcc.md), [04/inductors-at-rf](kb/04-real-components-hf/inductors-at-rf.md). |
| **TDR** | (1) **time-domain reflectometry** (a step or pulse instrument): [03/time-domain-reflectometry](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/time-domain-reflectometry.md). (2) a NanoVNA's **time-domain transform** of S11, which only emulates TDR within its bandwidth: [17/nanovna-librevna-practical](kb/17-test-measurement/nanovna-librevna-practical.md). |
| **TE / TM** | (1) **transverse electric / magnetic** modes and polarisations: [02/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators](kb/02-electromagnetics/waveguides-and-cavity-resonators.md), [02/boundary-conditions-reflection-refraction](kb/02-electromagnetics/boundary-conditions-reflection-refraction.md). Some texts swap the TE/TM naming for ground reflection; see [09/two-ray-ground-reflection](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/two-ray-ground-reflection.md). (2) **TM** = CCSDS **telemetry** (TM synchronisation and coding): [10/packet-framing-protocols](kb/10-comms-modulation/packet-framing-protocols.md). (3) the IPC-TM-650 **test method** for laminate Dk: [02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md). |
| **TIA** | (1) **transimpedance amplifier**: [05/amplifier-topologies](kb/05-analog-electronics/amplifier-topologies.md), [05/low-noise-analog-design](kb/05-analog-electronics/low-noise-analog-design.md). (2) the **Telecommunications Industry Association** (TIA-603 land-mobile standard): [10/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb](kb/10-comms-modulation/analog-modulation-am-fm-pm-ssb.md). |
| **processing gain** | The term, not just its symbol, means four different things. (1) DSSS chips per bit, R_c/R_b ([10/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css](kb/10-comms-modulation/spread-spectrum-dsss-fhss-css.md)). (2) LoRa's 2^SF, which is 3.01·SF dB ([15/lora-phy-css-math](kb/15-lora-ism-radios/lora-phy-css-math.md)). (3) ADC oversampling gain: 10 log(f_s/2B) for **real** samples ([07/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking](kb/07-noise-linearity/dynamic-range-sfdr-blocking.md), [18/adcs-dacs-for-sdr](kb/18-components-parts/adcs-dacs-for-sdr.md)) but 10 log(f_s/B) for **complex** IQ samples ([14/sdr-hardware-survey](kb/14-sdr-dsp/sdr-hardware-survey.md), [18/rf-transceiver-ics](kb/18-components-parts/rf-transceiver-ics.md)). (4) radar and FFT coherent-integration gain ([11/detection-theory-cfar](kb/11-radar/detection-theory-cfar.md)). Copying the complex form into a real-ADC budget is 3 dB optimistic. |
| **K / k-factor** | (1) **Rollett stability factor** K: [06/amplifier-stability-and-biasing](kb/06-rf-circuits/amplifier-stability-and-biasing.md). (2) **Rician K-factor**: [09/multipath-fading-doppler](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/multipath-fading-doppler.md). (3) the **effective-earth k-factor** (4/3): [09/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon](kb/09-propagation-link-budget/fresnel-zones-los-radio-horizon.md). (4) the **coupling coefficient** k: [01/transformers-coupled-inductors](kb/01-circuit-theory/transformers-coupled-inductors.md). |
| **Γ, σ, δ** | **Γ** is the reflection coefficient ([03/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch](kb/03-transmission-lines-matching/reflections-vswr-return-loss-mismatch.md)) but also the crystal g-sensitivity vector ([04/crystals-and-resonators](kb/04-real-components-hf/crystals-and-resonators.md)) and the ISF ([06/oscillator-phase-noise-ltv-isf](kb/06-rf-circuits/oscillator-phase-noise-ltv-isf.md)). **σ** is conductivity ([02/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness](kb/02-electromagnetics/skin-effect-conductor-loss-roughness.md)), RCS ([11/radar-cross-section](kb/11-radar/radar-cross-section.md)) or a standard deviation. **δ** is skin depth or the loss angle in tan δ ([02/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent](kb/02-electromagnetics/dielectrics-permittivity-loss-tangent.md)). |

**Page-key collisions.** Several `_sources.md` files cite pages by 2–3-letter keys that look like acronyms: NF (near/far field), MAG (magnetic materials), AF (array factor), PS (phase shifters), PL (planar arrays), TP (tapering), SA (sampling), FE (front-end protection), LS (linear systems), SF (special functions), DE (differential equations), PR (probability), NM (numerical methods), BC (beginner concepts), ROC (rockets, in 21). In those tables, read them as page names, not as the glossary meanings.
